    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Carlos Salazar

    <WebMethod()> Public Function Register(ByVal str As String, ByVal str1 As String) As String
        'str  is the application trying to be register
        'str1  is the application key being used to register with

        str1 = Replace(str1, "-", "")
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim TotalAmount As Integer
        Dim UsedAmount As Integer
        Dim Amount As Integer
        Dim ValidKey As Boolean
        Try
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            SqlConn.Open()

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT ramount, rused, rvalid FROM register WHERE rapp = @str AND rkey = @str1", SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@str", str)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@str1", str1)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                TotalAmount = CInt(sqlDr.GetValue(0)) 'total of register licenses           
                UsedAmount = CInt(sqlDr.GetValue(1)) 'amount of used licenses           
                ValidKey = CBool(sqlDr.GetValue(2))
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            If Not ValidKey Then
                Return "Key not valid or has been discontinued"
            End If
            Amount = UsedAmount + 1

            If Amount > TotalAmount Then
                Return "Amount exceeds current license agreement"
            Else
                Dim SqlConn1 As New SqlConnection(strConn)
                Dim strSQL1 As String = "UPDATE register SET rused = @Amount WHERE rapp = @str AND rkey = @str1"
                Dim cmd1 As New SqlCommand(strSQL1, SqlConn1)
                Dim sqlDr1 As SqlDataReader
                Dim cnt1 As Integer
                Try
                    cmd1.Parameters.Add("@str", str)
                    cmd1.Parameters.Add("@str1", str1)
                    cmd1.Parameters.Add("@Amount", Amount)
                    cmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                    SqlConn1.Open()
                    sqlDr1 = cmd1.ExecuteReader


                    Return "3f8ymueDG08943ciuhm4d309ynhXp71Totgtrpb"
                Catch ex As SqlException
                    Return "Error during registration process"
                Finally
                    SqlConn1.Close()
                End Try
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return "Error during registration process"

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetCotype(ByVal companyid As Integer) As String

        Dim result As String
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)   ' moved from Try by Joe Dela Cruz

        Try
            ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("SELECT buildersco FROM companies " & _
               "WHERE companyid = @companyid", SqlConn)

            sqlCmd1.Parameters.Add("@companyid", companyid)    ' added by Joe Dela Cruz    
            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            result = sqlCmd1.ExecuteScalar.ToString

            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return "Error" & CStr(ex.Number) & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally     ' Added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetDevelopers() As DataSet

        Dim ds As New DataSet()
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)   ' moved from Try by Joe Dela Cruz

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT companyid, name FROM companies " & _
                            "WHERE companyid in (SELECT companyid FROM buildersco) " & _
                             "ORDER BY name"

            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        Finally     ' Added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetProjects(ByVal coid As Integer, ByVal peopleid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim ds As New DataSet()

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT projectid, name FROM projects " & _
                      "WHERE companyid in (SELECT companyid FROM companies WHERE companyid = @companyid) " & _
                      "AND projectid in (SELECT projectid FROM usersaccesstoprojects WHERE peopleid = @peopleid) " & _
                      "order by name"

            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@companyid", coid)
            Sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@peopleid", peopleid)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            da.Fill(ds)

            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            ' SqlConn.Close moved to Finally below

        Finally     ' Added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '  Programmer:  Patricia Kline
    '  Created Date: 10/04/02                       
    '  Modified Date: 6/23/03 by Joe Dela Cruz
    '  Get project path for project
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function getProjectPath(ByVal projid As Integer) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)   ' moved out of Try by Joe Dela Cruz

        Try
            ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz for @projid
            Dim SqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT buildersco.directory AS copath, projects.directory AS projpath " & _
                    "FROM buildersco INNER JOIN companies ON buildersco.companyid = companies.companyid INNER JOIN projects ON buildersco.companyid = projects.companyid " & _
                    "WHERE projects.projectid = @projid", SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim a As String

            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)    ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = SqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                a = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString & "/" & sqlDr.GetValue(1).ToString
            End While

            Return a

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return "ERROR" & ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally     ' Added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetUsersCompanies(ByVal peopleid As Integer) As String()

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim strSQL As String = "SELECT count(*) FROM companies WHERE companyid IN (SELECT companyid FROM userstobuildersco WHERE peopleid = @peopleid)"
        Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
        Dim cnt As Integer

        Try
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@peopleid", peopleid)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(cmd.ExecuteScalar.ToString)

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw New Exception(ex.Message)

        Finally     ' Added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

        Dim SqlConn1 As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim strSQL1 As String = "SELECT name, companyid FROM companies WHERE companyid IN (SELECT companyid FROM userstobuildersco WHERE peopleid = @peopleid) order by name"
        Dim cmd1 As New SqlCommand(strSQL1, SqlConn1)
        Dim sqlDr1 As SqlDataReader
        Dim cnt1 As Integer

        Try
            cmd1.Parameters.Add("@peopleid", peopleid)
            cmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            Dim x As Integer = 0
            Dim result(cnt - 1) As String

            SqlConn1.Open()
            sqlDr1 = cmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr1.Read
                result(x) = sqlDr1.GetValue(0).ToString & "^" & sqlDr1.GetValue(1).ToString & "^"
                x = x + 1
            End While

            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw New Exception(ex.Message)

        Finally     ' Added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn1.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn1.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: John Ashbaugh              
    '   Created Date:8/27/02                 
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Used to crypt password for connect string
    '   THIS WORKS!!!!!!!!!!!!!         
    '************************************************************************

    Private Function Crypt(ByRef pass As String, ByRef Strg As String) As String

        Dim b As Integer
        Dim j As String
        Dim h As String
        Dim i As Integer
        Dim a As Integer
        Dim tmp1 As String
        Dim tmp2 As String

        a = 1

        For i = 1 To Len(Strg)
            b = Asc(Mid(pass, a, 1))
            a = a + 1
            If a > Len(pass) Then a = 1
            tmp1 = Chr(Asc(Mid(Strg, i, 1)) Xor b)
            tmp2 = tmp2 & tmp1
        Next

        Strg = tmp2
        h = ""

        For i = 1 To Len(Strg)
            j = Hex(Asc(Mid(Strg, i, 1)))
            If Len(j) = 1 Then j = "0" & j
            h = h & j
        Next

        Return h

    End Function


    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: John Ashbaugh                              Date:  8/27/02                 
    '   Event: UPLOAD BYTE FILE
    '   Modified By: Patricia Kline                            Date: 11/21/02
    '                Joe Dela Cruz 6/18/03
    '   Changed: Return String instead of boolean for error msg 
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function UploadFile(ByVal fs() As Byte, ByVal folder As String, ByVal devpath As String, ByVal FlName As String) As String

        folder = Replace(folder, "'", "''")     ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
        devpath = Replace(devpath, "'", "''")   ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
        FlName = Replace(FlName, "'", "''")     ' added by Joe Dela Cruz

        Dim dir As System.IO.Directory
        Dim file As String = "e:\vcnet\vcnservice\iphotos\" & devpath & "\" & folder

        'create directory if doesnt already exist
        If dir.Exists(file) = False Then
            dir.CreateDirectory(file)
        End If

        'appends filename
        file = file & "\" & FlName

        Try
            Dim m As New System.IO.MemoryStream(fs)
            Dim f As New System.IO.FileStream(file, IO.FileMode.OpenOrCreate)
            m.WriteTo(f)
            m.Close()
            f.Close()
            f = Nothing
            m = Nothing

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As Exception
            Return Err.Number & "^" & Err.Description

        End Try

    End Function

            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '***    Programmer:  Carlos Salazar                                          ***
    '***    Created Date: 10/04/02                                               ***
    '***    Modified Date: 6/23/03 by Joe Dela Cruz                                                      ***
    '***    Insert Statement for adding Problem Codes                            ***
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csInsertProblemCodes(ByVal code As String, ByVal description As String) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlstmt As String

        Try
            code = Replace(code, "'", "''")
            description = Replace(description, "'", "''")

            ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz
            sqlstmt = LCase("SELECT count(*) FROM problemcodes " & _
                                    "WHERE problemscode = @code")

            Dim SqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            SqlCmd1.Parameters.Add("@code", code)    ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Open()
            End If

            cnt = CInt(SqlCmd1.ExecuteScalar)

            If cnt = 0 Then
                ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz
                sqlstmt = LCase("INSERT INTO problemcodes (problemscode, description) " & _
                                      "VALUES (@code, @description)")                

                Dim SqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

                If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                    SqlConn.Open()
                End If

                SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@code", code)    ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
                SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@description", description)    ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
                SqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
                Return "True"   ' moved here by Joe Dela Cruz because of Else
            Else    ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
                Return "Dup"
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally     ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '  Programmer:  Carlos Salazar / Patricia Kline
    '  Created Date: 10/04/02                   
    '  Modified Date: 6/23/03 by Joe Dela Cruz
    '  Insert Statement for adding new problems and return problemid
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csInsertProblems(ByVal refid As Integer, ByVal houseid As Integer, ByVal pplid As Integer, ByVal repid As Integer, ByVal source As String, ByVal probcode As String, ByVal desc As String, ByVal matid As Integer) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)   ' moved out of Try by Joe Dela Cruz

        Try
            desc = Replace(desc, "'", "''")
            ' Next 2 lines added by Joe Dela Cruz
            source = Replace(source, "'", "''")
            probcode = Replace(probcode, "'", "''")

            Dim a As String
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            Dim datestamp As Date = Now

            ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz
            sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO problems (houseid, hom_peopleid, cus_peopleid, reporteddate, problemsource, problemscode, problemdesc, materialid, pro_problemsid) " & _
                            "VALUES (@houseid,@pplid,@repid,@datestamp,@source,@probcode,@desc,"

            If matid = 0 Then
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "NULL, "
            Else
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & matid & ", "
            End If

            If refid = 0 Then
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "NULL)"
            Else
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & refid & ")"
            End If

            Dim SqlCmd As New SqlCommand(LCase(sqlstmt), SqlConn)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@houseid", houseid)    ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@pplid", pplid)    ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@repid", repid)    ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@datestamp", datestamp)    ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@source", source)    ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@probcode", probcode)    ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@desc", desc)    ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Open()
            End If

            SqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Closed Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

            SqlConn.Open()
            ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz
            sqlstmt = "SELECT max(problemsid) FROM problems " & _
                "WHERE houseid = @houseid " & _
                "AND hom_peopleid = @pplid " & _
                "AND cus_peopleid = @repid " & _
                "AND reporteddate = @datestamp " & _
                "AND problemscode = @probcode " & _
                "AND problemdesc = @desc "

            SqlCmd.CommandText = sqlstmt
            a = SqlCmd.ExecuteScalar.ToString
            
            Return a

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally     ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csInsertInHouseProblemNotes(ByVal problemid As Integer, ByVal peopleid As Integer, ByVal notes As String) As String

        notes = Replace(notes, "'", "''")

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)   ' moved out of Try by Joe Dela Cruz

        Try

            ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz for @problemid,@peopleid, @notes
            Dim SqlCmd As New SqlCommand(LCase("INSERT INTO problemsinhousenotes (problemsid, peopleid, notedate, comment) " & _
                                        "VALUES (@problemid,@peopleid,'" & Now & "',@notes)"), SqlConn)
            
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@problemid", problemid)  ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@peopleid", peopleid)    ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@notes", notes)          ' added by Joe Dela Cruz

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Open()
            End If

            SqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally     ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    ' Programmer:  Carlos Salazar                                          
    ' Created Date: 10/04/02   
    ' Modified Date: 6/24/03 by Joe Dela Cruz
    ' Insert Statement for adding new homeowners to people table
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csInsertCustomer(ByVal fname As String, ByVal lname As String, ByVal mi As String, ByVal address As String, ByVal city As String, ByVal st As String, ByVal zip As String, ByVal ph As String, ByVal email As String, ByVal cell As String, ByVal fax As String, ByVal work As String, ByVal isuser As Integer) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)   ' moved out of Try by Joe Dela Cruz

        Try
            Dim a As String
            Dim sqlstmt As String            

            fname = Replace(fname, "'", "''")
            lname = Replace(lname, "'", "''")
            address = Replace(address, "'", "''")
            city = Replace(city, "'", "''")
            ' Next 8 lines added by JOe Dela Cruz
            mi = Replace(mi, "'", "''")
            st = Replace(st, "'", "''")
            zip = Replace(zip, "'", "''")
            ph = Replace(ph, "'", "''")
            email = Replace(email, "'", "''")
            cell = Replace(cell, "'", "''")
            fax = Replace(fax, "'", "''")
            work = Replace(work, "'", "''")

            ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz for parameters
            sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO people (fname, lname, m_initial, address, city, state, zip, " & _
                                    "phone, email, cell, fax, worknumber, isuser, homeowner) " & _
                            "VALUES (@fname,@lname,@mi," & _
                                    "@address,@city,@st,@zip," & _
                                    "@ph,@email,@cell," & _
                                    "@fax,@work,@isuser,1)"
            Dim SqlCmd As New SqlCommand(LCase(sqlstmt), SqlConn)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@fname", fname)      ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@lname", lname)      ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@mi", mi)            ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@address", address)  ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@city", city)        ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@st", st)            ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@zip", zip)          ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@ph", ph)            ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@email", email)      ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@cell", cell)        ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@fax", fax)          ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@work", work)        ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@isuser", isuser)    ' added by Joe Dela Cruz

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Open()
            End If

            SqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Closed Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

            SqlConn.Open()
            ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz for parameters
            sqlstmt = "SELECT max(peopleid) FROM people " & _
                "WHERE fname = @fname " & _
                "AND lname = @lname " & _
                "AND m_initial = @mi " & _
                "AND address = @address " & _
                "AND city = @city " & _
                "AND state = @st " & _
                "AND zip = @zip "

            SqlCmd.CommandText = sqlstmt
            a = SqlCmd.ExecuteScalar.ToString       ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            Return a

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally     ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)   ' moved out of Try by JOe Dela Cruz

        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz

                    "WHERE houseid = @houseid", SqlConn)            
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(sqlCmd)

            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@houseid", houseid)      ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        Finally     ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: Patricia Kline   
    '   Date:  5/03/03                 
    '   Event: Download BYTE FILE    
    '   Modified Date: 6/18/03 by Joe Dela Cruz
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function DownloadFile(ByVal file As String) As Byte()

        file = Replace(file, "'", "''")     ' added by Joe Dela Cruz

        Try
            Dim fs As System.IO.FileStream
            Dim f As System.IO.File
            fs = f.Open(file, IO.FileMode.Open, IO.FileAccess.Read)
            Dim b(CInt(fs.Length) - 1) As Byte
            fs.Read(b, 0, CInt(fs.Length))
            fs.Close()

            Return b

        Catch ex As Exception
            Throw ex

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetCompanies() As DataSet

        Dim sqlstmt As String = "SELECT companyid, name FROM companies " & _
                    "ORDER BY name"
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)   ' moved from Try statement by Joe Dela Cruz

        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            ' Deleted SqlConn.Close() from here (by Joe Dela Cruz) and put in Finally below

        Finally     ' Added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If
        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Patricia Kline

    '   Modified Date: 6/23/2003 by Joe Dela Cruz

    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function Get_CompanyUsers(ByVal companyid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim ds As New DataSet()
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)   ' moved from Try statement by Joe Dela Cruz
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz to add @companyid
        sqlstmt = "SELECT peopleid, fname + ' ' + lname AS name " & _
                    "FROM people WHERE companyid = @companyid " & _
                    "AND peopleid in (SELECT peopleid FROM users) " & _
                    "ORDER BY fname ASC"        

        Try

            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@companyid", companyid)    ' added by Joe Dela Cruz    
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        Finally     ' Added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline                             Date:10/28/02                   
    '   Event: checks to see if project already exists and if not adds the
    '          new project
    '   Modified Date: 6/18/03 by Joe Dela Cruz
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function AddProject(ByVal coid As Integer, ByVal projname As String, ByVal city As String, ByVal st As String, ByVal zip As String, ByVal type As String, ByVal start As String, ByVal finish As String) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim result As String
        Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

        projname = Replace(projname, "'", "''")
        city = Replace(city, "'", "''")

        Try
            ' Statement below modified by Joe Dela Cruz
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM projects " & _
                "WHERE name = @projname " & _
                "AND companyid = @coid", SqlConn)

            sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@projname", projname)    ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@coid", coid)            ' added by Joe Dela Cruz

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar.ToString)            

            If cnt > 0 Then
                Return "Duplicate"
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String

            ' Statement below modified by Joe Dela Cruz
            sqlstmt = LCase("INSERT INTO projects (companyid, name, city, state, zip, type, startdate, enddate) " & _
                "VALUES (@coid,@projname,@city,@st,@zip,@type,@start,@finish)")

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@projname", projname)    ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@coid", coid)            ' added by Joe Dela Cruz            
            sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@city", city)            ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@st", st)                ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@zip", zip)              ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@type", type)            ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@start", start)          ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@finish", finish)        ' added by Joe Dela Cruz

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Open()
            End If

            sqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

        Try            

            ' Statement below modified by Joe Dela Cruz
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT projectid FROM projects " & _
                "WHERE name = @projname " & _
                "AND companyid = @coid ", SqlConn)

            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@projname", projname)    ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@coid", coid)            ' added by Joe Dela Cruz                       

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()

            result = sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar.ToString          ' added by Joe Dela Cruz                                   

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

        Return result

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '***    Programmer:  Carlos Salazar                                          ***

    '***    Modified Date: 6/18/03 by Joe Dela Cruz                              ***

    <WebMethod()> Public Function psSelectHomes(ByVal projectid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim ds As New DataSet()
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try

            ' Modified by Joe Dela Cruz
            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT houseid, address FROM homes " & _
                    "WHERE projectid = @projectid " & _
                    "AND houseid NOT IN (SELECT houseid FROM seqsheets " & _
                    "WHERE projectid = @projectid)", SqlConn)

            ' Next line added by Joe Dela Cruz
            Sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@projectid", projectid)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date: 6/18/03 by Joe Dela Cruz                        
    '   Event: return all users that match search criteria in dataset
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function ViewSeqSheet(ByVal projid As String) As DataSet

        projid = Replace(projid, "'", "''") ' added by Joe Dela Cruz

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz
        sqlstmt = "SELECT seqsheets.unit, seqsheets.bldg, seqsheets.lot, seqsheets.plantype, seqsheets.unitplan, seqsheets.phase, seqsheets.tract, homes.address, seqsheets.houseid " & _
                    "FROM seqsheets LEFT OUTER JOIN homes ON seqsheets.houseid = homes.houseid " & _
                    "WHERE seqsheets.projectid = @projid"
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)   ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException            

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function


    '*******************************************************************************
    ' Programmer:  Patrica Kline
    ' Created Date: 11/11/02        
    ' Modified Date: 6/19/03 by Joe Dela Cruz
    ' Insert Statement for adding in Property address and return houseid
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function psInsertPropertyAddress(ByVal projectid As Integer, ByVal address As String) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)        
        Dim result As String

        address = Replace(address, "'", "''")

        Try
            Dim cnt As Integer
            ' next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM homes " & _
                    "WHERE projectid = @projectid " & _
                    "AND address = @address", SqlConn)

            ' Next 2 lines added by Joe Dela Cruz
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@projectid", projectid)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@address", address)

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()

            ' Next line added by Joe Dela Cruz
            cnt = CInt(sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar.ToString)

            If cnt > 0 Then
                Return "Dup"
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally     ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

        Try
            ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz
            Dim SqlCmd As New SqlCommand(LCase("INSERT INTO homes (projectid, address) VALUES (@projectid,@address)"), SqlConn)

            ' Next 2 lines added by Joe Dela Cruz
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@projectid", projectid)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@address", address)

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Open()
            End If

            SqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally     ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

        Try
            ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT houseid FROM homes " & _
                "WHERE projectid = @projectid " & _
                "AND address = @address ", SqlConn)

            ' Next 2 lines added by Joe Dela Cruz
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@projectid", projectid)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@address", address)

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()

            ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz
            result = sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar.ToString

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally     ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

        Return result

    End Function


    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline                             Date:10/28/02                   
    '   Modified Date: 6/19/03 by Joe Dela Cruz
    '   Event: checks to see if project already exists and if not adds the
    '          new project
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function SeqSheet(ByVal projid As Integer, ByVal unit As String, ByVal bldg As String, ByVal lot As String, ByVal plan As String, ByVal uplan As String, ByVal ph As String, ByVal tract As String, ByVal houseid As String) As String

        ' Next 7 lines added by Joe Dela Cruz
        unit = Replace(unit, "'", "''")
        bldg = Replace(bldg, "'", "''")
        lot = Replace(lot, "'", "''")
        plan = Replace(plan, "'", "''")
        ph = Replace(ph, "'", "''")
        tract = Replace(tract, "'", "''")
        houseid = Replace(houseid, "'", "''")

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing
        Dim curplan As String = ""
        Dim curphase As String = ""

        If bldg <> "" Then
            Try
                ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz
                Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT plantype, phase FROM seqsheets " & _
                    "WHERE projectid = @projid " & _
                    "AND bldg = @bldg ", SqlConn)
                Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader

                ' Next 2 lines added by Joe Dela Cruz
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@bldg", bldg)
                sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                SqlConn.Open()
                sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
                While sqlDr.Read
                    If plan <> sqlDr.GetValue(0).tostring Then
                        curplan = sqlDr.GetValue(0).tostring
                    End If
                    If ph <> sqlDr.GetValue(1).tostring Then
                        curphase = sqlDr.GetValue(1).tostring
                    End If
                End While
                sqlDr.Close()

                If curplan <> "" And curphase <> "" Then
                    Return "Both" & curplan & ":" & curphase
                End If

                If curplan <> "" Then
                    Return "Plan" & curplan
                End If

                If curphase <> "" Then
                    Return "Phas" & curphase
                End If

            Catch ex As SqlException
                Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

            Finally     ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
                If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                    SqlConn.Close()
                End If

            End Try
        End If

        Try
            ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM seqsheets " & _
                "WHERE projectid = @projid " & _
                "AND unit = @unit " & _
                "AND bldg = @bldg " & _
                "AND lot = @lot", SqlConn)
            
            ' Next 4 lines added by Joe Dela Cruz
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@unit", unit)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@bldg", bldg)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@lot", lot)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()            

            ' Next line added by Joe Dela Cruz            
            cnt = CInt(sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar.ToString)

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally     ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

        If cnt = 0 Then
            Try
                Dim sqlstmt As String
                ' Next line modified 6/19/03 by Joe Dela Cruz
                sqlstmt = LCase("INSERT INTO seqsheets (projectid, unit, bldg, lot, plantype, unitplan, phase, tract, houseid) " & _
                    "VALUES (@projid,@unit,@bldg,@lot,@plan,@uplan,@ph,@tract,@houseid)")

                Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

                ' Next 9 line added by Joe Dela Cruz
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@unit", unit)
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@bldg", bldg)
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@lot", lot)
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@plan", plan)
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@uplan", uplan)
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@ph", ph)
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@tract", tract)
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@houseid", houseid)

                If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                    SqlConn.Open()
                End If

                sqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            Catch ex As SqlException
                Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

            Finally     ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
                If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                    SqlConn.Close()
                End If

            End Try
        Else
            Try
                Dim sqlstmt As String
                ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz
                sqlstmt = LCase("UPDATE seqsheets SET plantype = @plan, " & _
                                    "unitplan = @uplan, phase = @ph, " & _
                                    "tract = @tract, houseid = @houseid " & _
                                "WHERE projectid = @projid AND unit = @unit " & _
                                "AND bldg = @bldg AND lot = @lot")

                Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

                ' Next 9 lines added by Joe Dela Cruz
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@unit", unit)
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@bldg", bldg)
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@lot", lot)
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@plan", plan)
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@uplan", uplan)
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@ph", ph)
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@tract", tract)
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@houseid", houseid)

                If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                    SqlConn.Open()
                End If

                sqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            Catch ex As SqlException
                Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

            Finally     ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
                If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                    SqlConn.Close()
                End If

            End Try
        End If

        Return "True"

    End Function


    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date: 6/19/03 by Joe Dela Cruz

    <WebMethod()> Public Function UpdatePlanSeqsheet(ByVal projid As Integer, ByVal bldg As String, ByVal plan As String) As String

        ' Next 2 lines added by Joe Dela Cruz
        bldg = Replace(bldg, "'", "''")

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Try

            Dim sqlstmt As String
            ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz
            sqlstmt = "UPDATE seqsheets SET plantype = @plan " & _
                        "WHERE projectid = @projid AND bldg = @bldg"
            
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            ' next 3 lines added by Joe Dela Cruz
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)            
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@bldg", bldg)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@plan", plan)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            ' Next line added by Joe Dela Cruz
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally     ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function UpdatePhaseSeqsheet(ByVal projid As Integer, ByVal bldg As String, ByVal ph As String) As String

        ' Next 2 lines added by Joe Dela Cruz
        bldg = Replace(bldg, "'", "''")
        ph = Replace(ph, "'", "''")

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try

            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "UPDATE seqsheets SET phase = @ph " & _
                        "WHERE projectid = @projid AND bldg = @bldg "            

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)            

            ' next 3 lines added by Joe Dela Cruz
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@bldg", bldg)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@ph", ph)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            ' Next line added by Joe Dela Cruz
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()            

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException            
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally     ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

#Region " Application "

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline                             Date:10/28/02                   
    '   Event: checks to see if company already exists and if not adds the
    '          new company
    '   Modified Date:  6/20/03 by Joe Dela Cruz
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function AddCompany(ByVal coname As String, ByVal addr As String, ByVal city As String, ByVal st As String, ByVal zip As String, ByVal phone As String, ByVal fax As String, ByVal email As String, ByVal website As String, ByVal architect As Byte, ByVal consulting As Byte, ByVal subcontractor As Byte, ByVal engineering As Byte, ByVal finance As Byte, ByVal government As Byte, ByVal insurance As Byte, ByVal legal As Byte, ByVal manufactuer As Byte, ByVal realestate As Byte, ByVal vendor As Byte) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim result As String
        Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

        coname = Replace(coname, "'", "''")
        addr = Replace(addr, "'", "''")
        city = Replace(city, "'", "''")
        email = Replace(email, "'", "''")
        website = Replace(website, "'", "''")
        ' Next 4 lines added by Joe Dela Cruz
        st = Replace(st, "'", "''")
        zip = Replace(zip, "'", "''")
        phone = Replace(phone, "'", "''")
        fax = Replace(fax, "'", "''")

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM companies " & _
                "WHERE name = @coname " & _
                "AND zip = @zip ", SqlConn)            

            ' Next 2 lines added by Joe Dela Cruz
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@coname", coname)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@zip", zip)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            ' Next line added by Joe Dela Cruz            
            cnt = CInt(sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar.ToString)

            If cnt > 0 Then
                Return "Duplicate"
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally     ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If
        End Try

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz
            sqlstmt = LCase("INSERT INTO companies (name, address, city, state, zip, phone, fax, email, website, architectco, consultingco, engineeringco, financeco, govenmentco, insuranceco, legalco, manufactuersco, realestateco, subcontractorsco, vendorsco)" & _
                " VALUES (@coname,@addr,@city,@st,@zip,@phone,@fax,@email,@website,@architect,@consulting,@engineering,@finance,@government,@insurance,@legal,@manufactuer,@realestate,@subcontractor,@vendor)")

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            ' Next 20 lines added by Joe Dela Cruz
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@coname", coname)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@addr", addr)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@city", city)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@st", st)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@zip", zip)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@phone", phone)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@fax", fax)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@email", email)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@website", website)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@architect", architect)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@consulting", consulting)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@engineering", engineering)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@finance", finance)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@government", government)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@insurance", insurance)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@legal", legal)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@manufactuer", manufactuer)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@realestate", realestate)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@subcontractor", subcontractor)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@vendor", vendor)

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Open()
            End If

            sqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally         ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

        Try
            ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT companyid FROM companies " & _
                "WHERE name = @coname " & _
                "AND zip = @zip", SqlConn)

            ' Next 2 lines added by Joe Dela Cruz
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@coname", coname)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@zip", zip)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            ' Next line added by Joe Dela Cruz            
            result = sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar.ToString
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally         ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

        If subcontractor = 1 Then
            Try
                Dim sqlstmt As String
                ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz
                sqlstmt = LCase("INSERT INTO vcremote.dbo.companies (companyid, coname, subcontractor)" & _
                    " VALUES (@result,@coname,@subcontractor)")

                Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

                ' Next 2 lines added by Joe Dela Cruz
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@result", result)
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@coname", coname)
                sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@subcontractor", subcontractor)

                If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                    SqlConn.Open()
                End If

                sqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            Catch ex As SqlException
                Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

            Finally         ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
                If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                    SqlConn.Close()
                End If

            End Try
        End If

        Return result

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: John Ashbaugh

    <WebMethod()> Public Function vcLogin(ByVal name As String, ByVal p As String, ByVal coname As String, ByVal app As String) As String

        Dim sqlStr As String
        Dim tstHomeowner As Boolean
        Select Case app
            Case "web"
                If LCase(coname) = "homeowner" Or LCase(coname) = "home owner" Then
                    tstHomeowner = True
                    sqlStr = "SELECT users.peopleid, people.companyid, usersaccess.winspection,usersaccess.mginspection, " & _
                                                                                   "usersaccess.wdocuments, usersaccess.mgdocuments, usersaccess.wcusservice, usersaccess.mgcusservice, usersaccess.wbidding, " & _
                                                                                   "usersaccess.mgbidding, usersaccess.wreports, usersaccess.mgreports, usersaccess.wland, " & _
                                                                                    "usersaccess.mgland, usersaccess.wacct, usersaccess.mgacct, usersaccess.wsched, " & _
                                                                                    "usersaccess.mgsched, usersaccess.wrfi, usersaccess.mgrfi, usersaccess.wmaterials, usersaccess.mgmaterials, " & _
                                                                                    "usersaccess.wmdetails, usersaccess.mgmdetails, usersaccess.wprpmgmt, usersaccess.mgprpmgmt " & _
                                                                                "FROM users, people, usersaccess " & _
                                                                                "WHERE users.peopleid = usersaccess.peopleid " & _
                                                                                "AND users.peopleid = people.peopleid " & _
                                                                                "AND screenname = '" & name & "' " & _
                                                                                "AND password = '" & p & "' " & _
                                                                                "AND people.homeowner = 1 "
                Else
                    sqlStr = "SELECT users.peopleid, people.companyid, usersaccess.winspection,usersaccess.mginspection, " & _
                                                               "usersaccess.wdocuments, usersaccess.mgdocuments, usersaccess.wcusservice, usersaccess.mgcusservice, usersaccess.wbidding, " & _
                                                               "usersaccess.mgbidding, usersaccess.wreports, usersaccess.mgreports, usersaccess.wland, " & _
                                                                "usersaccess.mgland, usersaccess.wacct, usersaccess.mgacct, usersaccess.wsched, " & _
                                                                "usersaccess.mgsched, usersaccess.wrfi, usersaccess.mgrfi, usersaccess.wmaterials, usersaccess.mgmaterials,  " & _
                                                                "usersaccess.wmdetails, usersaccess.mgmdetails, usersaccess.wprpmgmt, usersaccess.mgprpmgmt, usersaccess.sysadmin," & _
                                                                "companiesaccess.accounting, " & _
                                                               "companiesaccess.schedule, companiesaccess.inspection, companiesaccess.document, companiesaccess.landacqu, companiesaccess.bidding, " & _
                                                               "companiesaccess.propmgmt, companiesaccess.materials, companiesaccess.rfi, companiesaccess.customer, companies.name, " & _
                                                                "companiesaccess.masterdetails " & _
                                                               "FROM users, people, companies, usersaccess, companiesaccess " & _
                                                               "WHERE people.companyid = companies.companyid " & _
                                                               "AND users.peopleid = usersaccess.peopleid " & _
                                                               "AND companiesaccess.companyid = companies.companyid  " & _
                                                               "AND users.peopleid = people.peopleid " & _
                                                               "AND screenname = '" & name & "' " & _
                                                               "AND password = '" & p & "' AND companies.name= '" & coname & "'"

                End If
            Case "mob"
                sqlStr = "SELECT users.peopleid, people.companyid, usersaccess.minspection,usersaccess.mginspection, " & _
                                                           "usersaccess.mdocuments, usersaccess.mgdocuments, usersaccess.mcusservice, usersaccess.mgcusservice, usersaccess.mbidding, " & _
                                                           "usersaccess.mgbidding, usersaccess.mreports, usersaccess.mgreports, usersaccess.mland, " & _
                                                            "usersaccess.mgland, usersaccess.macct, usersaccess.mgacct, usersaccess.msched, " & _
                                                            "usersaccess.mgsched, usersaccess.mrfi, usersaccess.mgrfi, usersaccess.mmaterials, usersaccess.mgmaterials, " & _
                                                            "usersaccess.mmdetails, usersaccess.mgmdetails, usersaccess.mprpmgmt, usersaccess.mgprpmgmt,  usersaccess.sysadmin," & _
                                                            "companiesaccess.accounting, " & _
                                                           "companiesaccess.schedule, companiesaccess.inspection, companiesaccess.document, companiesaccess.landacqu, companiesaccess.bidding, " & _
                                                           "companiesaccess.propmgmt, companiesaccess.materials, companiesaccess.rfi, companiesaccess.customer, companies.name, " & _
                                                           "companiesaccess.masterdetails " & _
                                                           "FROM users, people, companies, usersaccess, companiesaccess " & _
                                                           "WHERE people.companyid = companies.companyid " & _
                                                           "AND users.peopleid = usersaccess.peopleid " & _
                                                           "AND companiesaccess.companyid = companies.companyid  " & _
                                                           "AND users.peopleid = people.peopleid " & _
                                                           "AND screenname = '" & name & "' " & _
                                                           "AND password = '" & p & "' AND companies.name= '" & coname & "'"

            Case Else   ' is the vcnet app
                sqlStr = "SELECT users.peopleid, people.companyid, usersaccess.ainspection,usersaccess.mginspection, " & _
                                "usersaccess.adocuments, usersaccess.mgdocuments, usersaccess.acusservice, usersaccess.mgcusservice, usersaccess.abidding, " & _
                                "usersaccess.mgbidding, usersaccess.areports, usersaccess.mgreports, usersaccess.aland, " & _
                                "usersaccess.mgland, usersaccess.aacct, usersaccess.mgacct, usersaccess.asched, " & _
                                "usersaccess.mgsched, usersaccess.arfi, usersaccess.mgrfi, usersaccess.amaterials, usersaccess.mgmaterials, " & _
                                "usersaccess.amdetails, usersaccess.mgmdetails, usersaccess.aprpmgmt, usersaccess.mgprpmgmt, " & _
                                "usersaccess.adatacollection, usersaccess.mgdatacollection, usersaccess.abarcode, usersaccess.mgbarcode, " & _
                                "usersaccess.sysadmin," & _
                                "companiesaccess.accounting, companiesaccess.schedule, companiesaccess.inspection, companiesaccess.document, companiesaccess.landacqu, " & _
                                "companiesaccess.bidding, companiesaccess.propmgmt, companiesaccess.materials, companiesaccess.rfi, companiesaccess.customer, companies.name, " & _
                                "companiesaccess.masterdetails, companiesaccess.datacollection, companiesaccess.barcode " & _
                            "FROM users, people, companies, usersaccess, companiesaccess " & _
                            "WHERE people.companyid = companies.companyid " & _
                            "AND users.peopleid = usersaccess.peopleid " & _
                            "AND companiesaccess.companyid = companies.companyid  " & _
                            "AND users.peopleid = people.peopleid " & _
                            "AND screenname = '" & name & "' " & _
                            "AND password = '" & p & "' AND companies.name= '" & coname & "'"

        End Select

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlStr, SqlConn)

            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim a As String = Nothing
            Dim peopleid As Integer = 0

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            If tstHomeowner Then
                While sqlDr.Read
                    peopleid = CInt(sqlDr.GetValue(0))
                    a = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(1).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(2).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(3).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(4).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(5).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(6).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(7).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(8).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(9).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(10).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(11).ToString & ":" & _
                            sqlDr.GetValue(12).ToString & ":"
                End While
            Else
                While sqlDr.Read
                    peopleid = CInt(sqlDr.GetValue(0))

                    If app = "app" Then
                        a = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(1).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(2).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(3).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(4).ToString & ":" & _
                                sqlDr.GetValue(5).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(6).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(7).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(8).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(9).ToString & ":" & _
                                sqlDr.GetValue(10).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(11).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(12).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(13).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(14).ToString & ":" & _
                                sqlDr.GetValue(15).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(16).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(17).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(18).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(19).ToString & ":" & _
                                sqlDr.GetValue(20).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(21).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(22).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(23).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(24).ToString & ":" & _
                                sqlDr.GetValue(25).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(26).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(27).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(28).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(29).ToString & ":" & _
                                sqlDr.GetValue(30).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(31).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(32).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(33).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(34).ToString & ":" & _
                                sqlDr.GetValue(35).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(36).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(37).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(38).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(39).ToString & ":" & _
                                sqlDr.GetValue(40).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(41).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(42).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(43).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(44).ToString & ":"
                    Else
                        a = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(1).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(2).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(3).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(4).ToString & ":" & _
                                sqlDr.GetValue(5).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(6).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(7).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(8).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(9).ToString & ":" & _
                                sqlDr.GetValue(10).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(11).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(12).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(13).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(14).ToString & ":" & _
                                sqlDr.GetValue(15).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(16).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(17).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(18).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(19).ToString & ":" & _
                                sqlDr.GetValue(20).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(21).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(22).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(23).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(24).ToString & ":" & _
                                sqlDr.GetValue(25).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(26).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(27).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(28).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(29).ToString & ":" & _
                                sqlDr.GetValue(30).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(31).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(32).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(33).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(34).ToString & ":" & _
                                sqlDr.GetValue(35).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(36).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(37).ToString & ":" & sqlDr.GetValue(38).ToString & ":"
                    End If
                End While
            End If

            If a = Nothing Then
                Return "Error"
            Else
                updateLogin(peopleid)
                If tstHomeowner Then
                    Return "HOME:" & a
                Else
                    Return "USER:" & a
                End If
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return "Error"
        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: John Ashbaugh              
    '   Created Date:8/27/02                 
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   updates last login date for user
    '   THIS WORKS!!!!!!!!!!!!!         
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function updateLogin(ByVal peopleid As Integer) As Boolean

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try
            ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz
            Dim SqlCmd As New SqlCommand("UPDATE users set lastlogindate= getdate() WHERE peopleid = @peopleid", SqlConn)

            ' Next line added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@peopleid", peopleid)
            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Open()
            End If

            SqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            Dim IP As String = GetIP()
            ' Next line modified by Joe Dela Cruz
            Dim SqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("INSERT INTO usersips (peopleid, ip) VALUES (@peopleid,@IP)", SqlConn)

            ' Next 2 lines added by Joe Dela Cruz
            SqlCmd1.Parameters.Add("@peopleid", peopleid)
            SqlCmd1.Parameters.Add("@IP", IP)
            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Open()
            End If

            SqlCmd1.ExecuteNonQuery()

            Return True

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return False

        Finally     ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: John Ashbaugh              
    '   Created Date:9/13/02                  
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Gets Clients ip
    '   THIS WORKS!!!!!!!!!!!!!         
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetIP() As String

        Dim request As System.Web.HttpRequest
        Dim ClientIP As String
        ClientIP = Context.Request.UserHostAddress.ToString
        Return ClientIP

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: John Ashbaugh              
    '   Created Date:9/13/02                  
    '   Modified Date: 6/18/03 by Joe Dela Cruz                         
    '   Used to validate ips
    '   THIS WORKS!!!!!!!!!!!!!         
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Private Function ValidateIP(ByVal ip As String) As Boolean

        ' Replace any ' with '' - added by Joe Dela Cruz
        ip = Replace(ip, "'", "''")

        'need to check ip table for valid ips
        If Left(ip, 12) = "66.63.143.23" Or Left(ip, 11) = "192.168.1." Or Left(ip, 9) = "127.0.0.1" Or Left(ip, 9) = "206.111.248" Or Left(ip, 9) = "64.105.40" Then
            Return True
        Else
            Return False
        End If

    End Function


    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: John Ashbaugh              
    '   Created Date:8/27/02                 
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   test connection of db if fail test other db
    '   THIS WORKS!!!!!!!!!!!!!         
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Private Function GetConn() As Boolean

        Dim tst As Boolean
        tst = TstConn(ds1)

        If tst Then
            strConn = ds1
            Return True
        Else
            tst = TstConn(ds2)
            If tst Then
                strConn = ds2
                Return True
            Else
                'all failed add new database connections here
                strConn = "FAILED"
                Return False
            End If
        End If

    End Function


    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: John Ashbaugh              
    '   Created Date:8/27/02                 
    '   Modified Date: 6/18/2003 by Joe Dela Cruz
    '   Test the connection of the database
    '   THIS WORKS!!!!!!!!!!!!!         
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Private Function TstConn(ByVal ds As String) As Boolean

        ' Replace any ' with '' - added by Joe Dela Cruz
        ds = Replace(ds, "'", "''")

        Dim strConnect As String = ds & dsO
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConnect)

        
        Try
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT connect FROM tstConnect", SqlConn)
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
            'SqlConn.Close()

            Return True

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return False

        Finally ' added by Joe Dela Cruz
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function
