#Region " Error Handling "

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: John Ashbaugh              
    '   Created Date:8/27/02                 
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Send email to admin@virtualconstruction.net
    '   WORKS 
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function SendErrEmail(ByVal Msg As String) As Boolean

        Try
            Dim mail As New Web.Mail.MailMessage()
            SmtpMail.SmtpServer = "192.168.1.57"
            mail.From = "admin@virtualconstruction.net"
            mail.To = "it-support@virtualconstruction.net"
            mail.Subject = "ERROR"
            mail.Body = Msg
            mail.BodyFormat = MailFormat.Text
            SmtpMail.Send(mail)
            Return True

        Catch ex As Exception
            Return False

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: Carlos Salazar              
    '   Created Date:8/27/02                 
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Send email to IT@virtualconstruction.net Customer Service
    '   WORKS 
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function SendTextMsg(ByVal Msg As String) As Boolean

        Try
            Dim results() As String = GetIT()

            Dim mail As New Web.Mail.MailMessage()
            SmtpMail.SmtpServer = "192.168.1.57"
            mail.From = "sqlserver@wcpci.com"
            mail.To = results(1) '& "@messaging.sprintpcs.com"
            mail.Subject = "VCNET ERROR"
            mail.Body = Msg
            mail.BodyFormat = MailFormat.Text
            SmtpMail.Send(mail)
            Return True

        Catch ex As Exception
            Return False

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: John Ashbaugh              
    '   Created Date:12/18/02                 
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Gets current IT person on duty
    '   THIS WORKS!!!!!!!!!!!!!         
    '************************************************************************

    Private Function GetIT() As String()

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM it_duty", SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            Dim x As Integer = 0
            Dim result(cnt - 1) As String
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("SELECT name, nextel, email FROM it_duty WHERE onduty = 1", SqlConn)

            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result(0) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString 'name of person on duty
                result(1) = sqlDr.GetValue(1).ToString 'phone number of person need to add the @page.nextel.com extension
                result(2) = sqlDr.GetValue(2).ToString 'full email address of person
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            SqlConn.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Dim result(1) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

            Return result
        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: John Ashbaugh                               Date: 9/5/02                 
    '   Error function to insert msg into database for error tracking
    '   Modified BY: Patricia Kline                             Date: 9/10/02                        
    '   Changes:  gets the next msgid for user and updates only users tbl
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function SendMsgToDB(ByVal coid As Integer, ByVal pplid As Integer, ByVal Msg As String, ByVal errNum As Integer) As String

        'need to figure out auto id # for inserting of msgs
        Msg = Replace(Msg, "'", "")

        Dim msgincr As Integer
        Dim sqlConn2 As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim result As String

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT msgincr FROM users " & _
                "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid, sqlConn2)

            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlConn2.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                msgincr = CInt(sqlDr.GetValue(0))
            End While

            sqlConn2.Close()

        Catch ex As SqlException
            sqlConn2.Close()
            result = "Error^" & ex.Message & ex.Number.ToString
            Return result

        End Try

        Try
            msgincr += 1
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(LCase("INSERT INTO messages (msgid, msgfrom, msgsource, msg, status, subject) " & _
                    "values (" & msgincr & ", " & pplid & ", " & coid & ", '" & Msg & "','" & errNum & "', 'error')"), sqlConn2)

            If Not sqlConn2.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                sqlConn2.Open()
            End If

            sqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            If Not sqlConn2.State = ConnectionState.Closed Then
                sqlConn2.Close()
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            sqlConn2.Close()
            result = "Error^" & ex.Message & ex.Number.ToString
            Return result

        End Try

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("INSERT INTO messagestousers (msgid, msgfrom, msgsource, msgto, msgfolder) " & _
                "VALUES (" & msgincr & ", " & pplid & ", " & coid & ", 1, 'errors')", sqlConn2)

            If Not sqlConn2.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                sqlConn2.Open()
            End If

            sqlCmd1.ExecuteNonQuery()

            If Not sqlConn2.State = ConnectionState.Closed Then
                sqlConn2.Close()
            End If

            If errNum <> 0 Then
                Call SendErrEmail(Msg & errNum)
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            sqlConn2.Close()
            result = "Error^" & ex.Message & ex.Number.ToString
            Return result

        End Try

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd2 As New SqlCommand("UPDATE users SET msgincr = " & msgincr & " WHERE peopleid = " & pplid, sqlConn2)

            If Not sqlConn2.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                sqlConn2.Open()
            End If

            sqlCmd2.ExecuteNonQuery()

            If Not sqlConn2.State = ConnectionState.Closed Then
                sqlConn2.Close()
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            sqlConn2.Close()
            result = "Error^" & ex.Message & ex.Number.ToString
            Return result

        End Try

        Return "True"

    End Function

#End Region

#Region " Remote Server "

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: John Ashbaugh              
    '   Created Date:8/27/02                 
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Get filesnames to download from DCD server to remote Server
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function get_UpdateFiles(ByVal companyid As Integer) As String()

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM processSync WHERE companyid = " & companyid, SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            SqlConn.Close()

            Dim x As Integer = 0
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("SELECT filename FROM processSync WHERE companyid = " & companyid, SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr1 As SqlDataReader
            Dim result(cnt - 1) As String
            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr1 = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr1.Read
                result(x) = sqlDr1.GetValue(0).ToString
                x += 1
            End While
            sqlDr1.Close()
            SqlConn.Close()

            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Dim result(1) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = ex.Message & ex.Number.ToString
            Return result
        End Try

    End Function

#End Region

#Region " General "

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: John Ashbaugh              
    '   Created Date:9/30/02                    
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: gets msgincr for server msgs       
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function getCoMsgincr(ByVal companyid As Integer) As Integer

        Dim msgincr As String
        Dim sqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT msgincr FROM companiesaccess WHERE companyid = " & companyid, sqlConn)
        Dim sqlDR As SqlDataReader
        sqlConn.Open()
        With sqlCmd

            Try
                sqlDR = .ExecuteReader()
                If sqlDR.FieldCount > 0 Then
                    While sqlDR.Read
                        With sqlDR
                            msgincr = .GetValue(0).ToString
                        End With
                    End While
                    Return CInt(msgincr)
                Else
                    Return 0
                End If

            Catch ex As SqlException
                Return 0

            End Try

            sqlConn.Close()
        End With

    End Function

#Region " vcnet i "

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: John Ashbaugh              
    '   Created Date:8/27/02                 
    '   Modified Date:  10/25/02                        
    '   Gets login info for vcneti application and returns values
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function vcnetiLogin(ByVal name As String, ByVal p As String, ByVal coname As String) As String()

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim result(2) As String
        Dim sqlStr As String
        sqlStr = "SELECT users.peopleid, people.companyid, usersaccess.sysadmin " & _
                 "FROM users, people, companies, usersaccess, companiesaccess " & _
                 "WHERE people.companyid = companies.companyid " & _
                 "AND users.peopleid = usersaccess.peopleid " & _
                 "AND users.peopleid = people.peopleid " & _
                 "AND screenname = '" & name & "' " & _
                 "AND password = '" & p & "'" ' AND companies.name= '" & coname & "'

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlStr, SqlConn)

            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim peopleid As Integer = 0

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            result(0) = ""

            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result(0) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                result(1) = sqlDr.GetValue(1).ToString
                result(2) = sqlDr.GetValue(2).ToString
                peopleid = CInt(sqlDr.GetValue(0))
            End While

            If result(0) = "" Then
                result(0) = "Error"
                result(1) = "0"
                result(2) = "Invalid username or password"
                Return result
            Else
                updateLogin(peopleid)
                Return result
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = "0"
            result(2) = "Invalid username or password"
            Return result

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

#End Region

    <WebMethod()> Public Function add_architectsco(ByVal coid As Integer) As String

        Try

            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM architectsco " & _
                                        "WHERE companyid = " & coid, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            SqlConn.Close()

            If cnt = 0 Then
                Dim sqlstmt As String
                sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO architectsco (companyid)  " & _
                    "VALUES (" & coid & ")"
                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                SqlConn.Open()
                sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

            Return "true"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Return ex.Message

        End Try

    End Function

#Region " People "

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: John Ashbaugh                               Date:10/28/02                   
    '   Event: Insert into peoples table
    '   Modified By: Patricia Kline                             Date:11/19/02
    '   Changes:  Check if user exists prior to adding, send back peopleid
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function AddPeople(ByVal companyid As Integer, ByVal fname As String, ByVal mi As String, ByVal lname As String, ByVal address As String, ByVal address2 As String, ByVal city As String, ByVal state As String, ByVal zip As String, ByVal phone As String, ByVal fax As String, ByVal cell As String, ByVal pager As String, ByVal email As String, ByVal isuser As Byte, ByVal contractor As Byte, ByVal subcontractor As Byte, ByVal investor As Byte, ByVal landowner As Byte, ByVal architect As Byte, ByVal superintendant As Byte, ByVal projectmanager As Byte, ByVal vendor As Byte, ByVal customerservice As Byte, ByVal engineer As Byte, ByVal inspector As Byte, ByVal adddup As Boolean) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim result As String
        Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

        fname = Replace(fname, "'", "''")
        mi = Replace(mi, "'", "''")
        lname = Replace(lname, "'", "''")
        address = Replace(address, "'", "''")
        address2 = Replace(address2, "'", "''")
        city = Replace(city, "'", "''")
        email = Replace(email, "'", "''")

        If adddup = False Then
            Try
                Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM people " & _
                    "WHERE companyid = " & companyid & " " & _
                    "AND fname = '" & fname & "' " & _
                    "AND lname = '" & lname & "' " & _
                    "AND email = '" & email & "'", SqlConn)
                Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader

                sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                SqlConn.Open()
                sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
                While sqlDr.Read
                    cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
                End While
                sqlDr.Close()
                SqlConn.Close()

                If cnt > 0 Then
                    Return "Duplicate"
                End If

            Catch ex As SqlException
                result = "Error^" & ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message
                Return result

            End Try

            Try
                Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM people " & _
                    "WHERE companyid = " & companyid & " " & _
                    "AND fname = '" & fname & "' " & _
                    "AND lname = '" & lname & "'", SqlConn)
                Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader

                sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                SqlConn.Open()
                sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
                While sqlDr.Read
                    cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
                End While
                sqlDr.Close()
                SqlConn.Close()

                If cnt > 0 Then
                    Return "Duplicate?"
                End If

            Catch ex As SqlException
                result = "Error^" & ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message
                Return result

            End Try
        End If

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = LCase("INSERT INTO people (companyid, fname, m_initial, lname, address, address2, city, state, zip, phone, fax, cell, pager, email, isuser, contractor, subcontractor, investor, landowner, architect, superintendant, projectmanager, vendor, customerservice, engineer, inspector)" & _
                " VALUES (" & companyid & ",'" & fname & "','" & mi & "','" & lname & "','" & address & "','" & address2 & "','" & city & "','" & state & "','" & zip & "','" & phone & "','" & fax & "','" & cell & "','" & pager & "','" & email & "'," & isuser & "," & contractor & "," & subcontractor & "," & investor & "," & landowner & "," & architect & "," & superintendant & "," & projectmanager & "," & vendor & "," & customerservice & "," & engineer & "," & inspector & ")")

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Open()
            End If

            sqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Closed Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            result = "Error^" & ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message
            Return result

        End Try

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT peopleid FROM people " & _
                "WHERE companyid = " & companyid & " " & _
                "AND fname = '" & fname & "' " & _
                "AND lname = '" & lname & "' " & _
                "AND email = '" & email & "'", SqlConn)

            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result = sqlDr.Getvalue(0).tostring
            End While

            sqlDr.Close()
            SqlConn.Close()

            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            result = "Error^" & ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message
            Return result

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event:  function to check if username exists for that company
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function check_username(ByVal username As String, ByVal companyid As Integer) As String

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM users " & _
                "WHERE peopleid in (SELECT peopleid FROM people WHERE companyid = " & companyid & ") " & _
                "AND screenname = '" & username & "'", SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While

            sqlDr.Close()
            SqlConn.Close()

            If cnt > 0 Then
                Return "Duplicate"
            Else
                Return "True"
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        End Try

    End Function

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function add_usersaccess(ByVal pplid As Integer, ByVal ains As Byte, ByVal wins As Byte, ByVal mins As Byte, ByVal mgins As Byte, ByVal adoc As Byte, ByVal wdoc As Byte, ByVal mdoc As Byte, ByVal mgdoc As Byte, ByVal acus As Byte, ByVal wcus As Byte, ByVal mcus As Byte, ByVal mgcus As Byte, ByVal abid As Byte, ByVal wbid As Byte, ByVal mbid As Byte, ByVal mgbid As Byte, ByVal arpt As Byte, ByVal wrpt As Byte, ByVal mrpt As Byte, ByVal mgrpt As Byte, ByVal alan As Byte, ByVal wlan As Byte, ByVal mlan As Byte, ByVal mglan As Byte, ByVal aacc As Byte, ByVal wacc As Byte, ByVal macc As Byte, ByVal mgacc As Byte, ByVal asch As Byte, ByVal wsch As Byte, ByVal msch As Byte, ByVal mgsch As Byte, ByVal arfi As Byte, ByVal wrfi As Byte, ByVal mrfi As Byte, ByVal mgrfi As Byte, ByVal amat As Byte, ByVal wmat As Byte, ByVal mmat As Byte, ByVal mgmat As Byte, ByVal amde As Byte, ByVal wmde As Byte, ByVal mmde As Byte, ByVal mgmde As Byte, ByVal aprp As Byte, ByVal wprp As Byte, ByVal mprp As Byte, ByVal mgprp As Byte, ByVal adtc As Byte, ByVal wdtc As Byte, ByVal mgdtc As Byte, ByVal abcl As Byte, ByVal wbcl As Byte, ByVal mgbcl As Byte, ByVal sysadmin As Byte, ByVal vcremote As Byte) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO usersaccess (peopleid, ainspection, winspection, minspection, mginspection, adocuments, wdocuments, mdocuments, mgdocuments, acusservice, wcusservice, mcusservice, mgcusservice, abidding, wbidding, mbidding, mgbidding, areports, wreports, mreports, mgreports, aland, wland, mland, mgland, aacct, wacct, macct, mgacct, asched, wsched, msched, mgsched, arfi, wrfi, mrfi, mgrfi, amaterials, wmaterials, mmaterials, mgmaterials, amdetails, wmdetails, mmdetails, mgmdetails, aprpmgmt, wprpmgmt, mprpmgmt, mgprpmgmt, adatacollection, wdatacollection, mgdatacollection, abarcode, wbarcode, mgbarcode, sysadmin, vcremote) " & _
                "VALUES (" & pplid & ", " & ains & ", " & wins & ", " & mins & ", " & mgins & ", " & adoc & ", " & wdoc & ", " & mdoc & ", " & mgdoc & ", " & acus & ", " & wcus & ", " & mcus & ", " & mgcus & ", " & abid & ", " & wbid & ", " & mbid & ", " & mgbid & ", " & arpt & ", " & wrpt & ", " & mrpt & ", " & mgrpt & ", " & alan & ", " & wlan & ", " & mlan & ", " & mglan & ", " & aacc & ", " & wacc & ", " & macc & ", " & mgacc & ", " & asch & ", " & wsch & ", " & msch & ", " & mgsch & ", " & arfi & ", " & wrfi & ", " & mrfi & ", " & mgrfi & ", " & amat & ", " & wmat & ", " & mmat & ", " & mgmat & ", " & amde & ", " & wmde & ", " & mmde & ", " & mgmde & ", " & aprp & ", " & wprp & ", " & mprp & ", " & mgprp & ", " & adtc & ", " & wdtc & ", " & mgdtc & ", " & abcl & ", " & wbcl & ", " & mgbcl & ", " & sysadmin & ", " & vcremote & ")"
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            Return "true"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event:  function to insert contractors info
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function add_contractors(ByVal pplid As Integer) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT count(*) FROM subcontractors " & _
                                        "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid

        Try
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar)

            If cnt = 0 Then
                sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO subcontractors (peopleid)  " & _
                    "VALUES (" & pplid & ")"

                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
            End If

            Return "true"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & " " & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function add_superintendants(ByVal pplid As Integer) As String

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM superintendants " & _
                                        "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            SqlConn.Close()

            If cnt = 0 Then
                Dim sqlstmt As String
                sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO superintendants (peopleid)  " & _
                    "VALUES (" & pplid & ")"
                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                SqlConn.Open()
                sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

            Return "true"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Return ex.Message

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event:  function to insert or update customerservice info
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function add_customerservice(ByVal pplid As Integer, ByVal initials As String, ByVal type As String) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT count(*) FROM customerservice " & _
                                        "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid

        Try
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar)

            If cnt = 0 Then
                sqlstmt = LCase("INSERT INTO customerservice (peopleid, initials, type) " & _
                            "VALUES (" & pplid & ", '" & initials & "', '" & type & "')")

                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
            Else
                sqlstmt = LCase("UPDATE customerservice " & _
                            "SET type = '" & type & "' " & _
                                "initials = '" & initials & "' " & _
                            "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid)
                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
            End If

            Return "true"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: return all users that match search criteria in dataset
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function search_users(ByVal whereclause As String) As DataSet

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT people.peopleid, people.fname, people.lname, people.email, users.screenname " & _
                    "FROM people LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                      "users ON people.peopleid = users.peopleid " & whereclause

        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            'Return result
        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: return people info for user selected
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function get_people(ByVal pplid As Integer) As String()

        Dim result(25) As String
        Dim sqlstmt As String

        sqlstmt = "SELECT people.fname, people.m_initial, people.lname, companies.name, people.address, people.address2, people.city, " & _
                      "people.state, people.zip, people.phone, people.fax, people.cell, people.pager, people.email, people.isuser, " & _
                      "people.contractor, people.subcontractor, people.investor, people.landowner, people.architect, " & _
                      "people.superintendant, people.projectmanager, people.vendor, people.customerservice, people.engineer, " & _
                      "people.inspector " & _
                    "FROM people INNER JOIN " & _
                      "companies ON people.companyid = companies.companyid " & _
                    "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid

        Try
            Dim x As Integer
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                For x = 0 To 25
                    If sqlDr.IsDBNull(x) Then
                        result(x) = ""
                    Else
                        result(x) = sqlDr.GetValue(x).ToString
                    End If
                Next
            End While

            sqlDr.Close()
            SqlConn.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            ReDim result(2)
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = CStr(ex.Number)
            result(2) = ex.Message
            Return result

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline

    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function get_userinfo(ByVal pplid As Integer) As String()

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim result() As String

        Try
            Dim cnt As Integer
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM users " & _
                "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid, SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()
            SqlConn.Close()

            If cnt = 0 Then
                ReDim result(0)
                result(0) = "ADD"
                Return result
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            ReDim result(2)
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = CStr(ex.Number)
            result(2) = ex.Message
            Return result

        End Try

        Try
            ReDim result(1)
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT screenname, password " & _
                        "FROM users " & _
                        "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid
            Dim x As Integer
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                For x = 0 To 1
                    If sqlDr.IsDBNull(x) Then
                        result(x) = ""
                    Else
                        result(x) = sqlDr.GetValue(x).ToString
                    End If
                Next
            End While

            sqlDr.Close()
            SqlConn.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            ReDim result(2)
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = CStr(ex.Number)
            result(2) = ex.Message
            Return result

        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function get_useraccess(ByVal pplid As Integer) As String()

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        Dim cnt As Integer

        Try
            Dim result(0) As String

            sqlstmt = "SELECT count(*) FROM usersaccess " & _
                "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar)

            If cnt = 0 Then
                result(0) = "ADD"
                Return result
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Dim result(2) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = CStr(ex.Number)
            result(2) = ex.Message
            Return result

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

        Try
            Dim result(55) As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT ainspection, winspection, minspection, mginspection, " & _
                            "adocuments, wdocuments,mdocuments, mgdocuments, " & _
                            "acusservice, wcusservice, mcusservice, mgcusservice, " & _
                            "abidding, wbidding, mbidding, mgbidding, " & _
                            "areports, wreports, mreports, mgreports, " & _
                            "aland, wland, mland, mgland, " & _
                            "aacct, wacct, macct, mgacct, " & _
                            "asched, wsched, msched, mgsched, " & _
                            "arfi, wrfi, mrfi, mgrfi, " & _
                            "amaterials, wmaterials, mmaterials, mgmaterials, " & _
                            "amdetails, wmdetails, mmdetails, mgmdetails, " & _
                            "aprpmgmt, wprpmgmt, mprpmgmt, mgprpmgmt, " & _
                            "adatacollection, wdatacollection,  mgdatacollection, " & _
                            "abarcode, wbarcode, mgbarcode, sysadmin, vcremote " & _
                        "FROM usersaccess " & _
                        "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid

            Dim x As Integer
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                For x = 0 To 55
                    If sqlDr.IsDBNull(x) Then
                        result(x) = ""
                    Else
                        result(x) = sqlDr.GetValue(x).ToString
                    End If
                Next
            End While

            sqlDr.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Dim result(2) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = CStr(ex.Number)
            result(2) = ex.Message
            Return result

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: Patricia Kline                              Date:10/28/02                   
    '   Event: Edit people table
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function edit_people(ByVal pplid As Integer, ByVal companyid As Integer, ByVal fname As String, ByVal mi As String, ByVal lname As String, ByVal address As String, ByVal address2 As String, ByVal city As String, ByVal state As String, ByVal zip As String, ByVal phone As String, ByVal fax As String, ByVal cell As String, ByVal pager As String, ByVal email As String, ByVal isuser As Byte, ByVal contractor As Byte, ByVal subcontractor As Byte, ByVal investor As Byte, ByVal landowner As Byte, ByVal architect As Byte, ByVal superintendant As Byte, ByVal projectmanager As Byte, ByVal vendor As Byte, ByVal customerservice As Byte, ByVal engineer As Byte, ByVal inspector As Byte, ByVal adddup As Boolean) As String

        fname = Replace(fname, "'", "''")
        mi = Replace(mi, "'", "''")
        lname = Replace(lname, "'", "''")
        address = Replace(address, "'", "''")
        address2 = Replace(address2, "'", "''")
        city = Replace(city, "'", "''")
        email = Replace(email, "'", "''")

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim result As String
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

        If adddup = False Then
            Try
                sqlstmt = "SELECT count(*) FROM people " & _
                    "WHERE companyid = " & companyid & " " & _
                    "AND fname = '" & fname & "' " & _
                    "AND lname = '" & lname & "' " & _
                    "AND email = '" & email & "' " & _
                    "AND peopleid <> " & pplid

                Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                SqlConn.Open()
                sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                cnt = CInt(sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar)

                If cnt > 0 Then
                    Return "Duplicate"
                End If

            Catch ex As SqlException
                Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

            Finally
                If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                    SqlConn.Close()
                End If

            End Try

            Try
                sqlstmt = "SELECT count(*) FROM people " & _
                    "WHERE companyid = " & companyid & " " & _
                    "AND fname = '" & fname & "' " & _
                    "AND lname = '" & lname & "' " & _
                    "AND peopleid <> " & pplid

                Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader

                sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                SqlConn.Open()
                cnt = CInt(sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar)

                If cnt > 0 Then
                    Return "Duplicate?"
                End If

            Catch ex As SqlException
                Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

            Finally
                If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                    SqlConn.Close()
                End If
            End Try
        End If

        Try
            sqlstmt = "UPDATE people SET companyid = " & companyid & ", " & _
                "fname = '" & fname & "', m_initial = '" & mi & "', lname = '" & lname & "', address = '" & address & "', " & _
                "address2 = '" & address2 & "', city = '" & city & "', state = '" & state & "', " & _
                "zip = '" & zip & "', phone = '" & phone & "', fax = '" & fax & "', " & _
                "cell ='" & cell & "', pager = '" & pager & "', email = '" & email & "', " & _
                "isuser = " & isuser & ", contractor = " & contractor & ", " & _
                "subcontractor = " & subcontractor & ", investor = " & investor & ", " & _
                "landowner = " & landowner & ", architect = " & architect & ", " & _
                "superintendant = " & superintendant & ", projectmanager = " & projectmanager & ", " & _
                "vendor = " & vendor & ", customerservice = " & customerservice & ", " & _
                "engineer = " & engineer & ", inspector = " & inspector & " " & _
                "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(LCase(sqlstmt), SqlConn)
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

#End Region

#Region " IV "

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   get iresults and send back to vcnet
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function iv_getiresults(ByVal whereclause As String) As String()

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM iresults WHERE " & whereclause, SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            Dim x As Integer = 0
            Dim result(cnt - 1) As String
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("SELECT DISTINCT iresultsid FROM iresults " & _
                "WHERE " & whereclause, SqlConn)

            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result(x) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                x = x + 1
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            SqlConn.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Dim result(1) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

            Return result
        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date: 5/30/03 - Added ORDER BY                        
    '   get inspection data and send back to vcnet for datagrid in dataset
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function iv_getinspections(ByVal whereclause As String) As DataSet
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT i.iresultsid, i.inspectiondate, i.phase, i.lot, i.bldg, i.unit, i.plantype, code.cat1, i.locationname, i.comment, " & _
                      "i.code + ' ' + code.subcat + ' ' + code.description AS codedesc, i.attention, sirs.sdate AS verified, photo.photofilename, code.tradename,  " & _
                      "comp.name AS company, insp.initials AS inspector, sub.name AS subcontractor, docs.docfilename AS sirfilename, followup.iresultsid AS followup,  " & _
                      "super.fname + ' ' + super.lname AS super, units.unitplantype  " & _
                    "FROM companies comp INNER JOIN  " & _
                      "people peo ON comp.companyid = peo.companyid INNER JOIN " & _
                      "iresults i INNER JOIN " & _
                      "inspectioncodes code ON i.code = code.code INNER JOIN " & _
                      "inspectors insp ON i.ins_peopleid = insp.peopleid ON peo.peopleid = insp.peopleid INNER JOIN " & _
                      "companies sub ON i.sub_companyid = sub.companyid INNER JOIN " & _
                      "superintendantsprojects ON i.projectid = superintendantsprojects.projectid AND " & _
                      "i.inspectiondate = superintendantsprojects.inspectiondate INNER JOIN " & _
                      "people super ON superintendantsprojects.peopleid = super.peopleid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                      "unitplantypes units ON i.projectid = units.projectid AND i.unit = units.unit LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                      "iresults followup ON i.ire_iresultsid = followup.iresultsid AND i.ire_projectid = followup.projectid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                      "documents docs INNER JOIN " & _
                      "inspectionreportdetails sirs ON docs.projectid = sirs.projectid AND docs.documentid = sirs.documentid ON i.iresultsid = sirs.iresultsid AND " & _
                      "i.projectid = sirs.projectid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                      "inspectionphotos photo ON i.iresultsid = photo.iresultsid AND i.projectid = photo.projectid " & _
                      "WHERE " & whereclause & _
                      "ORDER BY i.iresultsid ASC"

        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   get detail and send back to vcnet 
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function iv_getdetail(ByVal projectid As Integer, ByVal iresultsid As String, ByVal code As String) As String

        Dim result As String
        Dim sqlstmt As String

        sqlstmt = "SELECT count(*) " & _
                    "FROM documents INNER JOIN " & _
                      "iresultstodetails ON documents.projectid = iresultstodetails.projectid AND documents.documentid = iresultstodetails.documentid " & _
                     "WHERE documents.projectid = " & projectid & " AND iresultsid = '" & iresultsid & "'"

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()

            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            If cnt > 0 Then
                sqlstmt = "SELECT documents.docfilename " & _
                    "FROM documents INNER JOIN " & _
                      "iresultstodetails ON documents.projectid = iresultstodetails.projectid AND documents.documentid = iresultstodetails.documentid " & _
                     "WHERE documents.projectid = " & projectid & " AND iresultsid = '" & iresultsid & "'"

                Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text

                sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader
                While sqlDr.Read
                    result = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                End While
                sqlDr.Close()

                Return result
            Else
                sqlstmt = "SELECT count(*) " & _
                    "FROM documents INNER JOIN " & _
                        "codedetail ON documents.documentid = codedetail.documentid " & _
                     "WHERE documents.projectid = " & projectid & " AND code = '" & code & "'"

                Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text

                sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader
                While sqlDr.Read
                    cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
                End While
                sqlDr.Close()

                If cnt > 0 Then
                    sqlstmt = "SELECT documents.docfilename " & _
                        "FROM documents INNER JOIN " & _
                            "codedetail ON documents.documentid = codedetail.documentid " & _
                     "WHERE documents.projectid = " & projectid & " AND code = '" & code & "'"

                    Dim sqlCmd2 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                    sqlCmd2.CommandType = CommandType.Text

                    sqlDr = sqlCmd2.ExecuteReader
                    While sqlDr.Read
                        result = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                    End While
                    sqlDr.Close()

                    Return result
                Else
                    Return ""
                End If
            End If

            SqlConn.Close()

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            result = "ERROR^" & ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message
            Return result

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************

    '   get structural plans and send back to vcnet 
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function iv_getstructural(ByVal projectid As Integer, ByVal plantype As String, ByVal phase As String) As String

        Dim result As String
        Dim sqlstmt As String

        sqlstmt = "SELECT count(*) " & _
                    "FROM documents INNER JOIN " & _
                      "plans ON documents.documentid = plans.documentid INNER JOIN " & _
                      "plantypes ON plans.documentid = plantypes.documentid AND " & _
                      "plans.pagenbr = plantypes.pagenbr LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                      "phases ON plans.documentid = phases.documentid " & _
                     "WHERE documents.projectid = " & projectid & " AND plantype = '" & plantype & "' AND phase = '" & "' AND planscategory = 'structural plan'"

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()

            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            If cnt > 0 Then
                sqlstmt = "SELECT documents.docfilename " & _
                    "FROM documents INNER JOIN " & _
                      "plans ON documents.documentid = plans.documentid INNER JOIN " & _
                      "plantypes ON plans.documentid = plantypes.documentid AND " & _
                      "plans.pagenbr = plantypes.pagenbr LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                      "phases ON plans.documentid = phases.documentid " & _
                     "WHERE documents.projectid = " & projectid & " AND plantype = '" & plantype & "' AND phase = '" & phase & "' AND planscategory = 'structural plan'"

                Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text

                sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader
                While sqlDr.Read
                    result = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                End While
                sqlDr.Close()

                Return result
            Else
                sqlstmt = "SELECT count(*) " & _
                    "FROM documents INNER JOIN " & _
                      "plans ON documents.documentid = plans.documentid INNER JOIN " & _
                      "plantypes ON plans.documentid = plantypes.documentid AND " & _
                      "plans.pagenbr = plantypes.pagenbr LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                      "phases ON plans.documentid = phases.documentid " & _
                     "WHERE documents.projectid = " & projectid & " AND plantype = '" & plantype & "' AND phase IS NULL AND planscategory = 'structural plan'"

                Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text

                sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader
                While sqlDr.Read
                    cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
                End While
                sqlDr.Close()

                If cnt > 0 Then
                    sqlstmt = "SELECT documents.docfilename " & _
                    "FROM documents INNER JOIN " & _
                      "plans ON documents.documentid = plans.documentid INNER JOIN " & _
                      "plantypes ON plans.documentid = plantypes.documentid AND " & _
                      "plans.pagenbr = plantypes.pagenbr LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                      "phases ON plans.documentid = phases.documentid " & _
                     "WHERE documents.projectid = " & projectid & " AND plantype = '" & plantype & "' AND phase IS NULL AND planscategory = 'structural plan'"

                    Dim sqlCmd2 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                    sqlCmd2.CommandType = CommandType.Text

                    sqlDr = sqlCmd2.ExecuteReader
                    While sqlDr.Read
                        result = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                    End While
                    sqlDr.Close()

                    Return result
                Else
                    Return ""
                End If
            End If

            SqlConn.Close()

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            result = "ERROR^" & ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message
            Return result

        End Try

    End Function

    ' 8 lost iv services from Marc
    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   send all projects for selected developer to vcent
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function iv_getProjects(ByVal developer As String, ByVal peopleid As Integer) As String()

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM projects " & _
                "WHERE companyid in (SELECT companyid FROM companies WHERE name = '" & developer & "') " & _
                "AND projectid in (SELECT projectid FROM usersaccesstoprojects WHERE peopleid = " & peopleid & ")", SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            Dim x As Integer = 0
            Dim result(cnt - 1) As String
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("SELECT name FROM projects " & _
                               "WHERE companyid in (SELECT companyid FROM companies WHERE name = '" & developer & "') " & _
                               "AND projectid in (SELECT projectid FROM usersaccesstoprojects WHERE peopleid = " & peopleid & ")", SqlConn)
            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result(x) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                x = x + 1
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            SqlConn.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Dim result(1) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

            Return result
        End Try

    End Function



    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   get path selected developer/project and sent to vcnet
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function iv_getProjinfo(ByVal developer As String, ByVal project As String) As String()

        Dim companyid As Integer
        Dim path As String

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("SELECT companyid, directory FROM buildersco " & _
                "WHERE companyid in (SELECT companyid FROM companies WHERE name = '" & developer & "')", SqlConn)

            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                companyid = CInt(sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString)
                path = sqlDr.GetValue(1).ToString
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            SqlConn.Close()

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Dim result(1) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

            Return result
        End Try

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim result(2) As String
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("SELECT projectid, directory, companyid FROM projects " & _
                "WHERE name = '" & project & "' AND companyid = " & companyid, SqlConn)

            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result(0) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                result(1) = path & "\" & sqlDr.GetValue(1).ToString & "\"
                result(2) = sqlDr.GetValue(2).ToString
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            path = result(0)
            SqlConn.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Dim result(1) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

            Return result
        End Try

    End Function
