    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event:  Function to get inspection report details and reminders
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function iv_getsirrpt(ByVal projid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT LEFT(documents.docfilename, LEN(documents.docfilename) - 4) AS sirnbr, inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid, iresults.inspectiondate, " & _
                        "iresults.phase, inspectioncodes.tradename, iresults.lot, iresults.unit, inspectionreportdetails.sdate, " & _
                        "inspectionreportreminders.reminder, dbo.documents.documentid " & _
                    "FROM inspectionreportdetails INNER JOIN " & _
                        "iresults ON inspectionreportdetails.projectid = iresults.projectid AND " & _
                        "inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid = iresults.iresultsid INNER JOIN " & _
                        "inspectioncodes ON iresults.code = inspectioncodes.code INNER JOIN " & _
                        "documents ON inspectionreportdetails.documentid = documents.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                        "inspectionreportreminders ON inspectionreportdetails.documentid = inspectionreportreminders.documentid AND " & _
                        "inspectionreportdetails.projectid = inspectionreportreminders.projectid AND " & _
                        "inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid = inspectionreportreminders.iresultsid " & _
                    "WHERE documents.projectid = @projid " & _
                    "ORDER BY inspectionreportdetails.sdate, LEFT(documents.docfilename, LEN(documents.docfilename) - 4), inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid, " & _
                        "inspectionreportreminders.reminder"

        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            'Return result
        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event:  Function to get attns by trade
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function iv_attntraderpt(ByVal projid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT c.tradename, i.attention, s.sdate " & _
                    "FROM iresults i INNER JOIN " & _
                        "inspectioncodes c ON i.code = c.code LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                        "inspectionreportdetails s ON i.projectid = s.projectid AND i.iresultsid = s.iresultsid " & _
                    "WHERE i.projectid = " & projid & _
                    "ORDER BY c.tradename"

        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            'Return result
        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Joe Dela Cruz                                                
    '   Created Date: 01/21/03                                                    
    '   Modified Date:                                                            
    '   Event:  Get Inspection data to use in Inspection Results Report for a 
    '           specific project                                                                    
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetInspectionResultsData(ByVal projid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim strSQL As String
        strSQL = "SELECT iresults.iresultsid, iresults.phase, iresults.lot, iresults.bldg, iresults.unit, " & _
                      "iresults.plantype, iresults.locationname AS location, inspectioncodes.description, iresults.comment, " & _
                      "inspectors.initials, iresults.inspectiondate, inspectioncodes.cat1 AS inspectiontype, projects.name " & _
                    "FROM         iresults INNER JOIN " & _
                      "inspectioncodes ON iresults.code = inspectioncodes.code INNER JOIN " & _
                      "inspectors ON iresults.ins_peopleid = inspectors.peopleid INNER JOIN " & _
                      "projects ON iresults.projectid = projects.projectid " & _
        "WHERE (iresults.projectid = " & projid & ") " & _
        "ORDER BY iresults.iresultsid"

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Joe Dela Cruz                                                 *
    '   Created Date: 02/24/03                                                     *
    '   Modified Date:                                                             *
    '   Get trade anomolies from inspectioncodes table for projects from ireswults *
    '   with attention = 1 for a specific company                                  *
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetTradeAnomoliesData(ByVal companyid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim strSQL As String

        strSQL = "SELECT DISTINCT dbo.inspectioncodes.tradename AS trade, COUNT(dbo.inspectioncodes.tradename) AS tradecount, dbo.projects.name AS projectname,projects.projectid " & _
                           "FROM dbo.iresults INNER JOIN " & _
                               "dbo.inspectioncodes ON dbo.iresults.code = dbo.inspectioncodes.code INNER JOIN " & _
                               "dbo.projects ON dbo.iresults.projectid = dbo.projects.projectid INNER JOIN " & _
                               "dbo.companies ON dbo.projects.companyid = dbo.companies.companyid " & _
                           "WHERE     (dbo.iresults.attention = 1) AND (dbo.projects.companyid = " & companyid & ") " & _
                           "GROUP BY dbo.inspectioncodes.tradename, dbo.projects.name, projects.projectid " & _
                           "ORDER BY dbo.inspectioncodes.tradename"
        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Joe Dela Cruz                                                 *
    '   Created Date: 3/4/03                                                       *
    '   Modified Date:                                                             *
    '   Get trade anomolies from inspectioncodes table from ireswults              *
    '   with attention = 1 for a specific project                                  *
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetTradeAnomoliesPerProjectData(ByVal projectid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim strSQL As String
        strSQL = "SELECT DISTINCT dbo.inspectioncodes.tradename AS trade, COUNT(dbo.inspectioncodes.tradename) AS tradecount, dbo.projects.name AS projectname,projects.projectid " & _
            "FROM dbo.iresults INNER JOIN " & _
                "dbo.inspectioncodes ON dbo.iresults.code = dbo.inspectioncodes.code INNER JOIN " & _
                "dbo.projects ON dbo.iresults.projectid = dbo.projects.projectid INNER JOIN " & _
                "dbo.companies ON dbo.projects.companyid = dbo.companies.companyid " & _
            "WHERE     (dbo.iresults.attention = 1) AND (dbo.projects.projectid = " & projectid & ") " & _
            "GROUP BY dbo.inspectioncodes.tradename, dbo.projects.name, projects.projectid " & _
            "ORDER BY dbo.inspectioncodes.tradename"

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Joe Dela Cruz                                                 *
    '   Created Date: 03/10/03                                                     *
    '   Modified Date: 4/17/03                                                     *
    '   Get inspection anomolies from iresults, inspectionreportdetails table for  *
    '   projects for a specific company                                            *
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetInspectionsBarChartData(ByVal companyid As Integer) As DataSet


        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim strSQL As String
        strSQL = "SELECT DISTINCT projects.name, count(projects.name) AS [Inspection With No Attentions],NULL AS [Attentions More Than 14 Days], NULL AS [Attentions Signed Off], NULL AS [Attentions Less Than 14 Days] " & _
                    "FROM    iresults INNER JOIN projects ON iresults.projectid = projects.projectid INNER JOIN " & _
                            "companies ON projects.companyid = companies.companyid " & _
                "WHERE (iresults.attention = 0) AND projects.companyid = " & companyid & _
                "GROUP BY projects.name " & _
                "ORDER BY projects.name"

        Try

            Dim ds1 As New DataSet()
            Dim cmdTotalInspections As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)

            ' Fill dataset for count for Total Inspection with no Attentions, but also
            ' include columns for count for attentions more than 14 days,
            ' count for attentions signed off, and count for attentions less than 14 days
            cmdTotalInspections.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim daTotalInspections As New SqlDataAdapter(cmdTotalInspections)
            daTotalInspections.Fill(ds1)

            ' Fill dataset for count for Total Attentions More Than 14 Days
            strSQL = "SELECT DISTINCT projects.name,count(projects.name) as anomoliesmorethan14dayscount " & _
                    "FROM    inspectionreportdetails INNER JOIN " & _
                    "iresults ON inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid = iresults.iresultsid AND " & _
                    "inspectionreportdetails.projectid = iresults.projectid INNER JOIN " & _
                    "projects ON iresults.projectid = projects.projectid INNER JOIN " & _
                    "companies ON projects.companyid = companies.companyid " & _
                    "WHERE   projects.companyid = " & companyid & " AND (inspectionreportdetails.sdate IS NULL) AND (iresults.attention = 1) " & _
                        "AND DATEDIFF(day,iresults.inspectiondate,GETDATE()) > 14 " & _
                    "GROUP BY projects.name " & _
                    "ORDER BY projects.name"

            Dim cmdAnomoliesMoreThan14days As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
            Dim daAnomoliesMoreThan14days As New SqlDataAdapter(cmdAnomoliesMoreThan14days)
            Dim ds2 As New DataSet()
            daAnomoliesMoreThan14days.TableMappings.Add("Table", "AnomoliesMoreThan14days")
            daAnomoliesMoreThan14days.Fill(ds2)

            Dim intRowCount As Integer = 0
            Dim intRowCount1 As Integer = 0
            Dim intLoopCounter As Integer = 0
            Dim intLoopCounter1 As Integer = 0
            Dim intCount As Integer = 0
            Dim strProjectName As String = ""

            ' Loop through dataset ds1 with Total Inspections
            ' with No Attentions, and populate column with
            ' count for Attention More Than 30 Days with
            ' count from ds2
            intRowCount = ds1.Tables(0).Rows.Count
            intRowCount1 = ds2.Tables(0).Rows.Count
            If intRowCount > 0 And intRowCount1 > 0 Then
                For intLoopCounter = 0 To intRowCount - 1
                    strProjectName = CStr(ds1.Tables(0).Rows(intLoopCounter).Item(0))
                    For intLoopCounter1 = 0 To intRowCount1 - 1
                        If strProjectName = CStr(ds2.Tables(0).Rows(intLoopCounter1).Item(0)) Then
                            ds1.Tables(0).Rows(intLoopCounter).Item(2) = ds2.Tables(0).Rows(intLoopCounter1).Item(1)
                            Exit For
                        End If
                    Next
                Next
            End If

            ' Fill dataset ds3 for count for Total Attentions Signed Off
            strSQL = "SELECT DISTINCT projects.name,count(projects.name) as anomoliesclosed " & _
                    "FROM    inspectionreportdetails INNER JOIN " & _
                    "iresults ON inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid = iresults.iresultsid AND " & _
                    "inspectionreportdetails.projectid = iresults.projectid INNER JOIN " & _
                    "projects ON iresults.projectid = projects.projectid INNER JOIN " & _
                    "companies ON projects.companyid = companies.companyid " & _
                    "WHERE   projects.companyid = " & companyid & " AND  (inspectionreportdetails.sdate IS NOT NULL) AND (iresults.attention = 1) " & _
                    "GROUP BY projects.name " & _
                    "ORDER BY projects.name"

            ' Loop through dataset ds1 with Total Inspections
            ' with No Attentions, and populate column with
            ' count for Attention Signed Off with
            ' count from ds3
            Dim cmdAnomoliesClosed As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
            Dim daAnomoliesClosed As New SqlDataAdapter(cmdAnomoliesClosed)
            Dim ds3 As New DataSet()
            daAnomoliesClosed.TableMappings.Add("Table", "AnomoliesClosed")
            daAnomoliesClosed.Fill(ds3)
            intRowCount1 = ds3.Tables(0).Rows.Count
            If intRowCount > 0 And intRowCount1 > 0 Then
                For intLoopCounter = 0 To intRowCount - 1
                    strProjectName = CStr(ds1.Tables(0).Rows(intLoopCounter).Item(0))
                    For intLoopCounter1 = 0 To intRowCount1 - 1
                        If strProjectName = CStr(ds3.Tables(0).Rows(intLoopCounter1).Item(0)) Then
                            ds1.Tables(0).Rows(intLoopCounter).Item(3) = ds3.Tables(0).Rows(intLoopCounter1).Item(1)
                            Exit For
                        End If
                    Next
                Next
            End If

            ' Fill dataset ds4 for count for Total Attentions Less Than 30 days
            strSQL = "SELECT DISTINCT projects.name,count(projects.name) as anomolieslessthan14days " & _
                    "FROM    inspectionreportdetails INNER JOIN " & _
                    "iresults ON inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid = iresults.iresultsid AND " & _
                    "inspectionreportdetails.projectid = iresults.projectid INNER JOIN " & _
                    "projects ON iresults.projectid = projects.projectid INNER JOIN " & _
                    "companies ON projects.companyid = companies.companyid " & _
                    "WHERE   projects.companyid = " & companyid & " AND (inspectionreportdetails.sdate IS NULL) AND (iresults.attention = 1) " & _
                        "AND DATEDIFF(day,iresults.inspectiondate,GETDATE()) < 14 " & _
                    "GROUP BY projects.name " & _
                    "ORDER BY projects.name"

            Dim cmdAnomoliesLessThan14days As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
            Dim daAnomoliesLessThan14days As New SqlDataAdapter(cmdAnomoliesLessThan14days)
            Dim ds4 As New DataSet()
            daAnomoliesLessThan14days.TableMappings.Add("Table", "AnomoliesLessThan14days")
            daAnomoliesLessThan14days.Fill(ds4)

            ' Loop through dataset ds1 with Total Inspections
            ' with No Attentions, and populate column with
            ' count for Attention Less Than 30 Days with
            ' count from ds4
            intRowCount1 = ds4.Tables(0).Rows.Count
            If intRowCount > 0 And intRowCount1 > 0 Then
                For intLoopCounter = 0 To intRowCount - 1
                    strProjectName = CStr(ds1.Tables(0).Rows(intLoopCounter).Item(0))
                    For intLoopCounter1 = 0 To intRowCount1 - 1
                        If strProjectName = CStr(ds4.Tables(0).Rows(intLoopCounter1).Item(0)) Then
                            ds1.Tables(0).Rows(intLoopCounter).Item(4) = ds4.Tables(0).Rows(intLoopCounter1).Item(1)
                            Exit For
                        End If
                    Next
                Next
            End If

            Return ds1

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    'test
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetInspectionSummary(ByVal companyid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim strSQL As String
        strSQL = "SELECT dbo.projects.name, dbo.iresults.inspectiondate, dbo.iresults.attention, dbo.inspectionreportdetails.sdate " & _
            "FROM dbo.iresults INNER JOIN " & _
                "dbo.projects ON dbo.iresults.projectid = dbo.projects.projectid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                "dbo.inspectionreportdetails ON dbo.iresults.iresultsid = dbo.inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid AND " & _
                "dbo.iresults.projectid = dbo.inspectionreportdetails.projectid " & _
            "WHERE dbo.projects.companyid = " & companyid

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: get douments filters for company and send back to vcnet
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function doc_getfilters(ByVal companyid As Integer) As String()

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

            sqlstmt = "SELECT count(*) " & _
                        "FROM companiesdocumentfilters INNER JOIN " & _
                            "documentfilters ON companiesdocumentfilters.documentfilter = documentfilters.documentfilter " & _
                        "WHERE companiesdocumentfilters.companyid = " & companyid

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            Dim x As Integer = 0
            Dim result(cnt - 1) As String

            sqlstmt = "SELECT documentfilters.filtertable, companiesdocumentfilters.documentfilter, companiesdocumentfilters.filterdisplay, " & _
                            "companiesdocumentfilters.companyid " & _
                        "FROM companiesdocumentfilters INNER JOIN " & _
                            "documentfilters ON companiesdocumentfilters.documentfilter = documentfilters.documentfilter " & _
                        "WHERE companiesdocumentfilters.companyid = " & companyid & " " & _
                        "ORDER BY companiesdocumentfilters.ordernbr"

            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result(x) = sqlDr.GetString(0) & "." & sqlDr.GetString(1) & "^" & sqlDr.GetString(2)
                x = x + 1
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            SqlConn.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Dim result(2) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = CStr(ex.Number)
            result(2) = ex.Message

            Return result
        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: get drop down results for each combobox
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function doc_getresults(ByVal field As String, ByVal table As String, ByVal projid As Integer) As String()

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

            sqlstmt = "SELECT count(DISTINCT " & field & ") " & _
                        "FROM " & table & " " & _
                        "WHERE documentid in (SELECT documentid FROM documents WHERE projectid  = " & projid & ")"

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            Dim x As Integer = 0
            Dim result(cnt - 1) As String

            sqlstmt = "SELECT DISTINCT " & field & " " & _
                        "FROM " & table & " " & _
                        "WHERE documentid in (SELECT documentid FROM documents WHERE projectid  = " & projid & ") " & _
                        "ORDER BY " & field

            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result(x) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                x = x + 1
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            SqlConn.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Dim result(2) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = CStr(ex.Number)
            result(2) = ex.Message

            Return result
        End Try

    End Function


    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: get drop down results for each combobox based off other 
    '           filters
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function doc_getresults2(ByVal field As String, ByVal whereclause As String) As String()

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT count(DISTINCT " & field & ") " & _
                        "FROM warranties RIGHT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "documentdates RIGHT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "inspectioncategories RIGHT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "documents ON inspectioncategories.documentid = documents.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "inspectionreportdetails ON dbo.documents.documentid = dbo.inspectionreportdetails.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "companies INNER JOIN " & _
                            "documentauthors ON companies.companyid = documentauthors.companyid ON  " & _
                            "documents.documentid = documentauthors.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "documentlocations ON documents.documentid = documentlocations.documentid ON " & _
                            "documentdates.documentid = documents.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "documenttitles ON documents.documentid = documenttitles.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "phases ON documents.documentid = phases.documentid ON warranties.documentid = documents.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "plans LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "plantypes ON plans.documentid = plantypes.documentid AND plans.pagenbr = plantypes.pagenbr LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "details ON plans.documentid = details.documentid AND plans.pagenbr = details.pagenbr ON " & _
                            "documents.documentid = plans.documentid " & _
                        "WHERE " & whereclause & " "

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar)

            Dim x As Integer = 0
            Dim result(cnt - 1) As String
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            sqlstmt = "SELECT DISTINCT " & field & " " & _
                        "FROM warranties RIGHT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "documentdates RIGHT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "inspectioncategories RIGHT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "documents ON inspectioncategories.documentid = documents.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "inspectionreportdetails ON documents.documentid = inspectionreportdetails.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "companies INNER JOIN " & _
                            "documentauthors ON companies.companyid = documentauthors.companyid ON " & _
                            "documents.documentid = documentauthors.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "documentlocations ON documents.documentid = documentlocations.documentid ON " & _
                            "documentdates.documentid = documents.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "documenttitles ON documents.documentid = documenttitles.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "phases ON documents.documentid = phases.documentid ON warranties.documentid = documents.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "plans LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "plantypes ON plans.documentid = plantypes.documentid AND plans.pagenbr = plantypes.pagenbr LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "details ON plans.documentid = details.documentid AND plans.pagenbr = details.pagenbr ON " & _
                            "documents.documentid = plans.documentid " & _
                        "WHERE " & whereclause & " " & _
                        "AND " & field & " is not null " & _
                        "ORDER BY " & field

            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result(x) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                x = x + 1
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Dim result(2) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = CStr(ex.Number)
            result(2) = ex.Message

            Return result

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Closed Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: get document info for document selected
    '           filters
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function doc_getdocinfo(ByVal docid As Integer) As String()

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

            sqlstmt = "SELECT documents.docfilename, documents.documentcategory, documentdates.documentdate, documenttitles.title, companies.name, " & _
                        "documentlocations.location, inspectioncategories.inspectioncategory, plans.planscategory, plans.pagenbr, details.detailnbr, " & _
                        "plantypes.plantype, phases.phase, warranties.effectivedate, warranties.expirationdate " & _
                        "FROM warranties RIGHT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "documentdates RIGHT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "inspectioncategories RIGHT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "documents ON inspectioncategories.documentid = documents.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "companies INNER JOIN " & _
                            "documentauthors ON companies.companyid = documentauthors.companyid ON " & _
                            "documents.documentid = documentauthors.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "documentlocations ON documents.documentid = documentlocations.documentid ON " & _
                            "documentdates.documentid = documents.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "documenttitles ON documents.documentid = documenttitles.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "phases ON documents.documentid = phases.documentid ON warranties.documentid = documents.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "plans LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "plantypes ON plans.documentid = plantypes.documentid AND plans.pagenbr = plantypes.pagenbr LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "details ON plans.documentid = details.documentid AND plans.pagenbr = details.pagenbr ON " & _
                            "documents.documentid = plans.documentid " & _
                    "WHERE documents.documentid = " & docid & " "

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim x As Integer = 0
            Dim result(13) As String

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            Dim temp As String
            While sqlDr.Read
                For x = 0 To 13
                    If sqlDr.IsDBNull(x) = True Then
                        temp = ""
                    Else
                        temp = sqlDr.GetValue(x).ToString
                    End If

                    If result(x) = "" Then
                        result(x) = temp
                    ElseIf result(x) <> temp Then
                        result(x) = "multiple"
                    End If
                Next
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()
            SqlConn.Close()

            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Dim result(2) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = CStr(ex.Number)
            result(2) = ex.Message

            Return result
        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function doc_getnotes(ByVal whereclause As String) As DataSet

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT people.fname + ' ' + people.lname AS author, documentnotes.notedate, documentnotes.docnote " & _
                    "FROM documentnotes INNER JOIN " & _
                      "people ON documentnotes.peopleid = people.peopleid " & _
                    "WHERE " & whereclause & " ORDER BY notedate"

        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            'Return result
        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function doc_insertnotes(ByVal docid As Integer, ByVal peopleid As Integer, ByVal note As String) As String

        note = Replace(note, "'", "''")

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO documentnotes (documentid, peopleid, notedate, docnote) " & _
                    "VALUES (" & docid & ", " & peopleid & ", '" & Now & "', '" & note & "')"

        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
            SqlConn.Close()

            Return "true"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function doc_getcategories(ByVal projectid As Integer) As String()

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

            sqlstmt = "SELECT count(DISTINCT documentcategory) " & _
                        "FROM documents WHERE projectid = " & projectid

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            Dim x As Integer = 0
            Dim result(cnt - 1) As String

            sqlstmt = "SELECT DISTINCT documentcategory " & _
                        "FROM documents WHERE projectid = " & projectid

            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result(x) = sqlDr.GetString(0)
                x = x + 1
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            SqlConn.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Dim result(1) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

            Return result
        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: get dataset of all document without homeowneer access
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function doc_getnoaccess(ByVal projectid As Integer, ByVal custid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT documents.documentid, documents.docfilename, documents.documentcategory, documenttitles.title, companies.name AS author " & _
                        "FROM companies INNER JOIN " & _
                          "documentauthors ON companies.companyid = documentauthors.companyid RIGHT OUTER JOIN " & _
                          "documents ON documentauthors.documentid = documents.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                          "documenttitles ON documents.documentid = documenttitles.documentid " & _
                        "WHERE (documents.projectid = " & projectid & ") " & _
                        "AND documents.documentid NOT in (SELECT documentid FROM documentsaccess WHERE peopleid = " & custid & ")"


        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: get dataset of all document with homeowneer access
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function doc_getaccess(ByVal projectid As Integer, ByVal pplid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT documents.documentid, documents.docfilename, documents.documentcategory, documenttitles.title, companies.name AS coname " & _
                        "FROM companies INNER JOIN " & _
                          "documentauthors ON companies.companyid = documentauthors.companyid RIGHT OUTER JOIN " & _
                          "documents ON documentauthors.documentid = documents.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                          "documenttitles ON documents.documentid = documenttitles.documentid " & _
                        "WHERE ((documents.documentid IN " & _
                          "(SELECT documentid " & _
                          "FROM documentsaccess WHERE peopleid = " & pplid & "))) AND (documents.projectid = " & projectid & ")"

        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            'Return result
        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************

    '   Event:  function to insert documents by category into documentaccess
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function doc_insertbycat(ByVal projid As Integer, ByVal category As String, ByVal custid As Integer) As String

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO documentsaccess (documentid, peopleid) " & _
            "SELECT documentid, " & custid & " FROM documents " & _
            "WHERE projectid = " & projid & " " & _
            "AND documentcategory = '" & category & "' " & _
            "AND documentid not in (SELECT documentid FROM documentsaccess WHERE peopleid = " & custid & ")"

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
            SqlConn.Close()

            Return "true"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event:  function to return address(s) for homeowmers -- for web
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function doc_getoccupants(ByVal peopleid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT homes.projectid, homes.address, projects.city, projects.state, projects.zip, buildersco.directory AS codir, " & _
                      "projects.directory AS projdir, homes.houseid " & _
                    "FROM homes INNER JOIN " & _
                      "projects ON homes.projectid = projects.projectid INNER JOIN " & _
                      "buildersco ON projects.companyid = buildersco.companyid " & _
                    "WHERE (homes.houseid IN " & _
                     "(SELECT houseid FROM occupants " & _
                    "WHERE peopleid = " & peopleid & "))"
        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: get dataset of all documents with homeowneer access for 
    '          home selected
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function doc_homeowner(ByVal projid As Integer, ByVal address As String) As DataSet

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT documents.docfilename, documents.documentcategory, documenttitles.title, companies.name " & _
                    "FROM companies INNER JOIN " & _
                      "documentauthors ON companies.companyid = documentauthors.companyid RIGHT OUTER JOIN " & _
                      "documents ON documentauthors.documentid = documents.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                      "documenttitles ON documents.documentid = documenttitles.documentid " & _
                    "WHERE (documents.projectid = " & projid & ") AND (documents.documentid IN " & _
                      "(SELECT documentid " & _
                      "FROM documentsaccess)) AND (documents.documentid NOT IN " & _
                      "(SELECT documentid " & _
                      "FROM documentlocations)) OR " & _
                      "(documents.projectid = " & projid & ") AND (documents.documentid IN " & _
                      "(SELECT documentid " & _
                      "FROM documentsaccess)) AND (documents.documentid IN " & _
                      "(SELECT documentid " & _
                    "FROM documentlocations " & _
                    "WHERE location = '" & address & "'))"

        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            'Return result
        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   get path for developer and sent to vcnet
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function doc_getDevPath(ByVal coid As Integer) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim companyid As Integer
        Dim result As String

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("SELECT directory FROM buildersco " & _
                "WHERE companyid  = " & coid, SqlConn)
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                result = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            result = "ERROR" & ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message
            Return result

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function doc_addmasterdetail(ByVal companyid As Integer, ByVal filename As String, ByVal title As String, ByVal category As String) As String

        filename = Replace(filename, "'", "''")
        title = Replace(title, "'", "''")
        category = Replace(category, "'", "''")

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlstmt As String

        Try
            sqlstmt = LCase("INSERT INTO masterdetails (companyid, docfilename, title, category) " & _
                    "VALUES (" & companyid & ", '" & filename & "', '" & title & "', '" & category & "')")

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            Return "true"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If
        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function sir_getmgmt(ByVal companyid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT remindernbr, reminderto, dayinterval, deliverymethod, remindertext " & _
                    "FROM sirmgmt " & _
                    "WHERE companyid = " & companyid

            'Return result
        End Try

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: add reminder to sirmgmt
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function sir_addmgmt(ByVal companyid As Integer, ByVal rto As String, ByVal days As Integer, ByVal method As String, ByVal rtext As String) As String

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT remindernbr FROM sirmgmt " & _
            "WHERE companyid = " & companyid
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
        Dim reminder As Integer = 0

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                reminder = CInt(sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString)
            End While
            SqlConn.Close()

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        End Try

        reminder += 1
        sqlstmt = LCase("INSERT INTO sirmgmt (companyid, remindernbr, reminderto,  dayinterval, deliverymethod, remindertext) " & _
                "VALUES (" & companyid & ", " & reminder & ", '" & rto & "', '" & days & "', '" & method & "', '" & rtext & "')")

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            SqlConn.Close()

            Return "true"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: update sir reminders with new values set by user
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function sir_editmgmt(ByVal coid As Integer, ByVal remindnbr As Integer, ByVal rto As String, ByVal days As Integer, ByVal method As String, ByVal rtext As String) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = LCase("UPDATE sirmgmt SET reminderto = '" & rto & "', dayinterval ='" & days & "', " & _
                        "deliverymethod = '" & method & "', remindertext= '" & rtext & "' " & _
                "WHERE companyid = " & coid & " AND remindernbr = " & remindnbr)

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: send all sirnbrs in dataset for selected project to vcnet
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function sir_getdocs(ByVal companyid As Integer, ByVal projid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim sqlstmt As String

        If projid = 0 Then
            sqlstmt = "SELECT documentid, left(docfilename, len(docfilename)-4) " & _
                "FROM documents " & _
                "WHERE documentid in (SELECT documentid FROM inspectionreportdetails) " & _
                "AND projectid in (select projectid from projects where companyid = " & companyid & ") " & _
                "ORDER BY docfilename"
        Else
            sqlstmt = "SELECT documentid, left(docfilename, len(docfilename)-4) " & _
                "FROM documents " & _
                "WHERE documentid in (SELECT documentid FROM inspectionreportdetails) " & _
                "AND projectid = " & projid & " " & _
                "ORDER BY docfilename"
        End If

        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            'Return result
        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function sir_getitems(ByVal docid As Integer) As DataSet

            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: signoff sir item(s)
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function sir_signoff(ByVal docid As Integer, ByVal item As String, ByVal sdate As Date) As String

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        If item = "(all)" Then
            sqlstmt = LCase("UPDATE inspectionreportdetails SET sdate ='" & sdate & "' " & _
                "WHERE documentid = " & docid)
        Else
            sqlstmt = LCase("UPDATE inspectionreportdetails SET sdate ='" & sdate & "' " & _
                            "WHERE documentid = " & docid & " AND iresultsid = '" & item & "'")
        End If

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
            SqlConn.Close()

            Return "true"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: get phone and fax number of employee selected
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function sir_getpplphone(ByVal pplid As Integer) As String()

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT phone, fax, email FROM people WHERE peopleid = " & pplid

        Try
            Dim result(2) As String
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            SqlConn.Open()

            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result(0) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                result(1) = sqlDr.GetValue(1).ToString
                result(2) = sqlDr.GetValue(2).ToString
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Dim result(2) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = CStr(ex.Number)
            result(2) = ex.Message
            Return result

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Joe Dela Cruz                                                 *
    '   Created Date: 02/3/03                                                      *
    '   Modified Date:  4/22/03                                                    *
    '   Check if company has divisions checking divisions table                    *
    '   using sending company id parameter                                         *
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function CheckCompanyDivisions(ByVal companyid As Integer) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim strSQL As String = "SELECT count(*) FROM divisions WHERE companyid = @companyid"
        Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
        Dim cnt As Integer

        Try
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@companyid", companyid)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(cmd.ExecuteScalar.ToString)
            SqlConn.Close()

            Return cnt.ToString

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Message & "^" & ex.Number.ToString

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Joe Dela Cruz                                                 *
    '   Created Date: 02/3/03                                                      *
    '   Modified Date:  4/22/03                                                    *
    '   Get company information for companies with no division                     *
    '   using sending company id parameter                                         *
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetCompanyInfo(ByVal companyid As Integer) As String()

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim result(5) As String
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            SqlConn.Open()

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT address,city,state,zip,phone,fax FROM companies WHERE companyid = @companyid", SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@companyid", companyid)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result(0) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString 'company address
                result(1) = sqlDr.GetValue(1).ToString 'company city
                result(2) = sqlDr.GetValue(2).ToString 'company state
                result(3) = sqlDr.GetValue(3).ToString 'company zip
                result(4) = sqlDr.GetValue(4).ToString 'company phone
                result(5) = sqlDr.GetValue(5).ToString 'company fax                
            End While

            sqlDr.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Dim result(2) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = CStr(ex.Number)
            result(2) = ex.Message
            Return result

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Joe Dela Cruz                                                 *
    '   Created Date: 02/3/03                                                      *
    '   Modified Date:  4/22/03                                                    *
    '   Get division information for companies                                     * 
    '   using sending company id parameter                                         *
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetDivisionInfo(ByVal companyid As Integer, ByVal division As String) As String()

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM divisions WHERE companyid=@companyid AND division=@division", SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@companyid", companyid)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@division", division)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()

            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While

            sqlDr.Close()

            Dim result(5) As String
            If cnt = 0 Then
                SqlConn.Close()
                result(0) = "Error"
                result(1) = "0"
                result(2) = "No record for this division"
                Return result
            End If

            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("SELECT address,city,state,zip,phone,fax FROM divisions WHERE companyid = @companyid AND division=@division", SqlConn)
            sqlCmd1.Parameters.Add("@companyid", companyid)
            sqlCmd1.Parameters.Add("@division", division)
            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result(0) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString 'division address
                result(1) = sqlDr.GetValue(1).ToString 'division city
                result(2) = sqlDr.GetValue(2).ToString 'division state
                result(3) = sqlDr.GetValue(3).ToString 'division zip
                result(4) = sqlDr.GetValue(4).ToString 'division phone
                result(5) = sqlDr.GetValue(5).ToString 'division fax                
            End While

            sqlDr.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Dim result(2) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = CStr(ex.Number)
            result(2) = ex.Message
            Return result

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '   Created Date: 02/3/03                                                      *
    '   Modified Date:  4/21/03                                                    *
    '   Get divisions that a reminder needs to be sent out for all companies       *
    '   using sending company id parameter                                         *
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetDivisionReminders(ByVal sendingcompanyid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim strSQL As String

        ' Use two queries joined with UNION 
        ' to get all divisions that need to send out reminders
        strSQL = "SELECT dbo.divisions.division, dbo.divisions.divisiondesc " & _
                "FROM dbo.divisions INNER JOIN " & _
                    "dbo.projectstodivisions ON dbo.divisions.companyid = dbo.projectstodivisions.companyid AND  " & _
                    " dbo.divisions.division = dbo.projectstodivisions.division " & _
                 "WHERE (dbo.projectstodivisions.projectid IN " & _
                    "(SELECT dbo.iresults.projectid " & _
                    "FROM dbo.people INNER JOIN " & _
                        "dbo.iresults ON dbo.people.peopleid = dbo.iresults.ins_peopleid INNER JOIN " & _
                        "dbo.inspectionreportdetails ON dbo.iresults.projectid = dbo.inspectionreportdetails.projectid AND " & _
                        "dbo.iresults.iresultsid = dbo.inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                        "dbo.inspectionreportreminders ON dbo.inspectionreportdetails.documentid = dbo.inspectionreportreminders.documentid AND " & _
                        "dbo.inspectionreportdetails.projectid = dbo.inspectionreportreminders.projectid AND " & _
                        "dbo.inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid = dbo.inspectionreportreminders.iresultsid " & _
                    "WHERE (dbo.inspectionreportdetails.sdate IS NULL) AND (dbo.iresults.attention = 1) AND (dbo.iresults.projectid <> 1) " & _
                    "GROUP BY dbo.iresults.iresultsid, dbo.iresults.projectid " & _
                    "HAVING ((COUNT(dbo.inspectionreportreminders.reminder) + 1) IN " & _
                        "(SELECT remindernbr " & _
                        "FROM dbo.sirmgmt " & _
                        "WHERE (remindernbr = 1 AND DATEDIFF(day, MAX(iresults.inspectiondate), GETDATE()) >= dayinterval) OR " & _
                            "(remindernbr > 1 AND DATEDIFF(day, MAX(inspectionreportreminders.reminder), GETDATE()) >= dayinterval))))) AND " & _
                        "(dbo.projectstodivisions.companyid = 2) " & _
                "GROUP BY dbo.divisions.division, dbo.divisions.divisiondesc "

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim ds As New DataSet()
        Try
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@sendingcompanyid", sendingcompanyid)
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)
            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Joe Dela Cruz                                                 *
    '   Created Date: 02/3/03                                                      *
    '   Modified Date:  4/21/03                                                    *
    '   Get each project that a reminder needs to be sent out for all companies    *
    '   using sending company id parameter and division parameter                  *
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetSIRReminders(ByVal sendingcompanyid As Integer, ByVal division As String) As DataSet

        Dim strSQL As String

        strSQL = "sp_GetSIRReminders"
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim ds As New DataSet()

        Try

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.StoredProcedure
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@sendingcompanyid", sendingcompanyid)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@division", division)
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)
            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Joe Dela Cruz                                                 *
    '   Created Date: 01/21/03                                                     *
    '   Modified Date: 05/08/03                                                    *
    '   Get SIR Open Items data to use in SIR Open Items Report for a specific     *
    '   project id and reminder number that are passed in as parameters.           *
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetSIROpenItems(ByVal projid As Integer, ByVal reminderno As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim strSQL As String

        ' If reminders for iresultsid are in inspectionreportreminders table,
        ' use the SQL statement below to query which iresultsids are on 
        ' the reminder number passed in as a parameter
        If reminderno > 0 Then
            strSQL = "SELECT documents.documentid,LEFT(documents.docfilename, LEN(documents.docfilename) - 4) AS sirnbr, " & _
                        "dbo.inspectionreportreminders.iresultsid, iresults.inspectiondate, inspectioncodes.cat1, " & _
                        "iresults.lot, iresults.phase, projects.name, iresults.bldg, companies.name AS CompanyName " & _
                    "FROM         dbo.inspectionreportreminders INNER JOIN " & _
                          "dbo.iresults ON dbo.inspectionreportreminders.projectid = dbo.iresults.projectid AND  " & _
                          "dbo.inspectionreportreminders.iresultsid = dbo.iresults.iresultsid INNER JOIN " & _
                          "dbo.inspectionreportdetails ON dbo.inspectionreportreminders.projectid = dbo.inspectionreportdetails.projectid AND  " & _
                          "dbo.inspectionreportreminders.iresultsid = dbo.inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid INNER JOIN " & _
                          "dbo.inspectioncodes ON dbo.iresults.code = dbo.inspectioncodes.code INNER JOIN " & _
                          "dbo.projects ON dbo.iresults.projectid = dbo.projects.projectid INNER JOIN " & _
                          "dbo.companies ON projects.companyid = companies.companyid INNER JOIN " & _
                          "dbo.documents ON dbo.inspectionreportdetails.documentid = dbo.documents.documentid " & _
                    "WHERE (iresults.projectid = " & projid & ") AND (inspectionreportdetails.sdate Is NULL) " & _
                    "AND DATEDIFF(day,inspectionreportreminders.reminder,GETDATE()) >= (SELECT DISTINCT dbo.sirmgmt.dayinterval FROM dbo.iresults INNER JOIN dbo.people ON dbo.iresults.ins_peopleid = dbo.people.peopleid INNER JOIN dbo.sirmgmt ON dbo.people.companyid = dbo.sirmgmt.companyid WHERE remindernbr= " & reminderno & " AND iresults.projectid= " & projid & " AND deliverymethod='fax') " & _
                    "GROUP BY documents.documentid,LEFT(documents.docfilename, LEN(documents.docfilename) - 4),  dbo.inspectionreportreminders.iresultsid, dbo.iresults.inspectiondate, dbo.inspectioncodes.cat1, " & _
                    "iresults.lot,iresults.phase,dbo.projects.name, iresults.bldg,companies.name " & _
                    "HAVING COUNT(*) = " & reminderno & _
                    "ORDER BY iresults.phase,inspectionreportreminders.iresultsid"
            ' If there are iresultsids with no reminders sent out yet, use the following
            ' SQL statement below to query those iresultsids
        Else
            strSQL = "SELECT documents.documentid,LEFT(documents.docfilename, LEN(documents.docfilename) - 4) AS sirnbr, " & _
                        "inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid, iresults.inspectiondate, inspectioncodes.cat1, " & _
                    "iresults.lot, iresults.phase, projects.name, iresults.bldg, companies.name AS CompanyName " & _
                    "FROM inspectionreportdetails INNER JOIN " & _
                      "iresults ON inspectionreportdetails.projectid = iresults.projectid AND " & _
                      "inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid = iresults.iresultsid INNER JOIN " & _
                      "inspectioncodes ON iresults.code = inspectioncodes.code INNER JOIN " & _
                      "documents ON inspectionreportdetails.documentid = documents.documentid INNER JOIN " & _
                      "projects ON inspectionreportdetails.projectid = projects.projectid INNER JOIN " & _
                      "companies ON projects.companyid = companies.companyid " & _
                    "WHERE (iresults.projectid = " & projid & ") AND (inspectionreportdetails.sdate Is NULL) " & _
                     "AND inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid NOT IN (SELECT inspectionreportreminders.iresultsid FROM inspectionreportreminders WHERE projectid=  " & projid & ")" & _
                     "AND DATEDIFF(day,iresults.inspectiondate,GETDATE()) >= (SELECT DISTINCT dbo.sirmgmt.dayinterval FROM dbo.iresults INNER JOIN dbo.people ON dbo.iresults.ins_peopleid = dbo.people.peopleid INNER JOIN dbo.sirmgmt ON dbo.people.companyid = dbo.sirmgmt.companyid WHERE remindernbr= 1 AND iresults.projectid= " & projid & " AND deliverymethod='fax') " & _
                    "ORDER BY iresults.phase,inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid "

        End If

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If
        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function updatereminder(ByVal docid As Integer, ByVal projid As Integer, ByVal iresultsid As String) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO inspectionreportreminders (documentid, projectid, iresultsid, reminder) " & _
                    "VALUES (" & docid & ", " & projid & ", '" & iresultsid & "', '" & Today.ToShortDateString & "')"

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
            SqlConn.Close()

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event:  check if person is in db and if not, add sir notifcation
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function sir_addnotification(ByVal pplid As Integer, ByVal projid As Integer) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim cnt As Integer = 0
        Dim email As String
        Dim result As String

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT count(*) FROM sirnotifications " & _
                        "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid & " " & _
                        "AND projectid = " & projid

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar)

            If cnt = 0 Then
                sqlstmt = "SELECT email FROM people " & _
                    "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid
                Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text

                If LCase(sqlCmd1.ExecuteScalar.GetType.ToString) = "system.dbnull" Then
                    email = ""
                Else
                    email = CStr(sqlCmd1.ExecuteScalar)
                End If

                If email <> "" Then
                    sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO sirnotifications (peopleid, projectid)  " & _
                                            "VALUES (" & pplid & ", " & projid & ")"
                    Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                    cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                    cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
                    result = "True"
                Else
                    result = "NoEmail"
                End If
            End If

            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

                                        "VALUES (" & pplid & ", " & projid & ")"
                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

                cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then

            End If

        End Try
