    '*******************************************************************************
    ' Programmer:  Patricia Kline
    ' Created Date:  3/29/03                                               
    ' Modified Date:                                                      
    ' Event: Get sir items
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function Get_SirReminder(ByVal docid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim ds As New DataSet()

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT dbo.companies.name AS coname, dbo.iresults.iresultsid, dbo.iresults.inspectiondate, dbo.iresults.phase, dbo.iresults.lot, dbo.iresults.bldg, " & _
                            "dbo.iresults.unit, dbo.iresults.plantype, dbo.iresults.comment, dbo.iresults.attention, dbo.inspectioncodes.tradename, dbo.projects.name AS projname, " & _
                            "dbo.people.fname + ' ' + dbo.people.lname AS super, people_1.fname AS inspector, left(dbo.documents.docfilename, len(dbo.documents.docfilename) - 4) as sir " & _
                        "FROM dbo.iresults INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.projects ON dbo.iresults.projectid = dbo.projects.projectid INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.companies ON dbo.projects.companyid = dbo.companies.companyid INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.inspectioncodes ON dbo.iresults.code = dbo.inspectioncodes.code INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.superintendantsprojects ON dbo.iresults.projectid = dbo.superintendantsprojects.projectid AND " & _
                            "dbo.iresults.inspectiondate = dbo.superintendantsprojects.inspectiondate INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.people ON dbo.superintendantsprojects.peopleid = dbo.people.peopleid INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.people people_1 ON dbo.iresults.ins_peopleid = people_1.peopleid INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.inspectionreports ON dbo.iresults.iresultsid = dbo.inspectionreports.iresultsid AND " & _
                            "dbo.iresults.projectid = dbo.inspectionreports.projectid INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.documents ON dbo.inspectionreports.documentid = dbo.documents.documentid AND " & _
                            "dbo.inspectionreports.projectid = dbo.documents.projectid " & _
                        "WHERE (dbo.documents.documentid = " & docid & ")"

            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function msg_addfolder(ByVal pplid As Integer, ByVal folder As String) As String

        folder = Replace(folder, "'", "''")

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = LCase("INSERT INTO folders (peopleid, folder) " & _
                        "VALUES (" & pplid & ", '" & folder & "')")

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            Return "true"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: folder list for user
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function msg_getfolders(ByVal pplid As Integer) As String()

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM folders " & _
                "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid, SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            Dim x As Integer = 0
            Dim result(cnt - 1) As String
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("SELECT folder FROM folders " & _
                "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid & " ORDER BY folder", SqlConn)
            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result(x) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                x = x + 1
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Dim result(2) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = CStr(ex.Number)
            result(2) = ex.Message
            Return result

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '   Programmer: Marc Olano

    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function msg_getfolders_web(ByVal pplid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT 'Inbox' as folder UNION SELECT 'Sent Items'"

        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)

            sqlstmt = "SELECT folder FROM folders " & _
                "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid & " ORDER BY folder"
            Dim cmd2 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            cmd2.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da2 As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd2)

            da2.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()

        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function msg_getavailcontacts(ByVal companyid As Integer, ByVal pplid As Integer, ByVal group As String) As DataSet

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT people.peopleid, people.fname + ' ' + people.lname AS name " & _
                    "FROM users users INNER JOIN " & _
                      "people people ON users.peopleid = people.peopleid " & _
                "WHERE (companyid = " & companyid & " OR accessible = 1) " & _
                "AND users.peopleid not in (1, " & pplid & ") " & _
                "AND users.peopleid not in (SELECT contactid FROM addressbook WHERE groupname = '" & group & "' " & _
                "AND peopleid = " & pplid & ")"

        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            'Return result
        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function msg_getaddrbook(ByVal pplid As Integer, ByVal group As String) As DataSet

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT peopleid, fname + ' ' + lname AS name FROM people " & _
                "WHERE peopleid in (SELECT contactid FROM addressbook " & _
                    "WHERE groupname = '" & group & "' AND peopleid = " & pplid & ")"

        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            'Return result
        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: group list for user
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function msg_getgroups(ByVal pplid As Integer) As String()

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(DISTINCT groupname) FROM addressbook " & _
                "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid & " AND groupname <> 'addrbook'", SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            Dim x As Integer = 0
            Dim result(cnt - 1) As String
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("SELECT DISTINCT groupname FROM addressbook " & _
                "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid & " AND groupname <> 'addrbook' ORDER BY groupname", SqlConn)
            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result(x) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                x = x + 1
            End While

            sqlDr.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Dim result(2) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = CStr(ex.Number)
            result(2) = ex.Message
            Return result

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: John Ashbaugh            
    '   Created Date: 3/27/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: count of unread msgs
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function msg_getmsgsUnReadCount(ByVal pplid As Integer, ByVal folder As String) As Integer

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT count(*) " & _
                    "FROM messages INNER JOIN users ON messages.msgfrom = users.peopleid INNER JOIN people ON users.peopleid = people.peopleid INNER JOIN messagestousers ON messages.msgid = messagestousers.msgid AND messages.msgfrom = messagestousers.msgfrom AND messages.msgsource = messagestousers.msgsource " & _
                    "WHERE messagestousers.msgto in (1, " & pplid & ") " & _
                    "AND msgfolder = '" & folder & "' AND messagestousers.isdeleted = 0 AND messagestousers.isread = 0"
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
        Dim count As Integer
        cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
        Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
        Try
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                count = CInt(sqlDr.GetValue(0))
            End While
            SqlConn.Close()
            Return count
        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
            SqlConn.Close()
        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: list of messages in folder for user
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function msg_getmessages(ByVal pplid As Integer, ByVal folder As String) As DataSet

        folder = Replace(folder, "'", "''")

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT messages.msgid, people.fname + ' ' + people.lname AS mfrom, messages.msgdate, messages.subject, messages.msg, " & _
                       "messages.status, messages.msgfrom, messages.msgsource, messagestousers.msgto " & _
                    "FROM messages INNER JOIN " & _
                        "messagestousers ON messages.msgid = messagestousers.msgid AND messages.msgfrom = messagestousers.msgfrom AND " & _
                        "messages.msgsource = messagestousers.msgsource INNER JOIN " & _
                        "people ON messages.msgfrom = people.peopleid " & _
                    "WHERE messagestousers.msgto in (1, " & pplid & ") " & _
                    "AND msgfolder = '" & folder & "' AND messagestousers.isdeleted = 0"

        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)
            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: get names of people that the message was to
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function msg_getmsgto(ByVal msgid As Integer, ByVal pplfrom As Integer, ByVal source As Integer) As String()

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM users " & _
                    "WHERE peopleid in (SELECT msgto FROM messagestousers " & _
                    "WHERE msgid = " & msgid & " " & _
                    "AND msgfrom = " & pplfrom & " " & _
                    "AND msgsource = " & source & " " & _
                    "AND (msgto <> msgfrom OR msgto = 1))", SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            Dim x As Integer = 0
            Dim result(cnt - 1) As String
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("SELECT fname + ' ' + lname as name FROM people " & _
                    "WHERE peopleid in (SELECT msgto FROM messagestousers " & _
                    "WHERE msgid = " & msgid & " " & _
                    "AND msgfrom = " & pplfrom & " " & _
                    "AND msgsource = " & source & " " & _
                    "AND (msgto <> msgfrom OR msgto = 1))", SqlConn)
            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result(x) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                x = x + 1
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            SqlConn.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Dim result(1) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

            Return result
        End Try

    End Function

                    "AND msgto = " & mto

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            SqlConn.Close()

        Catch ex As SqlException

            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function msg_updatefolder(ByVal id As Integer, ByVal from As Integer, ByVal source As Integer, ByVal mto As Integer, ByVal folder As String) As String

        folder = Replace(folder, "'", "''")

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = LCase("UPDATE messagestousers SET msgfolder = '" & folder & "' " & _
                    "WHERE msgid = " & id & " " & _
                    "AND msgfrom = " & from & " " & _
                    "AND msgsource = " & source & " " & _
                    "AND msgto = " & mto)

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            Return "true"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: Patricia Kline             
    '   Created Date:  12/1/02                 
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event:  function to insert new msg into database 
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function msg_addmsg(ByVal coid As Integer, ByVal pplfrom As Integer, ByVal pplto As String, ByVal groupto As String, ByVal subject As String, ByVal Msg As String, ByVal status As Integer) As String

        Msg = Replace(Msg, "'", "")
        subject = Replace(subject, "'", "")

        Dim msgincr As Integer
        Dim sqlConn2 As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim strMsgIncr As String
        Dim result As String
        Dim sqlstmt As String

        'need to figure out auto id # for inserting of msgs
        Try
            sqlstmt = "SELECT MAX(msgid) FROM messages " & _
                "WHERE msgsource = " & coid & " " & _
                "AND msgfrom = " & pplfrom

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, sqlConn2)

            sqlConn2.Open()
            If LCase(sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar().GetType.tostring) <> "system.dbnull" Then
                msgincr = CInt(sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar())
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            result = "Error^" & ex.Message & ex.Number.ToString
            Return result

        Finally
            If Not sqlConn2.State = ConnectionState.Closed Then
                sqlConn2.Close()
            End If

        End Try

        msgincr += 1

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(LCase("INSERT INTO messages (msgid, msgfrom, msgsource, msg, status, subject) " & _
                    "VALUES (" & msgincr & ", " & pplfrom & ", " & coid & ", '" & Msg & "','" & status & "', '" & subject & "')"), sqlConn2)

            If Not sqlConn2.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                sqlConn2.Open()
            End If

            sqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

        Catch ex As SqlException
            result = "Error^" & ex.Message & ex.Number.ToString
            Return result

        Finally
            If Not sqlConn2.State = ConnectionState.Closed Then
                sqlConn2.Close()
            End If

        End Try

        Dim where As String

        sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO messagestousers (msgid, msgfrom, msgsource, msgto, msgfolder) " & _
                 "SELECT " & msgincr & ", " & pplfrom & ", " & coid & ", peopleid, 'inbox' FROM people "

        If pplto <> "" Then
            where = "WHERE peopleid in (" & pplto & ")"
        End If

        If groupto <> "" Then
            If where = "" Then
                where = "WHERE peopleid in (SELECT contactid FROM addressbook " & _
                    "WHERE peopleid = " & pplfrom & " AND groupname in (" & groupto & "))"
            Else
                where = where & " OR peopleid in (SELECT contactid FROM addressbook " & _
                    "WHERE peopleid = " & pplfrom & " AND groupname in (" & groupto & "))"
            End If
        End If

        sqlstmt = sqlstmt & where

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, sqlConn2)

            If Not sqlConn2.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                sqlConn2.Open()
            End If

            sqlCmd1.ExecuteNonQuery()

        Catch ex As SqlException
            result = "Error^" & ex.Message & ex.Number.ToString
            Return result

        Finally
            If Not sqlConn2.State = ConnectionState.Closed Then
                sqlConn2.Close()
            End If

        End Try

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("INSERT INTO messagestousers (msgid, msgfrom, msgsource, msgto, msgfolder) " & _
                "VALUES (" & msgincr & ", " & pplfrom & ", " & coid & ", " & pplfrom & ", 'sent items')", sqlConn2)

            If Not sqlConn2.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                sqlConn2.Open()
            End If

            sqlCmd1.ExecuteNonQuery()

        Catch ex As SqlException
            result = "Error^" & ex.Message & ex.Number.ToString
            Return result

        Finally
            If Not sqlConn2.State = ConnectionState.Closed Then
                sqlConn2.Close()
            End If

        End Try

        Return "True"

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function msg_getunreadrpt(ByVal pplid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT people.fname + ' ' + people.lname AS messageto, messages.msgdate, messages.subject " & _
                    "FROM messagestousers INNER JOIN " & _
                        "people ON messagestousers.msgto = people.peopleid INNER JOIN " & _
                        "messages ON messagestousers.msgid = messages.msgid AND messagestousers.msgfrom = messages.msgfrom AND " & _
                        "messagestousers.msgsource = messages.msgsource " & _
                    "WHERE (messagestousers.isread = 0) " & _
                    "AND messagestousers.msgto <> messagestousers.msgfrom " & _
                    "AND messagestousers.msgto <> 1 " & _
                    "AND messagestousers.msgfrom = " & pplid

        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csCheckOccupant(ByVal houseid As Integer) As String

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(peopleid) FROM occupants WHERE firstowner = 1 AND houseid = " & houseid, SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While

            sqlDr.Close()
            SqlConn.Close()

            Return cnt.ToString

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '***    Programmer:  Carlos Salazar                                          ***
    '***    Created Date: 10/04/02                                               ***
    '***    Modified Date:                                                       ***
    '***    Select Statement prior to insert into occupants for peopleid        ***
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csSelectOccupantPeopleid(ByVal houseid As Integer, ByVal peopleid As Integer) As String

        Dim result As String = "0"

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT peopleid FROM occupants WHERE firstowner = 1 AND peopleid <> " & peopleid & " AND houseid = " & houseid, SqlConn)

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            Return result
            SqlConn.Close()

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '  Programmer:  Carlos Salazar / Patricia Kline
    '  Modified Date:                                                       
    '  Update Statement to set owner = 0 for new ownership and set all 
    '   untransferrable warranties to 0
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csUpdateOccupant(ByVal houseid As Integer) As String

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim SqlCmd As New SqlCommand("UPDATE occupants SET firstowner = 0 WHERE houseid = " & houseid, SqlConn)

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Open()
            End If

            SqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Open()
            End If

            Dim SqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("UPDATE warranties SET active = 0 " & _
                "WHERE houseid = " & houseid & " AND transferable = 0", SqlConn)

            SqlCmd1.ExecuteNonQuery()

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Open()
            End If

            SqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Closed Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    ' Programmer:  Carlos Salazar/Patricia Kline
    ' Created Date: 10/04/02                                          
    ' Check if homeowners have been added and if not Insert Statement 
    ' for adding new customers Occupants table           
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csInsertOccupants(ByVal peopleid As Integer, ByVal homeid As Integer) As String

        Dim today As Date = Now()

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim SqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM occupants " & _
                "WHERE peopleid = " & peopleid & " AND houseid = " & homeid, SqlConn)
            Dim SqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            SqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            SqlDr = SqlCmd1.ExecuteReader
            While SqlDr.Read
                cnt = SqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            SqlDr.Close()
            SqlConn.Close()

            If cnt = 0 Then
                Dim SqlCmd As New SqlCommand(LCase("INSERT INTO occupants (peopleid, houseid, date, firstowner) " & _
                                                    "VALUES (" & peopleid & ", " & homeid & ", '" & today & "', 1)"), SqlConn)

                If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                    SqlConn.Open()
                End If

                SqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

                If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Closed Then
                    SqlConn.Close()
                End If
            End If

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    ' Programmer:  Carlos Salazar                                       
    ' Created Date: 10/04/02                                          
    ' Select Statement for doing the search onto customer service   
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csHomeownerSearch(ByVal whereclause As String) As DataSet

        Dim ds As New DataSet()

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT DISTINCT people.peopleid, people.fname, people.lname, people.phone, homes.address, projects.city, projects.state, projects.zip, projects.projectid, homes.houseid, homes.coe, homes.walkthru, homes.walkthrus " & _
                    "FROM homes INNER JOIN projects ON homes.projectid = projects.projectid INNER JOIN occupants ON homes.houseid = occupants.houseid RIGHT OUTER JOIN people ON occupants.peopleid = people.peopleid " & _
                    "WHERE " & whereclause & " ORDER BY people.lname, people.fname, homes.address", SqlConn)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: get names of people that the message was to
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function get_states() As String()

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM states ", SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            Dim x As Integer = 0
            Dim result(cnt - 1) As String
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("SELECT state FROM states ", SqlConn)
            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result(x) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                x = x + 1
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            SqlConn.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Dim result(2) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = CStr(ex.Number)
            result(2) = ex.Message

            Return result
        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '***    Programmer:  Carlos Salazar                                          ***
    '***    Created Date: 10/04/02                                               ***
    '***    Modified Date:10/28/02 John Ashbaugh                                 ***
    '***    Select Statement for getting all customer info to display            ***
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csSelectUserInfo(ByVal peopleid As String) As String()

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim SqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT people.fname, people.m_initial, people.lname, people.address, people.city, people.state, people.zip, people.phone, people.fax, people.cell, people.worknumber, people.email, homeowners.sex, homeowners.age, homeowners.occupation, homeowners.marital_status, homeowners.dependants, homeowners.spouse_fname, homeowners.spouse_lname, users.screenname, users.password, usersaccess.winspection, usersaccess.wdocuments, usersaccess.wcusservice " & _
                "FROM usersaccess INNER JOIN users ON usersaccess.peopleid = users.peopleid RIGHT OUTER JOIN people ON users.peopleid = people.peopleid LEFT OUTER JOIN homeowners ON people.peopleid = homeowners.peopleid WHERE people.peopleid = " & peopleid, SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim a(23) As String
            Dim x As Integer

            SqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = SqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                For x = 0 To 23
                    a(x) = sqlDr.GetValue(x).ToString
                Next
            End While

            Return a

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Dim a(2) As String
            a(0) = "Error"
            a(1) = ex.Number.ToString
            a(2) = ex.Message
            Return a

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '***    Programmer:  Carlos Salazar                                          ***
    '***    Created Date: 10/04/02                                               ***
    '***    Update Statement for customers (1st part)                            ***
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csUpdateCustomer(ByVal fname As String, ByVal lname As String, ByVal initial As String, ByVal address As String, ByVal city As String, ByVal st As String, ByVal zip As String, ByVal ph As String, ByVal cell As String, ByVal fax As String, ByVal work As String, ByVal email As String, ByVal peopleid As Integer) As String

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

            fname = Replace(fname, "'", "''")
            lname = Replace(lname, "'", "''")
            address = Replace(address, "'", "''")
            city = Replace(city, "'", "''")

            sqlstmt = "UPDATE people SET fname = '" & fname & "', m_initial = '" & initial & "', " & _
                            "lname = '" & lname & "', address = '" & address & "', email = '" & email & "', " & _
                            "city = '" & city & "', state = '" & st & "', zip = '" & zip & "', phone = '" & ph & "', " & _
                            "fax = '" & fax & "', cell = '" & cell & "', worknumber = '" & work & "' " & _
                        "WHERE peopleid = " & peopleid

            Dim SqlCmd As New SqlCommand(LCase(sqlstmt), SqlConn)
            Dim SqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim a As String

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Open()
            End If

            SqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Closed Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    ' Programmer:  Patricia Kline                                           
    ' Created Date: 10/04/02                                              
    ' Event: Insert or Update homeowner infomation                               
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csHomeownerInfo(ByVal pplid As Integer, ByVal sex As String, ByVal age As String, ByVal maritalstatus As String, ByVal dependants As Integer, ByVal occupation As String, ByVal spousefname As String, ByVal spouselname As String) As String

        Try
            occupation = Replace(occupation, "'", "''")
            spousefname = Replace(spousefname, "'", "''")
            spouselname = Replace(spouselname, "'", "''")

            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM homeowners " & _
                                        "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            SqlConn.Close()

            Dim sqlstmt As String

            If cnt = 0 Then
                sqlstmt = LCase("INSERT INTO homeowners (peopleid, sex, age, marital_status, dependants, occupation,spouse_fname, spouse_lname) " & _
                    "VALUES (" & pplid & ", '" & sex & "', " & age & ", '" & maritalstatus & "', '" & dependants & "', '" & occupation & "', '" & spousefname & "', '" & spouselname & "')")
                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                SqlConn.Open()
                sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
                SqlConn.Close()
            Else
                sqlstmt = LCase("UPDATE homeowners " & _
                    "SET sex = '" & sex & "', age = " & age & ", marital_status = '" & maritalstatus & "', dependants = '" & dependants & "', occupation = '" & occupation & "', spouse_fname = '" & spousefname & "', spouse_lname = '" & spouselname & "'" & _
                    "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid)
                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                SqlConn.Open()
                sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '  Programmer:  Carlos Salazar / Patricia Kline
    '  Created Date: 10/04/02                      
    '  Select Statement for problems and homeowner calls
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csCallLog(ByVal houseid As Integer, ByVal peopleid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim ds As New DataSet()

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT CONVERT(VARCHAR(5), p.problemsid) AS problemsid, p.reporteddate, c.description AS category, p.problemdesc, p.closed, p.closeddate, users.screenname " & _
                "FROM problems p INNER JOIN problemcodes c ON p.problemscode = c.problemscode INNER JOIN users ON p.cus_peopleid = users.peopleid " & _
                "WHERE p.houseid = " & houseid & " " & _
                "UNION ALL " & _
                "SELECT 'NOTE', notedate, '', notes, 0, null, users.screenname FROM peoplenotes INNER JOIN users ON peoplenotes.peopleid = users.peopleid WHERE peoplenotes.peopleid = " & peopleid & " ORDER BY reporteddate DESC;", SqlConn)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        End Try

    End Function

                    "WHERE problemsid = " & problemid & " " & _
                    "ORDER BY notedate DESC", SqlConn)

            Throw ex

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csOpenProblems(ByVal companyid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim ds As New DataSet()

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT p.problemsid, p.reporteddate, homes.address, c.description AS category, p.problemdesc, p.materialid, p.houseid " & _
                "FROM homes INNER JOIN projects ON homes.projectid = projects.projectid INNER JOIN problems p INNER JOIN problemcodes c ON p.problemscode = c.problemscode ON homes.houseid = p.houseid " & _
                "WHERE projects.companyid = " & companyid & " AND p.closed = 0", SqlConn)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    ' Programmer:  Patricia Kline
    ' Created Date: 10/04/02                    
    ' Select warranty types for material and home.
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csMaterialWarrantyTypes(ByVal houseid As Integer, ByVal materialid As Integer) As String()

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM warranties " & _
                                            "WHERE houseid = " & houseid & " " & _
                                            "AND projectmaterialid = " & materialid & " " & _
                                            "AND active > 0", SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While

            sqlDr.Close()

            Dim x As Integer = 0
            Dim result(cnt - 1) As String
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("SELECT type, active FROM warranties " & _
                                            "WHERE houseid = " & houseid & " " & _
                                            "AND projectmaterialid = " & materialid & " " & _
                                            "AND active > 0", SqlConn)

            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result(x) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString & "^" & sqlDr.GetValue(1).ToString
                x = x + 1
            End While

            sqlDr.Close()
            SqlConn.Close()

            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Dim result(2) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = CStr(ex.Number)
            result(2) = ex.Message

            Return result

        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csProblemOrders(ByVal problemid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim ds As New DataSet()

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT CONVERT(varchar(10), problemorders.problemid) + '-' + CONVERT(varchar(5), problemorders.ordernbr) AS nbr, problemorders.orderdate, companies.name, problemorders.taskdesc, problemorders.orderamt, problemorders.closedate, problemorders.closeby, problemorders.companyid " & _
                "FROM problemorders INNER JOIN companies ON problemorders.companyid = companies.companyid " & _
                "WHERE problemorders.problemid = " & problemid, SqlConn)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event:  function to insert problem orders
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csInsertProblemOrders(ByVal problemid As Integer, ByVal coid As Integer, ByVal task As String, ByVal amt As String) As String

        task = Replace(task, "'", "''")
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

        Try
            sqlstmt = "SELECT count(*) FROM problemorders " & _
                        "WHERE problemid = " & problemid

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar) + 1

            sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO problemorders (problemid, ordernbr, orderdate, companyid, taskdesc, orderamt) " & _
                "VALUES (" & problemid & ", " & cnt & ", '" & Now & "', " & coid & ", '" & task & "', " & amt & ")"

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline

                        "WHERE problemid = " & problemid & " AND ordernbr = " & nbr
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

        Catch ex As SqlException

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function
    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Patricia Kline
    '   Created Date: 10/04/02

    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csUsersAccess(ByVal pplid As Integer, ByVal customer As Integer, ByVal inspection As Integer, ByVal document As Integer) As String

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM usersaccess " & _
                                        "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            SqlConn.Close()

            Dim sqlstmt As String

            If cnt = 0 Then
                sqlstmt = LCase("INSERT INTO usersaccess (peopleid, wcusservice, winspection, wdocuments) " & _
                    "VALUES (" & pplid & ", " & customer & ", " & inspection & ", " & document & ")")
                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                SqlConn.Open()
                sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
                SqlConn.Close()
            Else
                sqlstmt = LCase("UPDATE usersaccess " & _
                    "SET wcusservice = " & customer & ", winspection = " & inspection & ", wdocuments = " & document & " " & _
                    "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid)
                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                SqlConn.Open()
                sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                             
    '   Event:  function to check if username exists for homeownwer
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function cs_check_username(ByVal username As String) As String

        Dim cnt As Integer

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM users " & _
                "WHERE screenname = '" & username & "'", SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While

            sqlDr.Close()
            SqlConn.Close()

            If cnt > 0 Then
                Return "Duplicate"
            Else
                Return "True"
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    ' Carlos Salazar / Patricia Kline
    ' Created Date: 10/04/02                     
    ' Select Statement For all documents pertaining to specific houseid 
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csHouseDocuments(ByVal houseid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim ds As New DataSet()

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT documents.documentid, documents.documentcategory, documenttitles.title, documentdates.documentdate " & _
                "FROM documents INNER JOIN homes ON documents.projectid = homes.projectid LEFT OUTER JOIN documenttitles ON documents.documentid = documenttitles.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN documentdates ON documents.documentid = documentdates.documentid " & _
                "WHERE (documents.documentid NOT IN (SELECT documentid FROM documentlocations) OR documents.documentid IN (SELECT documentid FROM documentlocations WHERE houseid = " & houseid & ")) AND (homes.houseid = " & houseid & ")", SqlConn)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '  Programmer:  Carlos Salazar  / Patricia Kline
    '  Created Date: 10/04/02                       
    '  Get document filename and path for documentid 
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csSelectDocumentFilename(ByVal docid As Integer) As String

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim SqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT buildersco.directory AS copath, projects.directory AS projpath, documents.docfilename " & _
                    "FROM documents INNER JOIN buildersco INNER JOIN companies ON buildersco.companyid = companies.companyid INNER JOIN projects ON buildersco.companyid = projects.companyid ON documents.projectid = projects.projectid " & _
                    "WHERE documentid = " & docid, SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim a As String

            SqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = SqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                a = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString & "/" & sqlDr.GetValue(1).ToString & "/" & sqlDr.GetValue(2).ToString
            End While

            Return a

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return "ERROR" & ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csSB800DeliveryMethod(ByVal statement As String, ByVal peopleid As Integer) As String

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim SqlCmd As New SqlCommand(statement & " " & peopleid, SqlConn)
            Dim SqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim a As String = Nothing

            SqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            SqlDr = SqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While SqlDr.Read
                a = SqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString & ":"
            End While

            If a = Nothing Then
                Return "Error"
            Else
                Return a
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return "Error"

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Carlos Salazar  / Patricia Kline
    '   Created Date: 11/11/02                       
    '   Check if sb800 reminder exists and if not insert new sb800 reminder
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csInsertSB800MGMT(ByVal stepnbr As Integer, ByVal pplid As Integer, ByVal method As Integer, ByVal dayinterval As Integer) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        Dim methodtype As String
        Dim info As String

        Try
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            sqlstmt = "SELECT count(*) FROM sb800mgmt " & _
                      "WHERE stepnbr  = " & stepnbr & " " & _
                      "AND peopleid = " & pplid & " " & _
                      "AND deliverymethod = " & method

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar)

            If cnt = 0 Then
                If method = 1 Then
                    methodtype = "email address"
                    sqlstmt = "SELECT email FROM people " & _
                        "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid
                Else
                    methodtype = "text message number"
                    sqlstmt = "SELECT txtmsgnumber FROM people " & _
                        "WHERE peopleid = " & pplid
                End If

                Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text

                If LCase(sqlCmd1.ExecuteScalar.GetType.ToString) = "system.dbnull" Then
                    info = ""
                Else
                    info = CStr(sqlCmd1.ExecuteScalar)
                End If

                If info <> "" Then
                    sqlstmt = LCase("INSERT INTO sb800mgmt (stepnbr, peopleid, deliverymethod, dayinterval) " & _
                            "VALUES (" & stepnbr & ", " & pplid & ", '" & method & "', " & dayinterval & ")")
                    Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                    cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                    cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

                    Return "True"
                Else
                    Return "No " & methodtype
                End If
            Else
                Return "Dup"
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************

                SqlConn.Open()

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

                SqlConn.Open()
            End If

            SqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Closed Then
                SqlConn.Close()

        End Try

    End Function

    '   Insert Statement for adding in SB800 Tracking           
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function csInsertSB800Tracking(ByVal problemid As Integer, ByVal stepnbr As Integer) As String

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM sb800tracking " & _
                                        "WHERE problemid  = " & problemid & " " & _
                                        "AND stepnbr = " & stepnbr, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While

            SqlConn.Close()

            If cnt = 0 Then
                sqlstmt = LCase("INSERT INTO sb800tracking (problemid, stepnbr, startdate) VALUES (" & problemid & ", " & stepnbr & ", '" & Now & "')")
                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                SqlConn.Open()
                sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        End Try

    End Function

                    "FROM materials INNER JOIN materialstoprojects ON materials.materialsid = materialstoprojects.materialsid AND materials.materialsid = materialstoprojects.materialsid INNER JOIN companies ON materialstoprojects.companyid = companies.companyid AND materialstoprojects.companyid = companies.companyid INNER JOIN housematerials ON materialstoprojects.projectmaterialid = housematerials.projectmaterialid " & _
                    "WHERE housematerials.houseid = " & houseid & " " & _
                    "ORDER BY materials.materialscategory, materials.name"

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        End Try

    End Function

    '   Created Date: 11/11/02                                  
    '   Select Statement for referenced materials for a material
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function matReferencedMaterials(ByVal matid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim ds As New DataSet()

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT companies.companyid, companies.name AS coname, materials.materialsid, materials.materialscategory, materials.name, materials.description, materials.options, materials.leadtime, materials.price " & _
                    "FROM materials INNER JOIN companies ON materials.companyid = companies.companyid " & _
                    "WHERE  (dbo.materials.materialsid IN (SELECT materialsid FROM materialsreference WHERE materialsidref = " & matid & ")) " & _
                    "OR  (dbo.materials.materialsid IN (SELECT materialsidref FROM materialsreference WHERE materialsid = " & matid & ")) ", SqlConn)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '***    Programmer:  Carlos Salazar                                          ***
    '***    Created Date: 11/11/02                                               ***
    '***    Modified Date:                                                       ***
    '***    Insert Statement for adding in Materials Categories                  ***
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function matInsertMaterialsCategory(ByVal category As String) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim cnt As Integer = 0
        Dim sqlstmt As String

        category = Replace(category, "'", "''")

        Try
            sqlstmt = "SELECT count(*) FROM materialcategories " & _
                            "WHERE materialscategory = '" & category & "'"

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Open()
            End If

            cnt = CInt(sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar)

            If cnt = 0 Then
                Dim SqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand(LCase("INSERT INTO materialcategories (materialscategory) VALUES ('" & category & "')"), SqlConn)

                If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                    SqlConn.Open()
                End If

                SqlCmd1.ExecuteNonQuery()
            End If

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    ' Programmer:  Carlos Salazar  / patricia kline                                     
    ' Created Date: 11/11/02                                           
    ' Insert Statement for adding in Materials                           
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function matInsertMaterials(ByVal coid As Integer, ByVal category As String, ByVal name As String, ByVal descr As String, ByVal options As String, ByVal leadtime As String, ByVal price As String) As String

        category = Replace(category, "'", "''")
        name = Replace(name, "'", "''")
        descr = Replace(descr, "'", "''")
        options = Replace(options, "'", "''")
        leadtime = Replace(leadtime, "'", "''")

        Try
            Dim a As String
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

            sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO materials (companyid, materialscategory, name, description, options, leadtime, price) " & _
                            "VALUES (" & coid & ", '" & category & "', '" & name & "', '" & descr & "', '" & options & "', '" & leadtime & "', " & price & ")"
            Dim SqlCmd As New SqlCommand(LCase(sqlstmt), SqlConn)

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Open()
            End If

            SqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Closed Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlstmt = "SELECT max(materialsid) FROM materials " & _
                "WHERE companyid = " & coid & " " & _
                "AND materialscategory = '" & category & "' " & _
                "AND name = '" & name & "' " & _
                "AND description = '" & descr & "' " & _
                "AND options = '" & options & "' " & _
                "AND leadtime = '" & leadtime & "' " & _
                "AND price =  " & price

            SqlCmd.CommandText = sqlstmt
            Dim SqlDr As SqlDataReader
            SqlDr = SqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While SqlDr.Read
                a = SqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
            End While

            SqlConn.Close()
            Return a

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    ' Programmer:  patricia kline                                    
    ' Created Date: 11/11/02                                           
    ' Insert Statement for adding in Ref. Materials                     
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function matInsertMaterialsReference(ByVal matid As Integer, ByVal refid As Integer) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try
            Dim cnt As Integer = 0
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM materialsreference " & _
                                    "WHERE (materialsid = " & matid & " " & _
                                    "AND materialsidref = " & refid & ") " & _
                                    "OR (materialsid = " & refid & " " & _
                                    "AND materialsidref = " & matid & ")", SqlConn)

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar)
            SqlConn.Close()

            If cnt = 0 Then
                Dim sqlstmt As String = "INSERT INTO materialsreference (materialsid, materialsidref) " & _
                                        "VALUES (" & matid & ", " & refid & ")"
                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                SqlConn.Open()
                cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
                Return "True"
            Else
                Return "Dup"
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Closed Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

        Try

                        "VALUES (" & companyid & ", " & materialsid & ", '" & purchasedate & "', " & projid & ", " & quantity & ", " & warranty & ")"), SqlConn)

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline                                        
    '   Created Date: 01/07/03                                               
    '   Modified Date:                                                       
    '   checks if data has been entered and if not, inserts data into the 
    '   housematerials table in the vcnet database. 
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function InsertHouseMaterials(ByVal matid As Integer, ByVal homeid As Integer, ByVal quan As Integer) As String

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO housematerials (projectmaterialid, houseid, quantity) " & _
                    "VALUES(" & matid & "," & homeid & "," & quan & ")"

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM housematerials " & _
                                        "WHERE projectmaterialid = " & matid & " " & _
                                        "AND houseid = " & homeid, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            SqlConn.Close()

            If cnt = 0 Then
                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                SqlConn.Open()
                sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
                SqlConn.Close()
                Return "True"
            Else
                Return "Dup"
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    ' Programmer:  Carlos Salazar                                          
    ' Created Date: 11/11/02                                                                                         
    ' Insert Statement for adding in Property address                     
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function matInsertPropertyAddress(ByVal projectid As Integer, ByVal address As String) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        address = Replace(address, "'", "''")

        Try
            Dim cnt As Integer
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM homes " & _
                    "WHERE projectid = " & projectid & " " & _
                    "AND address = '" & address & "'", SqlConn)

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = CInt(sqlDr.GetValue(0))
            End While

            sqlDr.Close()
            SqlConn.Close()

            If cnt > 0 Then
                Return "Dup"
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        End Try

        Try
            Dim SqlCmd As New SqlCommand(LCase("INSERT INTO homes (projectid, address) VALUES (" & projectid & ", '" & address & "')"), SqlConn)

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Open()
            End If

            SqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Closed Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '***    Programmer:  Joe Dela Cruz                                           ***
    '***    Created Date: 01/02/03                                               ***
    '***    Modified Date:                                                       ***
    '***    This web service gets Material records from the materials table and  ***
    '***    companies table                                                      ***
    '***    There are 5 parameters passed into webmethod:                        ***
    '***    material name, description, category, company name, and option.      ***
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetMaterialsData(ByVal name As String, ByVal description As String, ByVal category As String, ByVal companyname As String, ByVal options As String) As DataSet

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        Dim strName As String
        Dim strDescription As String
        Dim strCategory As String
        Dim strCompanyName As String
        Dim strOptions As String
        Dim blnNoWhereClause As Boolean

        blnNoWhereClause = True

        ' Trim spaces from passed in paramaters and replace single quotes with double single quotes
        strName = Replace(name.Trim, "'", "''")
        strDescription = Replace(description.Trim, "'", "''")
        strCategory = Replace(category.Trim, "'", "''")
        strCompanyName = Replace(companyname.Trim, "'", "''")
        strOptions = Replace(options.Trim, "'", "''")
        blnNoWhereClause = True

        ' Begin creating SQL string
        sqlstmt = "SELECT materials.materialsid, materials.name, materials.description, materials.options, materials.materialscategory, companies.name AS Vendor, materials.companyid, materials.price, materials.leadtime " & _
                   "FROM materials INNER JOIN companies ON materials.companyid = companies.companyid "

        ' Add materials.name field in WHERE clause if name parameter is not blank
        If name.Trim <> "" Then
            sqlstmt = sqlstmt & " WHERE materials.name LIKE '%" & strName & "%'"
            blnNoWhereClause = False
        End If

        ' Add materials.description field in WHERE clause if description parameter is not blank
        If description.Trim <> "" Then
            If blnNoWhereClause = True Then
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & " WHERE materials.description LIKE '%" & strDescription & "%'"
                blnNoWhereClause = False
            Else
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & " AND materials.description LIKE '%" & strDescription & "%'"
            End If
        End If

        ' Add materials.materialscategory field in WHERE clause if category parameter is not blank
        If category.Trim <> "" Then
            If blnNoWhereClause = True Then
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & " WHERE materials.materialscategory LIKE '%" & strCategory & "%'"
                blnNoWhereClause = False
            Else
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & " AND materials.materialscategory LIKE '%" & strCategory & "%'"
            End If
        End If

        ' Add companies.name field in WHERE clause if company name parameter is not blank
        If companyname.Trim <> "" Then
            If blnNoWhereClause = True Then
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & " WHERE materials.companyid IN (SELECT companyid FROM companies WHERE name LIKE '%" & strCompanyName & "%')"
                blnNoWhereClause = False
            Else
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & " AND materials.companyid IN (SELECT companyid FROM companies WHERE name LIKE '%" & strCompanyName & "%')"
            End If
        End If

        ' Add materials.options field in WHERE clause if options parameter is not blank
        If options.Trim <> "" Then
            If blnNoWhereClause = True Then
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & " WHERE materials.options LIKE '%" & strOptions & "%'"
                blnNoWhereClause = False
            Else
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & " AND materials.options LIKE '%" & strOptions & "%'"
            End If
        End If

        ' Add ordering clause to sqlstmt string
        sqlstmt = sqlstmt & " ORDER BY materialsid"

        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()

        End Try

    End Function

#End Region
