    '***    material name, description, category, company name, and option.      ***
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetSb800Problems(ByVal companyid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT people.fname + ' ' + people.lname AS customer_name, sb800tracking.startdate AS start_date, sb800tracking.completedate AS end_date, " & _
                      "sb800.taskdesc AS step, problemcodes.description AS prob_desc, " & _
                      "problems.problemsid,sb800tracking.stepnbr " & _
                    "FROM problemcodes INNER JOIN problems ON problemcodes.problemscode = problems.problemscode INNER JOIN sb800tracking ON problems.problemsid = sb800tracking.problemid INNER JOIN people ON problems.cus_peopleid = people.peopleid INNER JOIN sb800 ON sb800tracking.stepnbr = sb800.stepnbr INNER JOIN homes ON problems.houseid = homes.houseid INNER JOIN projects ON homes.projectid = projects.projectid  " & _
                    "WHERE (problems.closed = 0) AND projects.companyid = " & companyid & " " & _
                    "ORDER BY problems.problemsid, sb800tracking.stepnbr"

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Joe Dela Cruz                                                 *
    '   Created Date: 02/11/03                                                     *
    '   Modified Date:                                                             *
    '   Get open problems per project on a summary level per companyid             *
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetOpenProblemsSummaryData(ByVal companyid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim strSQL As String

        strSQL = "SELECT distinct   dbo.problems.problemsid AS RequestNo, dbo.problems.problemdesc AS description, people.fname + ' ' + people.lname AS Buyer,dbo.homes.walkthru AS walkthru, dbo.seqsheets.phase, dbo.seqsheets.tract, dbo.seqsheets.lot, " & _
                        "dbo.problems.reporteddate, DATEDIFF(day, problems.reporteddate, GETDATE()) AS days_open ,dbo.projects.name AS projectname, " & _
                        "(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM problemorders WHERE problems.problemsid = dbo.problemorders.problemid) AS TotalWO, " & _
                        "(SELECT COUNT(*) FROM problemorders WHERE problems.problemsid = dbo.problemorders.problemid AND problemorders.closedate IS NULL) AS OpenWO " & _
                    "FROM         dbo.problems INNER JOIN " & _
                        "dbo.homes ON dbo.problems.houseid = dbo.homes.houseid INNER JOIN " & _
                        "dbo.seqsheets ON dbo.homes.houseid = dbo.seqsheets.houseid INNER JOIN " & _
                        "dbo.projects ON dbo.homes.projectid = dbo.projects.projectid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                        "dbo.problemorders ON dbo.problems.problemsid = dbo.problemorders.problemid " & _
                        "INNER JOIN people ON problems.hom_peopleid = people.peopleid " & _
                    "WHERE  projects.companyid = " & companyid & " AND (dbo.problems.closed = 0)"

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()

        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetOpenProblemsData(ByVal coid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT problems.reporteddate, problemcodes.description, problems.problemsource AS Type,people.fname + ' ' + people.lname AS customer_name, people.phone AS customer_phone, DATEDIFF(day, problems.reporteddate, GETDATE()) AS days_open, homes.address AS address, projects.name AS projectname, problems.problemsid AS RefNo, " & _
                    "seqsheets.phase,seqsheets.tract,seqsheets.lot,dbo.customerservice.initials AS Rep " & _
                    "FROM problemcodes INNER JOIN problems ON problemcodes.problemscode = problems.problemscode INNER JOIN homes ON problems.houseid = homes.houseid INNER JOIN projects ON homes.projectid = projects.projectid INNER JOIN people ON problems.hom_peopleid = people.peopleid  " & _
                    "INNER JOIN seqsheets ON homes.houseid = seqsheets.houseid " & _
                    "LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.customerservice ON dbo.problems.cus_peopleid = dbo.customerservice.peopleid " & _
                    "WHERE projects.companyid = " & coid & " AND problems.closed = 0 " & _
                    "ORDER BY problems.reporteddate asc, problems.problemsid asc"

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim ds As New DataSet()

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Joe Dela Cruz                                                 *
    '   Created Date: 02/10/03                                                     *
    '   Modified Date:                                                             *
    '   Get subcontractors problem orders for a date range                         *
    '   and a status type (open, closed, or all items)                             * 
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetSubcontractorsProblemOrders(ByVal startdate As DateTime, ByVal enddate As DateTime, ByVal orderstatus As String) As DataSet
        Dim strSQL As String

        strSQL = "SELECT dbo.seqsheets.phase, dbo.seqsheets.tract, dbo.seqsheets.lot, dbo.seqsheets.bldg, convert(varchar(10), dbo.problemorders.problemid) + '-' + convert(varchar(10), dbo.problemorders.ordernbr) AS WorkOrder, dbo.problemorders.orderdate AS Received, dbo.problemorders.closedate AS Completed,dbo.problemorders.taskdesc AS RepairDescription, dbo.customerservice.initials AS Rep, dbo.seqsheets.projectid, dbo.projects.name AS projectname, " & _
                    "dbo.companies.name AS subcontractor, dbo.companies.phone AS subcontractorphone, dbo.companies.fax AS subcontractorfax, " & _
                    "(SELECT CASE WHEN problemorders.closedate IS NULL THEN	" & _
                    "DATEDIFF(day, problemorders.orderdate, GETDATE()) " & _
                    "ELSE DATEDIFF(day, problemorders.orderdate, problemorders.closedate) END ) AS days_open " & _
                    "FROM dbo.problemorders  " & _
                        "INNER JOIN dbo.problems ON dbo.problemorders.problemid = dbo.problems.problemsid " & _
                      "INNER JOIN dbo.seqsheets ON dbo.problems.houseid = dbo.seqsheets.houseid  " & _
                      "INNER JOIN  dbo.people ON dbo.people.peopleid = dbo.problems.hom_peopleid  " & _
                      "INNER JOIN dbo.projects ON dbo.seqsheets.projectid = dbo.projects.projectid  " & _
                      "INNER JOIN dbo.customerservice ON dbo.problems.cus_peopleid = dbo.customerservice.peopleid " & _
                      "INNER JOIN companies ON companies.companyid=problemorders.companyid " & _
                    "WHERE problemorders.orderdate BETWEEN '" & startdate & "' AND '" & enddate & "'"

        ' Add where condition for open, closed, or all orders
        If orderstatus.ToLower = "closed" Then
            strSQL = strSQL & " AND problemorders.closedate IS NOT NULL "
        ElseIf orderstatus.ToLower = "open" Then
            strSQL = strSQL & " AND problemorders.closedate IS NULL "
        End If

        strSQL = strSQL & "ORDER BY dbo.problemorders.ordernbr "

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Try

            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Joe Dela Cruz                                                 *
    '   Created Date: 02/10/03                                                     *
    '   Modified Date:                                                             *
    '   Get problem orders item information based on date range and if the         *
    '   problems are open, closed, or both.                                        *
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetProblemOrdersRecapData(ByVal startdate As DateTime, ByVal enddate As DateTime, ByVal orderstatus As String) As DataSet

        Dim strSQL As String

        strSQL = "SELECT projects.name AS projectname, seqsheets.phase,seqsheets.tract,seqsheets.lot,people.fname + ' ' + people.lname AS Buyer,people.address,people.phone AS Phone,problems.problemsid AS RequestNo, " & _
                    "dbo.customerservice.initials AS Rep, problems.problemsource AS Type, problemorders.orderdate AS Received, problemorders.closedate AS closed, problemorders.ordernbr AS Item, problemorders.taskdesc AS Description, " & _
                    "(SELECT CASE " & _
                    "WHEN problemorders.closedate IS NULL THEN 'Open' " & _
                    "ELSE 'Closed' END AS Category) AS Status, " & _
                    "(SELECT CASE WHEN problemorders.closedate IS NULL THEN	" & _
                    "   DATEDIFF(day, problemorders.orderdate, GETDATE()) " & _
                    "   ELSE DATEDIFF(day, problemorders.orderdate, problemorders.closedate) END ) AS days_open " & _
                    "FROM problemcodes INNER JOIN problems ON problemcodes.problemscode = problems.problemscode INNER JOIN homes ON problems.houseid = homes.houseid INNER JOIN projects ON homes.projectid = projects.projectid INNER JOIN people ON problems.hom_peopleid = people.peopleid " & _
                    "INNER JOIN seqsheets ON homes.houseid = seqsheets.houseid " & _
                    "LEFT OUTER JOIN dbo.customerservice ON dbo.problems.cus_peopleid = dbo.customerservice.peopleid " & _
                    "INNER JOIN problemorders ON dbo.problems.problemsid = dbo.problemorders.problemid "

        ' Add where condition for open, closed, or all orders
        If orderstatus.ToLower = "closed" Then
            strSQL = strSQL & " WHERE problems.closed = 1 AND "
        ElseIf orderstatus.ToLower = "open" Then
            strSQL = strSQL & " WHERE problems.closed = 0 AND "
        Else
            strSQL = strSQL & " WHERE "
        End If

        ' Add condition in WHERE clause for date range
        strSQL = strSQL & " problems.reporteddate BETWEEN '" & startdate & "' AND '" & enddate & "'" & _
                    " ORDER BY problems.reporteddate asc, problems.problemsid asc "

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try

            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

#Region " Warranties "

    '*******************************************************************************
    '***    Programmer:  Joe Dela Cruz / patricia kline
    '***    Created Date: 01/07/03                                               ***
    '***    Modified Date:                                                       ***
    '***    The GetMaterialsCategoryData function returns data from the          ***
    '***    materials table in the vcnet database.                               ***
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetHouseMaterialCategories(ByVal houseid As Integer) As String()

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT count(DISTINCT materialscategory) FROM materials " & _
                "WHERE materialsid IN (SELECT materialsid FROM materialstoprojects " & _
                "WHERE projectmaterialid IN (SELECT projectmaterialid FROM housematerials " & _
                "WHERE houseid = " & houseid & "))"

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            sqlstmt = "SELECT DISTINCT materialscategory FROM materials " & _
                "WHERE materialsid IN (SELECT materialsid FROM materialstoprojects " & _
                "WHERE projectmaterialid IN (SELECT projectmaterialid FROM housematerials " & _
                "WHERE houseid = " & houseid & "))"

            Dim x As Integer = 0
            Dim result(cnt - 1) As String
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result(x) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                x = x + 1
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            SqlConn.Close()

            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Dim result(2) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = CStr(ex.Number)
            result(2) = ex.Message

            Return result

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '    Programmer:  patricia kline
    '    Created Date: 01/07/03     
    '    Returns material names based off selected material category and houseid
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetHouseMaterialNames(ByVal Category As String, ByVal houseid As Integer) As String()

        Category = Replace(Category, "'", "''")

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT count(DISTINCT name) FROM materials " & _
                "WHERE materialsid IN (SELECT materialsid FROM materialstoprojects " & _
                "WHERE projectmaterialid IN (SELECT projectmaterialid FROM housematerials " & _
                "WHERE houseid = " & houseid & ")) " & _
                "AND materialscategory = '" & Category.Trim & "'"

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar)

            sqlstmt = "SELECT DISTINCT name AS name FROM materials " & _
                "WHERE materialsid IN (SELECT materialsid FROM materialstoprojects " & _
                "WHERE projectmaterialid IN (SELECT projectmaterialid FROM housematerials " & _
                "WHERE houseid = " & houseid & ")) " & _
                "AND materialscategory = '" & Category.Trim & "'"

            Dim x As Integer = 0
            Dim result(cnt - 1) As String
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result(x) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                x = x + 1
            End While

            sqlDr.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Dim result(2) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = CStr(ex.Number)
            result(2) = ex.Message

            Return result

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '  Programmer:  Joe Dela Cruz / patricia kline
    '  Created Date: 01/07/03                     
    '  Modified Date:                                                       
    '  The GetMaterialsDescription function returns the materials descriptions
    '  for the house and material name selected.
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetHouseMaterialDescriptions(ByVal Name As String, ByVal houseid As Integer) As DataSet

        Name = Replace(Name, "'", "''")

        Dim strSQL As String
        strSQL = "SELECT materialstoprojects.projectmaterialid, materials.description " & _
                    "FROM materials INNER JOIN materialstoprojects ON materials.materialsid = materialstoprojects.materialsid " & _
                           "WHERE (materialstoprojects.projectmaterialid IN (SELECT projectmaterialid FROM housematerials WHERE houseid = " & houseid & ") " & _
                           "AND name = '" & Name.Trim & "')"

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException

        End Try

    End Function



    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  patricia kline
    '   Created Date: 01/07/03     
    '   Modified Date:             
    '   Get warranty documents for a specific project
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function WarrantyDocuments(ByVal projid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim strSQL As String
        strSQL = "SELECT documents.documentid, documents.docfilename, documenttitles.title " & _
                    "FROM documents INNER JOIN " & _
                      "documenttitles ON documents.documentid = documenttitles.documentid " & _
                    "WHERE (documents.documentcategory = 'warranties' AND documents.projectid = " & projid & ")"

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Joe Dela Cruz   / patricia kline                                        
    '   Created Date: 01/07/03                                               
    '   Modified Date:                                                       
    '   The InsertWarranties function inserts data into the warranties   
    '   table in the vcnet database. 
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function InsertWarranties(ByVal homeid As Integer, ByVal docid As Integer, ByVal matid As Integer, ByVal EffDate As String, ByVal ExpDate As String, ByVal Transferrable As Integer, ByVal Type As String) As String

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        If docid > -1 Then
            sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO warranties (houseid, documentid, projectmaterialid, effectivedate,expirationdate,transferable,type) " & _
                        "VALUES(" & homeid & "," & docid & "," & matid & ",'" & EffDate & "','" & ExpDate & "'," & Transferrable & ",'" & Type & "')"
        Else
            sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO warranties (houseid, projectmaterialid, effectivedate, expirationdate, transferable, type) " & _
                        "VALUES(" & homeid & "," & matid & ",'" & EffDate & "','" & ExpDate & "'," & Transferrable & ",'" & Type & "')"
        End If

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM warranties " & _
                                        "WHERE projectmaterialid = " & matid & " " & _
                                        "AND houseid = " & homeid & " " & _
                                        "AND type = '" & Type & "'", SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            SqlConn.Close()

            If cnt = 0 Then
                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                SqlConn.Open()
                sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
                SqlConn.Close()
                Return "True"
            Else
                Return "Dup"
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  patricia kline
    '   Created Date: 01/07/03     
    '   Modified Date:             
    '   function returns all warranites for a home
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetHouseWarranties(ByVal houseid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim strSQL As String
        strSQL = "SELECT materials.name, materials.description, warranties.effectivedate, warranties.expirationdate, warranties.type, warranties.transferable, documents.docfilename " & _
                    "FROM warranties INNER JOIN materialstoprojects ON warranties.projectmaterialid = materialstoprojects.projectmaterialid INNER JOIN materials ON materialstoprojects.materialsid = materials.materialsid LEFT OUTER JOIN documents ON warranties.documentid = documents.documentid " & _
                    "WHERE warranties.houseid = " & houseid & " " & _
                    "ORDER BY materials.name"

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: Marc Olano
    '   Created Date:5/2/03                 
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Checks hits for website from mass email by Kate Swytak.
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function emailHitsCheck() As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim cnt As Integer

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT count(*) " & _
                            "FROM dbo.emailhits "

            Dim cmd2 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd2.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(cmd2.ExecuteScalar)

            cnt = cnt + 1

            sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO emailhits (counter) values (" & cnt & ")"

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()


            Return "true"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function track_addPages(ByVal trackingid As Integer, ByVal page As String, ByVal timeon As String) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO trackingpages (trackingid, page, timeon) " & _
                        "VALUES (@trackingid, @page, @timeon)"

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@trackingid", trackingid)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@page", page)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@timeon", timeon)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: Marc Olano            
    '   Created Date: 07/25/03                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event:  function to insert tracked page info.
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function track_addInfo(ByVal hostaddress As String, ByVal agent As String, ByVal browser As String, ByVal crawler As String, ByVal urlrefer As String, ByVal visits As String, ByVal origrefer As String, ByVal origurl As String, ByVal logondate As String, ByVal visitedpages As String, ByVal totaltime As String) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO tracking (hostaddress, agent, browser, crawler, urlrefer, origrefer, origurl, totaltime, logondate, visitedpages, visits) " & _
                        "VALUES "

            If hostaddress = "" Then
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "(NULL, "
            Else
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "(@hostaddress, "
            End If

            If agent = "" Then
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "NULL, "
            Else
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "@agent, "
            End If

            If browser = "" Then
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "NULL, "
            Else
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "@browser, "
            End If

            If crawler = "" Then
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "NULL, "
            Else
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "@crawler, "
            End If

            If urlrefer = "" Then
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "NULL, "
            Else
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "@urlrefer, "
            End If

            If origrefer = "" Then
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "NULL, "
            Else
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "@origrefer, "
            End If

            If origurl = "" Then
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "NULL, "
            Else
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "@origurl, "
            End If

            If totaltime = "" Then
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "NULL, "
            Else
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "@totaltime, "
            End If

            If logondate = Nothing Then
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "NULL, "
            Else
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "@logondate, "
            End If

            If visitedpages = "" Then
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "NULL, "
            Else
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "@visitedpages, "
            End If

            If visits = "" Then
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "NULL)"
            Else
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "@visits)"
            End If

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            If hostaddress <> "" Then
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@hostaddress", hostaddress)
            End If
            If agent <> "" Then
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@agent", agent)
            End If
            If browser <> "" Then
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@browser", browser)
            End If
            If crawler <> "" Then
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@crawler", crawler)
            End If
            If urlrefer <> "" Then
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@urlrefer", urlrefer)
            End If
            If origrefer <> "" Then
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@origrefer", origrefer)
            End If
            If origurl <> "" Then
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@origurl", origurl)
            End If
            If totaltime <> "" Then
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@totaltime", totaltime)
            End If
            If logondate <> Nothing Then
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@logondate", logondate)
            End If
            If visitedpages <> "" Then
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@visitedpages", visitedpages)
            End If
            If visits <> "" Then
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@visits", visits)
            End If

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: Marc Olano            
    '   Created Date: 07/25/03                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event:  function get trackingid.
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function track_getID(ByVal hostaddress As String, ByVal logondate As String) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT trackingid " & _
                        "FROM tracking " & _
                            "WHERE (hostaddress = @hostaddress) AND (logondate = @logondate)"

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Dim id As String
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@hostaddress", hostaddress)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@logondate", logondate)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            id = CStr(cmd.ExecuteScalar)
            Return id

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************

    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function track_timeandpages(ByVal trackingid As Integer, ByVal visitedpages As Integer, ByVal totaltime As String) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "UPDATE tracking " & _
                        "SET visitedpages = @visitedpages, totaltime = @totaltime " & _
                        "WHERE (trackingid = @trackingid)"

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@trackingid", trackingid)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@visitedpages", visitedpages)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@totaltime", totaltime)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function track_allinfo() As DataSet

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim ds As New DataSet()

        sqlstmt = "SELECT trackingid, hostaddress, browser, crawler, origurl, visitedpages " & _
                    "FROM tracking " & _
                    "ORDER BY trackingid DESC"

        Try

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Marc Olano
    '   Created Date: 07/28/03
    '   Event:  Returns pages for individual track id
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function track_pages(ByVal trackingid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim ds As New DataSet()

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT page, timeon " & _
                    "FROM trackingpages " & _
                    "WHERE trackingid = @trackingid " & _
                    "ORDER BY pageid asc"

        Try
            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@trackingid", trackingid)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: Chris Sibel              
    '   Created Date:5/2/03                 
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   gets login information to send to use
    '   WORKS
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function get_loginInfo(ByVal email As String) As DataSet

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim cnt As Integer

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT COUNT(*) AS cnt FROM dbo.people WHERE (email = @email)"

            Dim cmd2 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            cmd2.Parameters.Add("@email", email)
            cmd2.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(cmd2.ExecuteScalar)

            If cnt <> 0 Then
                sqlstmt = "SELECT dbo.users.screenname, dbo.users.password FROM dbo.people INNER JOIN dbo.users ON dbo.people.peopleid = dbo.users.peopleid WHERE (dbo.people.email = @email)"
                Dim ds As New DataSet()
                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@email", email)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
                Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

                da.Fill(ds)
                Return ds
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: Chris Sibel            
    '   Created Date:5/5/03                 
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Send login info to users email
    '   WORKS
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function SendLoginInfo(ByVal email As String, ByVal pwd As String, ByVal username As String) As String

        Try
            Dim mail As New Web.Mail.MailMessage()
            SmtpMail.SmtpServer = "192.168.1.57"
            mail.From = "sqlserver@wcpci.com"
            mail.To = email
            mail.Subject = "Requested Login Information"
            mail.Body = "Here is your requested login information: " & Chr(10) & Chr(10) & "Login Name: " & username & Chr(10) & "Password: " & pwd
            mail.BodyFormat = MailFormat.Text
            SmtpMail.Send(mail)

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Patricia Kline
    '   Created Date: 04/23/03
    '   Event:  Get people from rfiprojectto table for selected projectid 
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function rfi_Get_RfiProjectto(ByVal projid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim ds As New DataSet()

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT dbo.people.fname + ' ' + lname AS fullname, dbo.companies.name AS coname " & _
                  "FROM dbo.people INNER JOIN " & _
                    "dbo.rfiprojectto ON dbo.people.peopleid = dbo.rfiprojectto.peopleid INNER JOIN " & _
                    "dbo.companies ON dbo.people.companyid = dbo.companies.companyid " & _
                  "WHERE dbo.rfiprojectto.projectid = " & projid

        Try
            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Patricia Kline
    '   Created Date: 04/23/03
    '   Event:  Insert into rfiprojectto table with projectid and peopleid
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function rfi_Insert_RfiProjectto(ByVal projid As Integer, ByVal pplid As Integer) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim SqlStmt As String
        SqlStmt = "SELECT count(*) FROM rfiprojectto " & _
                 "WHERE projectid = " & projid & " " & _
                 "AND peopleid = " & pplid

        Try
            Dim cnt As Integer = 0
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand(SqlStmt, SqlConn)

            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(sqlCmd1.ExecuteScalar)
            SqlConn.Close()

            If cnt = 0 Then
                SqlStmt = LCase("INSERT INTO rfiprojectto (projectid, peopleid) " & _
                                           "VALUES (" & projid & ", " & pplid & ")")
                Dim SqlCmd As New SqlCommand(SqlStmt, SqlConn)

                If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                    SqlConn.Open()
                End If

                SqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
            End If

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Closed Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

        Catch ex As SqlException

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: marc olano             
    '   Created Date: 04/17/03                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event:  function to return existing rfi's for datagrid
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function rfi_getRFIs(ByVal projectid As Integer, ByVal peopleid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT dbo.rfis.rfiid, dbo.rfis.datecreated, dbo.rfis.closed, dbo.companies.name, dbo.rfis.submitted " & _
                    "FROM dbo.rfis " & _
                    "INNER JOIN dbo.people ON dbo.rfis.peopleid = dbo.people.peopleid " & _
                    "INNER JOIN dbo.projects ON dbo.rfis.peopleid = dbo.projects.projectid " & _
                    "INNER JOIN dbo.companies ON dbo.people.companyid = dbo.companies.companyid " & _
                    "WHERE (dbo.rfis.projectid = @projectid) AND (dbo.rfis.submitted = 1) OR " & _
                    "(dbo.rfis.projectid = @projectid) AND (dbo.rfis.peopleid = @peopleid) " & _
                    "ORDER BY dbo.rfis.rfiid DESC"

        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd.Parameters.Add("@peopleid", peopleid)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@projectid", projectid)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: marc olano            
    '   Created Date: 04/17/03                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event:  function to insert new RFI
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function rfi_addnewRFI(ByVal pplid As Integer, ByVal projid As Integer) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim datecreated As Date = Now
        Dim rfi As Integer

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO rfis (peopleid, projectid, datecreated, question) " & _
                "VALUES (@pplid , @projid ,'" & datecreated & "' , 'Question1')"

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@pplid", pplid)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            sqlstmt = "SELECT rfiid, datecreated " & _
                "FROM dbo.rfis " & _
                "WHERE (datecreated = '" & datecreated & "') " & _
                "AND (projectid = @projid) " & _
                "AND (peopleid = @pplid)"

            Dim cmd2 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd2.Parameters.Add("@pplid", pplid)
            cmd2.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)
            cmd2.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            rfi = CInt(cmd2.ExecuteScalar)

            Return rfi.ToString

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: marc olano             
    '   Created Date: 04/17/03                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event:  function to return contacts to projects
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function rfi_getPplName(ByVal pplid As Integer) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            Dim pplname As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT fname + ' ' + lname AS pplname " & _
                        "FROM dbo.people " & _
                        "WHERE (peopleid = @pplid)"

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd.Parameters.Add("@pplid", pplid)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            pplname = cmd.ExecuteScalar.ToString

            Return pplname.ToString

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return "Error" & ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: marc olano
    '   Created Date: 04/17/03                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event:  function to insert rfi info (from user)
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function rfi_addnewInfo(ByVal rfiid As Integer, ByVal jobnbr As String, ByVal respondby As String, ByVal specsection As String, ByVal drawingref As String, ByVal question As String, ByVal rfiRef As String) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try
            Dim respond As Date
            Dim ref As Integer
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "UPDATE rfis " & _
                        "SET jobnbr = @jobnbr, specsection = @specsection, drawingref = @drawingref, question = @question, submitted = 1 "

            If IsDate(respondby) Then
                respond = CDate(respondby)

                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & ", respondby= @respond "
            End If

            If IsNumeric(rfiRef) Then
                ref = CInt(rfiRef)
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & ", rfi_rfiid = @rfiRef "
            End If

            sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "WHERE rfiid = @rfiid"

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@rfiid", rfiid)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@jobnbr", jobnbr)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@specsection", specsection)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@drawingref", drawingref)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@question", question)

            If IsDate(respondby) Then
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@respond", respond)
            End If

            If IsNumeric(rfiRef) Then
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@rfiRef", ref)
            End If

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            Return "true"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: marc olano
    '   Created Date: 04/17/03                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event:  function to insert contact into user to: table
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function rfi_addUserTO(ByVal rfiid As Integer, ByVal sendTO As Integer) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim cnt As Integer

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT count(*) " & _
                            "FROM dbo.rfisto " & _
                            "WHERE (rfiid = @rfiid) " & _
                            "AND (peopleid = @sendTo)"

            Dim cmd2 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd2.Parameters.Add("@sendTO", sendTO)
            cmd2.Parameters.Add("@rfiid", rfiid)
            cmd2.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(cmd2.ExecuteScalar)

            If cnt = 0 Then
                sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO rfisto (rfiid, peopleid) values (@rfiid, @sendTO)"

                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

                cmd.Parameters.Add("@rfiid", rfiid)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@sendTO", sendTO)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
            End If

            Return "true"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: marc olano
    '   Created Date: 04/17/03                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event:  function to insert reply and return replyid
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function rfi_addReplyAttach(ByVal rfiid As Integer, ByVal pplid As Integer, ByVal reply As String) As String

        Dim datereplied As Date = Now
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim replyid As Integer

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO rfireplies (rfiid, peopleid, reply, datereplied) values (@rfiid, @pplid, @reply, '" & datereplied & "')"

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd.Parameters.Add("@rfiid", rfiid)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@pplid", pplid)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@reply", reply)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()

            sqlstmt = "SELECT replyid " & _
                        "FROM rfireplies " & _
                        "WHERE (rfiid = @rfiid) " & _
                        "AND (peopleid = @pplid) " & _
                        "AND (datereplied ='" & datereplied & "')"


            Dim cmd2 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            cmd2.Parameters.Add("@rfiid", rfiid)
            cmd2.Parameters.Add("@pplid", pplid)
            cmd2.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            replyid = CInt(cmd2.ExecuteScalar)

            Return replyid.ToString


        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If
        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: Chris Sibel              
    '   Created Date:5/27/03                 
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   gets email address based on peopleid
    '   WORKS
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function RFI_GetEmail(ByVal peopleid As Integer) As String


        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim cnt As Integer
        Dim email As String

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT COUNT(*) AS cnt FROM dbo.people WHERE (peopleid = @peopleid)"

            Dim cmd2 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            cmd2.Parameters.Add("@peopleid", peopleid)
            cmd2.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cmd2.ExecuteNonQuery()
            cnt = CInt(cmd2.ExecuteScalar)

            If cnt <> 0 Then
                sqlstmt = "SELECT dbo.people.email FROM dbo.people WHERE(peopleid = @peopleid)"
                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@peopleid", peopleid)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
                email = CStr(cmd.ExecuteScalar)
                Return email.ToString
            End If
        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    ' Programmer:  Patricia Kline                                    
    ' Created Date: 7/2/03
    ' Modified By: 
    ' Event: Get all inspection code categories
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function dc_GetCategories() As String()

        Dim result(0) As String
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim x As Integer = 1

        result(0) = ""

        Try
            sqlstmt = "SELECT DISTINCT cat1 FROM inspectioncodes"

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                ReDim Preserve result(x)
                result(x) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                x += 1
            End While

            sqlDr.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            ReDim result(2)
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = CStr(ex.Number)
            result(2) = ex.Message
            Return result

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 7/1/03                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: get all valid barcodes 
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function dc_GetBarcodes(ByVal projid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim ds As New DataSet()
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try
            sqlstmt = "SELECT dbo.documents.documentid, dbo.documents.docfilename " & _
                        "FROM dbo.documents INNER JOIN " & _
                            "plans ON documents.documentid = plans.documentid " & _
                        "WHERE dbo.documents.projectid = @projid " & _
                        " AND (plans.planscategory IN ('specifications', 'detail', 'structural detail')) " & _
                        "ORDER BY dbo.documents.docfilename "

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(sqlCmd)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    ' Programmer:  Patricia Kline                                    
    ' Created Date: 7/2/03
    ' Modified By: 
    ' Event: Get all unapproved iresults for specific project and inspector/super
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function dc_GetDataCollection(ByVal projid As Integer, ByVal pplid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim ds As New DataSet()
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlstmt As String

        Try
            sqlstmt = "SELECT datacollection.iresultsid, datacollection.unit, datacollection.bldg, datacollection.lot, datacollection.plantype, datacollection.phase, datacollection.code, " & _
                            "datacollection.locationname, companies.name AS subcontractor, documents.docfilename AS barcode, datacollection.attention, " & _
                            "datacollection.sir, datacollection.photo, datacollection.comment, datacollection.approved, datacollection.sub_companyid, datacollection.ire_iresultsid, " & _
                            "datacollection.sirnbr " & _
                        "FROM datacollection INNER JOIN " & _
                            "companies ON datacollection.sub_companyid = companies.companyid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "documents ON datacollection.barcodeid = documents.documentid " & _
                        "WHERE datacollection.projectid = @projid " & _
                        "AND approved = 0 " & _
                        "AND ins_peopleid = @pplid " & _
                        "ORDER BY Convert(Int, LEFT (datacollection.iresultsid, len(datacollection.iresultsid) - 8)) DESC"

            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)
            Sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@pplid", pplid)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    ' Programmer:  Patricia Kline                                    
    ' Created Date: 7/2/03
    ' Modified By: 
    ' Event: Get all unapproved iresults for specific project and inspector/super
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function dc_GetRefItems(ByVal projid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim ds As New DataSet()
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlstmt As String

        Try
            sqlstmt = "SELECT dbo.iresults.iresultsid, dbo.iresults.unit, dbo.iresults.bldg, dbo.iresults.lot, dbo.iresults.plantype, dbo.iresults.phase, dbo.iresults.code,  " & _
                            " dbo.iresults.locationname, dbo.companies.name AS subcontractor, dbo.documents.docfilename AS barcode, dbo.iresults.comment " & _
                        "FROM dbo.iresults INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.companies ON dbo.iresults.sub_companyid = dbo.companies.companyid INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.inspectionreportdetails ON dbo.iresults.projectid = dbo.inspectionreportdetails.projectid AND  " & _
                            "dbo.iresults.iresultsid = dbo.inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.documents INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.iresultstodetails ON dbo.documents.documentid = dbo.iresultstodetails.documentid ON " & _
                            "dbo.iresults.projectid = dbo.iresultstodetails.projectid " & _
                        "WHERE dbo.iresults.projectid = @projid " & _
                        "AND dbo.inspectionreportdetails.sdate IS NULL"

            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function dc_GetSeqByUnit(ByVal projid As Integer, ByVal unit As String) As String()

        Dim result(4) As String
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim x As Integer

        Try
            sqlstmt = "SELECT bldg, lot, plantype, unitplan, phase FROM seqsheets " & _
                        "WHERE unit = @unit AND projectid = @projid"

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@unit", unit)
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                For x = 0 To 4
                    result(x) = sqlDr.GetValue(x).ToString
                Next
            End While

            sqlDr.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            ReDim result(2)
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = CStr(ex.Number)
            result(2) = ex.Message
            Return result

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function dc_GetCode(ByVal cat As String, ByVal subcat As String, ByVal desc As String) As String()

        Dim result(1) As String
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim x As Integer

        Try
            sqlstmt = "SELECT code, location FROM inspectioncodes " & _
                        "WHERE cat1 = @cat " & _
                        "AND subcat = @subcat " & _
                        "AND description = @desc"

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@cat", cat)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@subcat", subcat)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@desc", desc)
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                For x = 0 To 1
                    result(x) = sqlDr.GetValue(x).ToString
                Next
            End While

            sqlDr.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            ReDim result(2)
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = CStr(ex.Number)
            result(2) = ex.Message
            Return result

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 7/1/03                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: get default subcontractor based on code and projid
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function dc_GetDefaultSub(ByVal projid As Integer, ByVal code As String) As String

        Dim result As String
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try
            sqlstmt = "SELECT dbo.companies.companyid " & _
                        "FROM dbo.companies INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.projectstosubcontractors ON dbo.companies.companyid = dbo.projectstosubcontractors.companyid " & _
                        "WHERE dbo.projectstosubcontractors.tradename IN (SELECT tradename FROM inspectioncodes WHERE code = @code) " & _
                        "AND projectid = @projid "

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@code", code)
            result = CStr(sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar)

            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return CStr(ex.Number) & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function dc_GetDefaultBarcode(ByVal projid As Integer, ByVal code As String) As String

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
