            sqlstmt = "SELECT dbo.documents.documentid " & _
                        "FROM dbo.documents INNER JOIN " & _
                             "dbo.codedetail ON dbo.documents.documentid = dbo.codedetail.documentid " & _
                        "WHERE projectid = @projid " & _
                        "AND code = @code "

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@code", code)
            result = CStr(sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar)

            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return "Error" & CStr(ex.Number) & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    ' Programmer:  Patricia Kline
    ' Created Date:  6/29/03                                               
    ' Modified Date:                                                    
    ' Event: Get macros
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function dc_GetMacros(ByVal pplid As Integer, ByVal code As String) As DataSet

        Dim ds As New DataSet()
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT m.description " & _
                        "FROM vcremote.dbo.macros m INNER JOIN " & _
                            "vcremote.dbo.codestomacros c ON m.peopleid = c.peopleid AND m.macroid = c.macroid " & _
                        "WHERE (m.peopleid = 1) AND ((c.catnbr & c.subcat) IN " & _
                            "(SELECT catnbr & subcatnbr " & _
                            "FROM inspectioncodes " & _
                            "WHERE code = @code)) " & _
                        "OR ((c.catnbr & c.subcat) IN" & _
                            "(SELECT catnbr & subcatnbr " & _
                            "FROM inspectioncodes " & _
                            "WHERE code = @code)) AND (c.sys = 1) " & _
                        "ORDER BY c.ordernbr"

            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@pplid", pplid)
            Sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@code", code)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date: 6/25/03 by Joe Dela Cruz                    
    '   Event:  check if super for date is in db and if not, add super
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function dc_addsuper(ByVal projid As Integer, ByVal pplid As Integer) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim cnt As Integer = 0

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT count(*) FROM superintendantsprojects " & _
                        "WHERE projectid = @projid " & _
                        "AND inspectiondate = '" & Today.ToShortDateString & "'"

        Try
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@pplid", pplid)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar)

            If cnt = 0 Then
                sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO superintendantsprojects (projectid, peopleid, inspectiondate)  " & _
                                        "VALUES (@projid, @pplid, '" & Today.ToShortDateString & "')"
                Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@pplid", pplid)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                cmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
            End If

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 7/1/03                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: get the next inspection item number for project
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function dc_GetNextID(ByVal projid As Integer, ByVal pplid As Integer) As String

        Dim result As String
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try
            sqlstmt = "SELECT iresultsid " & _
                        "FROM iresultids " & _
                        "WHERE projectid = @projid " & _
                        "AND peopleid =  @pplid "

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)
            sqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@pplid", pplid)

            result = CStr(sqlCmd.ExecuteScalar)

            If result = Nothing Then
                result = "1"
                sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO iresultids (projectid, peopleid, iresultsid) " & _
                            "VALUES (@projid, @pplid, " & CInt(result) + 1 & ")"
            Else
                sqlstmt = "UPDATE iresultids " & _
                            "SET iresultsid = " & CInt(result) + 1 & _
                            "WHERE projectid = @projid " & _
                            "AND peopleid =  @pplid "
            End If

            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlCmd1.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)
            sqlCmd1.Parameters.Add("@pplid", pplid)
            sqlCmd1.ExecuteNonQuery()

            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return CStr(ex.Number) & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '  Programmer:  Patricia Kline
    '  Created Date: 10/04/02         
    '  Modified Date: 
    '  Event: Add new iresult
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function dc_AddNew(ByVal projid As Integer, ByVal iresultsid As String, ByVal pplid As Integer, ByVal unit As String, ByVal bldg As String, ByVal lot As String, ByVal plantype As String, ByVal phase As String, ByVal code As String, ByVal locationname As String, ByVal sub_companyid As Integer, ByVal attention As Byte, ByVal photo As String, ByVal sir As Byte, ByVal comment As String, ByVal barcodeid As Integer, ByVal ref As String) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        Dim dayofyear As String = CStr(Format(Today.DayOfYear, "000"))
        Dim sirnbr As String = "s" & CStr(pplid) & dayofyear & Microsoft.VisualBasic.Right(CStr(Year(Today)), 1)

        Try
            sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO datacollection (projectid, iresultsid, inspectiondate, " & _
                            "ins_peopleid, unit, bldg, lot, plantype, phase, code, " & _
                            "locationname, sub_companyid, attention, photo, sirnbr, sir, " & _
                            "comment, barcodeid, ire_iresultsid, ire_projectid) " & _
                       "VALUES (@projid, @iresultsid, '" & Today.ToShortDateString & "', " & _
                            "@pplid, @unit, @bldg, @lot, @plantype, @phase, @code, " & _
                            "@locationname, @sub_companyid, @attention, @photo, @sirnbr, @sir, " & _
                            "@comment, @barcodeid "

            If ref = "NULL" Then
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & ", NULL, NULL)"
            Else
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & ", @ref, @projid)"
            End If

            Dim SqlCmd As New SqlCommand(LCase(sqlstmt), SqlConn)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@iresultsid", iresultsid)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@pplid", pplid)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@unit", unit)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@bldg", bldg)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@lot", lot)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@plantype", plantype)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@phase", phase)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@code", code)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@locationname", locationname)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@sub_companyid", sub_companyid)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@attention", attention)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@photo", photo)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@sirnbr", sirnbr)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@sir", sir)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@comment", comment)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@barcodeid", barcodeid)

            If ref <> "NULL" Then
                SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@ref", ref)
            End If

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Open()
            End If

            SqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '  Programmer:  Patricia Kline
    '  Created Date: 10/04/02         
    '  Modified Date: 
    '  Event: Add new iresult
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function dc_UpdateItem(ByVal projid As Integer, ByVal iresultsid As String, ByVal unit As String, ByVal bldg As String, ByVal lot As String, ByVal plantype As String, ByVal phase As String, ByVal code As String, ByVal locationname As String, ByVal sub_companyid As Integer, ByVal attention As Byte, ByVal photo As String, ByVal sir As Byte, ByVal comment As String, ByVal ref As String) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlstmt As String

        Try
            sqlstmt = "UPDATE datacollection " & _
                        "SET unit = @unit, bldg = @bldg, lot = @lot, plantype = @plantype, " & _
                            "phase = @phase, code = @code, locationname = @locationname, " & _
                            "sub_companyid = @sub_companyid, attention = @attention, " & _
                            "photo = @photo, sir = @sir, comment = @comment, "

            If ref = "NULL" Then
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "ire_iresultsid = NULL, ire_projectid = NULL "
            Else
                sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "ire_iresultsid = @ref, ire_projectid = @projid "
            End If

            sqlstmt = sqlstmt & "WHERE projectid = @projid " & _
                                "AND iresultsid = @iresultsid "

            Dim SqlCmd As New SqlCommand(LCase(sqlstmt), SqlConn)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@iresultsid", iresultsid)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@unit", unit)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@bldg", bldg)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@lot", lot)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@plantype", plantype)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@phase", phase)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@code", code)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@locationname", locationname)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@sub_companyid", sub_companyid)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@attention", attention)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@photo", photo)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@sir", sir)
            SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@comment", comment)

            If ref <> "NULL" Then
                SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@ref", ref)
            End If

            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Open()
            End If

            SqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '  Programmer:  Patricia Kline
    '  Created Date: 10/04/02         
    '  Modified Date: 
    '  Event: transfer from datacollection to iresults and mark as approved
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function dc_UpdateAppr(ByVal projid As Integer, ByVal ds As DataSet) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        Dim iresults As String = ""
        Dim docid As Integer

        Try
            Dim x As Integer

            With ds.Tables(0)
                For x = 0 To .Rows.Count - 1
                    If CBool(.Rows(x).Item(14)) = True Then
                        If iresults = "" Then
                            iresults = "'" & CStr(.Rows(x).Item(0)) & "'"
                        Else
                            iresults = iresults & ", '" & CStr(.Rows(x).Item(0)) & "'"
                        End If
                    End If
                Next
            End With

            If iresults <> "" Then
                sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO iresults (projectid, iresultsid, inspectiondate, sub_companyid, locationname, code, ins_peopleid, ire_iresultsid, ire_projectid, lot, bldg, unit, plantype, phase, comment, attention, inspected, approved) " & _
                           "SELECT projectid, iresultsid, inspectiondate, sub_companyid, locationname, code, ins_peopleid, ire_iresultsid, ire_projectid, lot, bldg, unit, plantype, phase, comment, attention, inspected, 1 FROM datacollection " & _
                             "WHERE projectid = @projid " & _
                            "AND iresultsid IN (" & iresults & ")"

                Dim SqlCmd As New SqlCommand(LCase(sqlstmt), SqlConn)
                SqlCmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)

                If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                    SqlConn.Open()
                End If

                SqlCmd.ExecuteNonQuery()
                iresults = ""

                With ds.Tables(0)
                    For x = 0 To .Rows.Count - 1
                        If CBool(.Rows(x).Item(14)) = True And CStr(.Rows(x).Item(12)) <> "" Then
                            If iresults = "" Then
                                iresults = "'" & CStr(.Rows(x).Item(0)) & "'"
                            Else
                                iresults = iresults & ", '" & CStr(.Rows(x).Item(0)) & "'"
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next
                End With

                sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO inspectionphotos (projectid, iresultsid, photofilename) " & _
                            "SELECT projectid, iresultsid, photo FROM datacollection " & _
                                "WHERE projectid = @projid " & _
                                    "AND iresultsid IN (" & iresults & ")"

                Dim SqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand(LCase(sqlstmt), SqlConn)
                SqlCmd1.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)

                If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                    SqlConn.Open()
                End If

                SqlCmd1.ExecuteNonQuery()
                iresults = ""

                With ds.Tables(0)
                    For x = 0 To .Rows.Count - 1
                        If CBool(.Rows(x).Item(14)) = True And CStr(.Rows(x).Item(9)) <> "" Then
                            If iresults = "" Then
                                iresults = "'" & CStr(.Rows(x).Item(0)) & "'"
                            Else
                                iresults = iresults & ", '" & CStr(.Rows(x).Item(0)) & "'"
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next
                End With

                sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO iresultstodetails (projectid, iresultsid, documentid) " & _
                            "SELECT projectid, iresultsid, barcodeid FROM datacollection " & _
                                "WHERE projectid = @projid " & _
                                    "AND iresultsid IN (" & iresults & ")"

                Dim SqlCmd2 As New SqlCommand(LCase(sqlstmt), SqlConn)
                SqlCmd2.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)

                If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                    SqlConn.Open()
                End If

                SqlCmd2.ExecuteNonQuery()

                sqlstmt = "DELETE datacollection " & _
                            "WHERE projectid = @projid " & _
                            "AND iresultsid IN (" & iresults & ")"

                Dim SqlCmd3 As New SqlCommand(LCase(sqlstmt), SqlConn)
                SqlCmd3.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)

                If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                    SqlConn.Open()
                End If

                SqlCmd3.ExecuteNonQuery()

                '0 datacollection.iresultsid, 1 datacollection.unit, 2 datacollection.bldg, 3 datacollection.lot, 4 datacollection.plantype, 5 datacollection.phase, 6 datacollection.code, 
                '7 datacollection.locationname, 8 companies.name AS subcontractor, 9 documents.docfilename AS barcode, 10 datacollection.attention, 
                '11 datacollection.sir, 12 datacollection.photo, 13 datacollection.comment, 14 datacollection.approved, 15 datacollection.sub_companyid, 16 datacollection.ire_iresultsid, 
                '17 datacollection.sirnbr                

                With ds.Tables(0)
                    For x = 0 To .Rows.Count - 1
                        If CBool(.Rows(x).Item(14)) = True And CBool(.Rows(x).Item(11)) = True Then
                            sqlstmt = "SELECT count(*) FROM documents " & _
                                        "WHERE projectid = @projid " & _
                                        "AND docfilename = '" & CStr(.Rows(x).Item(17)) & "'"

                            Dim SqlCmd4 As New SqlCommand(LCase(sqlstmt), SqlConn)
                            SqlCmd4.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)

                            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                                SqlConn.Open()
                            End If

                            Dim cnt As Integer = CInt(SqlCmd2.ExecuteScalar)

                            If cnt = 0 Then
                                sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO documents (documentcategory, projectid, docfilename) " & _
                                            "VALUES ('inspection report', @projid, '" & CStr(.Rows(x).Item(17)) & "')"

                                Dim SqlCmd5 As New SqlCommand(LCase(sqlstmt), SqlConn)
                                SqlCmd5.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)

                                If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                                    SqlConn.Open()
                                End If

                                SqlCmd5.ExecuteNonQuery()
                            End If

                            sqlstmt = "SELECT documentid FROM documents " & _
                                            "WHERE projectid = @projid " & _
                                            "AND docfilename = '" & CStr(.Rows(x).Item(17)) & "'"

                            Dim SqlCmd6 As New SqlCommand(LCase(sqlstmt), SqlConn)
                            SqlCmd6.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)

                            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                                SqlConn.Open()
                            End If

                            docid = CInt(SqlCmd6.ExecuteScalar)

                            sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO inspectionreports (documentid, projectid, iresultsid) " & _
                                            "VALUES (@docid, @projid, '" & CStr(.Rows(x).Item(0)) & "')"

                            Dim SqlCmd7 As New SqlCommand(LCase(sqlstmt), SqlConn)
                            SqlCmd7.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)
                            SqlCmd7.Parameters.Add("@docid", docid)

                            If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                                SqlConn.Open()
                            End If

                            SqlCmd7.ExecuteNonQuery()

                            If CBool(.Rows(x).Item(10)) = True Then
                                sqlstmt = "INSERT INTO inspectionreportdetails (documentid, projectid, iresultsid) " & _
                                                "VALUES (@docid, @projid, '" & CStr(.Rows(x).Item(0)) & "')"

                                Dim SqlCmd8 As New SqlCommand(LCase(sqlstmt), SqlConn)
                                SqlCmd8.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)
                                SqlCmd8.Parameters.Add("@docid", docid)

                                If Not SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                                    SqlConn.Open()
                                End If

                                SqlCmd8.ExecuteNonQuery()
                            End If
                        End If
                    Next
                End With

            End If

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 8/1/03                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: get list of all iresults that have been approved with photos 
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function dc_GetApprPhotos(ByVal projid As Integer) As String()

        Dim result(0) As String
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim x As Integer

        Try
            sqlstmt = "SELECT DISTINCT inspectionphotos.photofilename " & _
                        "FROM inspectionphotos LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "datacollection ON inspectionphotos.iresultsid = datacollection.iresultsid AND inspectionphotos.projectid = datacollection.projectid " & _
                        "WHERE (datacollection.projectid IS NULL) " & _
                        "ORDER BY inspectionphotos.photofilename"

            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                ReDim Preserve result(x)
                result(x) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                x += 1
            End While

            sqlDr.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            ReDim result(2)
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = CStr(ex.Number)
            result(2) = ex.Message
            Return result

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    ' Programmer:  Patricia Kline
    ' Created Date:  6/29/03                                               
    ' Modified Date:                                                    
    ' Event: Get sir items
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function dc_GetSirItems(ByVal projid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim ds As New DataSet()
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT dbo.companies.name AS coname, dbo.datacollection.iresultsid, dbo.datacollection.inspectiondate, dbo.datacollection.phase, dbo.datacollection.lot, " & _
                            "dbo.datacollection.bldg, dbo.datacollection.unit, dbo.datacollection.plantype, dbo.datacollection.comment, dbo.datacollection.attention, " & _
                            "dbo.inspectioncodes.tradename, dbo.projects.name AS projname, dbo.people.fname + ' ' + dbo.people.lname AS super, people_1.fname AS inspector, " & _
                            "dbo.datacollection.sirnbr, 0 as documentid " & _
                        "FROM dbo.superintendantsprojects INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.people ON dbo.superintendantsprojects.peopleid = dbo.people.peopleid INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.datacollection INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.people people_1 ON dbo.datacollection.ins_peopleid = people_1.peopleid INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.inspectioncodes ON dbo.datacollection.code = dbo.inspectioncodes.code INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.companies INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.projects ON dbo.companies.companyid = dbo.projects.companyid ON dbo.datacollection.projectid = dbo.projects.projectid ON " & _
                            "dbo.superintendantsprojects.projectid = dbo.datacollection.projectid AND " & _
                            "dbo.superintendantsprojects.inspectiondate = dbo.datacollection.inspectiondate " & _
                        "WHERE dbo.datacollection.projectid = @projid " & _
                        "AND dbo.datacollection.sir = 1 " & _
                        "AND dbo.datacollection.inspectiondate = '" & Today.ToShortDateString & "'"

            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   get path selected developer/project and sent to vcnet
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function mwgetProjinfo(ByVal developer As String, ByVal project As String) As String()

        Dim companyid As Integer
        Dim path As String

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("SELECT companyid, directory FROM buildersco " & _
                "WHERE companyid in (SELECT companyid FROM companies WHERE name = '" & developer & "')", SqlConn)

            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                companyid = CInt(sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString)
                path = sqlDr.GetValue(1).ToString
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            SqlConn.Close()

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Dim result(1) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

            Return result
        End Try

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim result(2) As String
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("SELECT projectid, directory, companyid FROM projects " & _
                "WHERE name = '" & project & "' AND companyid = " & companyid, SqlConn)

            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result(0) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                result(1) = path & "\" & sqlDr.GetValue(1).ToString & "\"
                result(2) = sqlDr.GetValue(2).ToString
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            path = result(0)
            SqlConn.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Dim result(1) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

            Return result
        End Try

    End Function


    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   send all projects for selected developer to vcent
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function mwgetProjects(ByVal developer As String, ByVal peopleid As Integer) As String()

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM projects " & _
                "WHERE companyid in (SELECT companyid FROM companies WHERE name = '" & developer & "') " & _
                "AND projectid in (SELECT projectid FROM usersaccesstoprojects WHERE peopleid = " & peopleid & ")", SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            Dim x As Integer = 0
            Dim result(cnt - 1) As String
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("SELECT name FROM projects " & _
                               "WHERE companyid in (SELECT companyid FROM companies WHERE name = '" & developer & "') " & _
                               "AND projectid in (SELECT projectid FROM usersaccesstoprojects WHERE peopleid = " & peopleid & ")", SqlConn)
            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result(x) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                x = x + 1
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            SqlConn.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            Dim result(1) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

            Return result
        End Try

    End Function


    <WebMethod()> Public Function mwGetOpenItems(ByVal projectid As Integer, ByVal search As Integer) As DataSet
        Dim strSQL As String
        If search = 1 Then
            strSQL = "SELECT iresults.lot as Lot, iresults.phase as Phase, iresults.inspectiondate as Date, iresults.locationname as Location, inspectioncodes.cat1 as Category, inspectioncodes.subcat as SubCat, iresults.comment as Comment FROM iresults INNER JOIN inspectioncodes ON iresults.code = inspectioncodes.code INNER JOIN inspectionreportdetails ON iresults.projectid = inspectionreportdetails.projectid AND iresults.iresultsid = inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid WHERE (iresults.projectid = @projectid) AND (inspectionreportdetails.sdate IS NULL) ORDER BY iresults.lot ASC, iresults.phase, iresults.locationname, inspectioncodes.cat1, inspectioncodes.subcat"
        ElseIf search = 2 Then
            strSQL = "SELECT iresults.bldg as Bldg, iresults.phase as Phase, iresults.inspectiondate as Date, iresults.locationname as Location, inspectioncodes.cat1 as Category, inspectioncodes.subcat as SubCat, iresults.comment as Comment FROM iresults INNER JOIN inspectioncodes ON iresults.code = inspectioncodes.code INNER JOIN inspectionreportdetails ON iresults.projectid = inspectionreportdetails.projectid AND iresults.iresultsid = inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid WHERE (iresults.projectid = @projectid) AND (inspectionreportdetails.sdate IS NULL) ORDER BY iresults.bldg ASC, iresults.phase, iresults.locationname, inspectioncodes.cat1, inspectioncodes.subcat"
        Else
            strSQL = "SELECT iresults.unit as Unit, iresults.phase as Phase, iresults.inspectiondate as Date, iresults.locationname as Location, inspectioncodes.cat1 as Category, inspectioncodes.subcat as SubCat, iresults.comment as Comment FROM iresults INNER JOIN inspectioncodes ON iresults.code = inspectioncodes.code INNER JOIN inspectionreportdetails ON iresults.projectid = inspectionreportdetails.projectid AND iresults.iresultsid = inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid WHERE (iresults.projectid = @projectid) AND (inspectionreportdetails.sdate IS NULL) ORDER BY iresults.unit ASC, iresults.phase, iresults.locationname, inspectioncodes.cat1, inspectioncodes.subcat"
        End If


        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@projectid", projectid)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()

        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function mwGetSeqSheetInfo(ByVal projectid As Integer) As String

        Dim strSQL As String
        strSQL = "SELECT lot, bldg, unit from seqsheets where projectid = @projectid"
        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@projectid", projectid)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim lot As Boolean
            Dim bldg As Boolean
            Dim unit As Boolean
            Dim retValues As String
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                If Not sqlDr.IsDBNull(0) And sqlDr.GetString(0) <> "" Then
                    lot = True
                End If
                If Not sqlDr.IsDBNull(1) And sqlDr.GetString(1) <> "" Then
                    bldg = True
                End If
                If Not sqlDr.IsDBNull(2) And sqlDr.GetString(2) <> "" Then
                    unit = True
                End If
            End While

            If lot Then
                retValues = "1"
            Else
                retValues = "0"
            End If

            If bldg Then
                retValues = retValues & "1"
            Else
                retValues = retValues & "0"
            End If
            If unit Then
                retValues = retValues & "1"
            Else
                retValues = retValues & "0"
            End If

            Return retValues

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
            SqlConn.Close()

        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function mwGetSeqSheetDetails(ByVal projectid As Integer, ByVal search As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim strSQL As String
        If search = 1 Then
            strSQL = "SELECT DISTINCT lot FROM seqsheets WHERE projectid = @projectid"
        ElseIf search = 2 Then
            strSQL = "SELECT DISTINCT bldg FROM seqsheets WHERE projectid = @projectid"
        Else
            strSQL = "SELECT DISTINCT unit FROM seqsheets WHERE projectid = @projectid"
        End If
        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@projectid", projectid)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
            SqlConn.Close()

        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Private Function mwGetSeqSheetDetails1(ByVal projectid As String, ByVal item As String, ByVal searchtype As String) As String
        Dim strSQL As String = "SELECT plantype, phase FROM seqsheets WHERE " & searchtype & " = @item AND projectid = @projectid"
        Dim plantype As String
        Dim phase As String
        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@projectid", projectid)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@item", item)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                plantype = sqlDr.GetString(0)
                phase = sqlDr.GetString(1)
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()
            SqlConn.Close()
            Return plantype & "^" & phase
        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
            SqlConn.Close()
        End Try
    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function mwGetCheckList(ByVal projectid As String, ByVal item As String, ByVal searchtype As String) As DataSet
        Dim tstRecords As Boolean
        Dim strVaules As String = mwGetSeqSheetDetails1(projectid, item, searchtype)

        Dim Str As String = strVaules
        Dim plantype As String = Microsoft.VisualBasic.Left(Str, InStr(Str, "^") - 1)
        Dim phase As String = Microsoft.VisualBasic.Right(Str, Len(Str) - InStr(Str, "^"))
        If plantype <> "" And phase <> "" Then
            tstRecords = True
        End If
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim ds As New DataSet()
        Dim strSQL As String
        Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
        Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
        If tstRecords Then
            Try
                'strSQL = "SELECT inspectioncodes.description, projectinspectioncodes.adescription, inspectioncodes.code, projectinspectioncodes.phase, inspectioncodes.cat1, inspectioncodes.subcat FROM projectinspectioncodes INNER JOIN inspectioncodes ON projectinspectioncodes.code = inspectioncodes.code WHERE (projectinspectioncodes.projectid = @projectid) AND (projectinspectioncodes.phase = @phase) AND (projectinspectioncodes.plantype = @plantype) ORDER BY catnbr, subcatnbr, itemnbr"
                strSQL = "SELECT distinct inspectioncodes.cat1 as Category  FROM projectinspectioncodes INNER JOIN inspectioncodes ON projectinspectioncodes.code = inspectioncodes.code WHERE (projectinspectioncodes.projectid = @projectid) AND (projectinspectioncodes.phase = @phase) AND (projectinspectioncodes.plantype = @plantype)"
                SqlConn = New SqlConnection(strConn)
                ds = New DataSet()
                cmd = New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@projectid", projectid)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@phase", phase)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@plantype", plantype)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)
                da.Fill(ds)
                Return ds
                SqlConn.Close()
            Catch ex As SqlException
                Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
                SqlConn.Close()
            End Try
        End If
    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function mwGetCountCheckListDetails(ByVal projectid As String, ByVal item As String, ByVal searchtype As String, ByVal category As String, ByVal peopleid As String) As Integer
        Dim tstRecords As Boolean
        Dim strVaules As String = mwGetSeqSheetDetails1(projectid, item, searchtype)
        Dim Str As String = strVaules
        Dim plantype As String = Microsoft.VisualBasic.Left(Str, InStr(Str, "^") - 1)
        Dim phase As String = Microsoft.VisualBasic.Right(Str, Len(Str) - InStr(Str, "^"))
        If plantype <> "" And phase <> "" Then
            tstRecords = True
        End If
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim ds As New DataSet()
        Dim strSQL As String
        Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
        Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
        If tstRecords Then
            Try
                If searchtype = "unit" Then
                    strSQL = "SELECT  count(*) FROM         projectinspectioncodes INNER JOIN inspectioncodes ON projectinspectioncodes.code = inspectioncodes.code LEFT OUTER JOIN (SELECT projectid, code, iresultsid, comment, attention, ins_peopleid FROM  iresults WHERE ins_peopleid = @peopleid AND unit = @item) i ON projectinspectioncodes.projectid = i.projectid AND inspectioncodes.code = i.code LEFT OUTER JOIN inspectionreportdetails ON i.projectid = inspectionreportdetails.projectid AND i.iresultsid = inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid WHERE     (projectinspectioncodes.projectid = @projectid) AND (projectinspectioncodes.phase = @phase) AND (inspectioncodes.cat1 = @category) AND (projectinspectioncodes.plantype = @plantype)"
                ElseIf searchtype = "bldg" Then
                    strSQL = "SELECT  count(*) FROM         projectinspectioncodes INNER JOIN inspectioncodes ON projectinspectioncodes.code = inspectioncodes.code LEFT OUTER JOIN (SELECT projectid, code, iresultsid, comment, attention, ins_peopleid FROM  iresults WHERE ins_peopleid = @peopleid AND bldg = @item AND (unit = '' OR unit is null)) i ON projectinspectioncodes.projectid = i.projectid AND inspectioncodes.code = i.code LEFT OUTER JOIN inspectionreportdetails ON i.projectid = inspectionreportdetails.projectid AND i.iresultsid = inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid WHERE     (projectinspectioncodes.projectid = @projectid) AND (projectinspectioncodes.phase = @phase) AND (inspectioncodes.cat1 = @category) AND (projectinspectioncodes.plantype = @plantype)"
                Else
                    strSQL = "SELECT  count(*) FROM         projectinspectioncodes INNER JOIN inspectioncodes ON projectinspectioncodes.code = inspectioncodes.code LEFT OUTER JOIN (SELECT projectid, code, iresultsid, comment, attention, ins_peopleid FROM  iresults WHERE ins_peopleid = @peopleid AND lot = @item) i ON projectinspectioncodes.projectid = i.projectid AND inspectioncodes.code = i.code LEFT OUTER JOIN inspectionreportdetails ON i.projectid = inspectionreportdetails.projectid AND i.iresultsid = inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid WHERE     (projectinspectioncodes.projectid = @projectid) AND (projectinspectioncodes.phase = @phase) AND (inspectioncodes.cat1 = @category) AND (projectinspectioncodes.plantype = @plantype)"
                End If







                'strSQL = "SELECT inspectioncodes.description as Description, projectinspectioncodes.adescription as Additional, inspectioncodes.code as Code, inspectioncodes.cat1, inspectioncodes.subcat FROM projectinspectioncodes INNER JOIN inspectioncodes ON projectinspectioncodes.code = inspectioncodes.code WHERE (projectinspectioncodes.projectid = @projectid) AND (projectinspectioncodes.phase = @phase) AND ( inspectioncodes.cat1 = @category) AND (projectinspectioncodes.plantype = @plantype) ORDER BY catnbr, subcatnbr, itemnbr"
                SqlConn = New SqlConnection(strConn)
                cmd = New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@projectid", projectid)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@phase", phase)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@plantype", plantype)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@category", category)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@peopleid", peopleid)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@item", item)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing
                SqlConn.Open()
                sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
                While sqlDr.Read
                    cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
                End While
                sqlDr.Close()
                Return cnt
            Catch ex As SqlException
                Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
                SqlConn.Close()
            End Try
        End If
    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function mwGetCheckListDetails(ByVal projectid As String, ByVal item As String, ByVal searchtype As String, ByVal category As String, ByVal cnt As Integer, ByVal peopleid As String) As String()
        Dim tstRecords As Boolean
        Dim strVaules As String = mwGetSeqSheetDetails1(projectid, item, searchtype)
        Dim Str As String = strVaules
        Dim plantype As String = Microsoft.VisualBasic.Left(Str, InStr(Str, "^") - 1)
        Dim phase As String = Microsoft.VisualBasic.Right(Str, Len(Str) - InStr(Str, "^"))
        If plantype <> "" And phase <> "" Then
            tstRecords = True
        End If
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim ds As New DataSet()
        Dim strSQL As String
        Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
        Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
        If tstRecords Then

            Try
                'strSQL = "SELECT  count(*) FROM projectinspectioncodes INNER JOIN inspectioncodes ON projectinspectioncodes.code = inspectioncodes.code WHERE (projectinspectioncodes.projectid = @projectid) AND (projectinspectioncodes.phase = @phase) AND ( inspectioncodes.cat1 = @category) AND (projectinspectioncodes.plantype = @plantype) ORDER BY catnbr, subcatnbr, itemnbr"
                'strSQL = "SELECT inspectioncodes.description as Description, projectinspectioncodes.adescription as Additional, inspectioncodes.code as Code, inspectioncodes.subcat FROM projectinspectioncodes INNER JOIN inspectioncodes ON projectinspectioncodes.code = inspectioncodes.code WHERE (projectinspectioncodes.projectid = @projectid) AND (projectinspectioncodes.phase = @phase) AND ( inspectioncodes.cat1 = @category) AND (projectinspectioncodes.plantype = @plantype) ORDER BY catnbr, subcatnbr, itemnbr"
                'strSQL = "SELECT inspectioncodes.description AS Description, projectinspectioncodes.adescription AS Additional, inspectioncodes.code AS Code,inspectioncodes.subcat, iresults.iresultsid, iresults.comment, iresults.attention, inspectionreportdetails.documentid FROM         inspectionreportdetails RIGHT OUTER JOIN                     iresults ON inspectionreportdetails.projectid = iresults.projectid AND inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid = iresults.iresultsid RIGHT OUTER JOIN projectinspectioncodes INNER JOIN inspectioncodes ON projectinspectioncodes.code = inspectioncodes.code ON iresults.code = inspectioncodes.code WHERE     (projectinspectioncodes.projectid = @projectid) AND (projectinspectioncodes.phase = @phase) AND (inspectioncodes.cat1 = @category) AND (projectinspectioncodes.plantype = @plantype) ORDER BY inspectioncodes.catnbr, inspectioncodes.subcatnbr, inspectioncodes.itemnbr"
                'strSQL = "SELECT inspectioncodes.description AS Description, projectinspectioncodes.adescription AS Additional, inspectioncodes.code AS Code, inspectioncodes.subcat, iresults.iresultsid, iresults.comment, iresults.attention, inspectionreportdetails.documentid FROM         projectinspectioncodes INNER JOIN inspectioncodes ON projectinspectioncodes.code = inspectioncodes.code LEFT OUTER JOIN iresults ON projectinspectioncodes.projectid = iresults.projectid AND inspectioncodes.code = iresults.code LEFT OUTER JOIN inspectionreportdetails ON iresults.projectid = inspectionreportdetails.projectid AND iresults.iresultsid = inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid WHERE (projectinspectioncodes.projectid = @projectid) AND (projectinspectioncodes.phase = @phase) AND (inspectioncodes.cat1 = @category) AND (projectinspectioncodes.plantype = @plantype) ORDER BY inspectioncodes.catnbr, inspectioncodes.subcatnbr, inspectioncodes.itemnbr"
                'strSQL = "SELECT     inspectioncodes.description AS Description, projectinspectioncodes.adescription AS Additional, inspectioncodes.code AS Code, inspectioncodes.subcat, i.iresultsid, i.comment, i.attention, inspectionreportdetails.documentid, i.ins_peopleid, i.inspected FROM         projectinspectioncodes INNER JOIN inspectioncodes ON projectinspectioncodes.code = inspectioncodes.code LEFT OUTER JOIN (SELECT projectid, code, iresultsid, comment, attention, ins_peopleid, inspected FROM  iresults WHERE ins_peopleid = @peopleid) i ON projectinspectioncodes.projectid = i.projectid AND inspectioncodes.code = i.code LEFT OUTER JOIN inspectionreportdetails ON i.projectid = inspectionreportdetails.projectid AND i.iresultsid = inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid WHERE     (projectinspectioncodes.projectid = @projectid) AND (projectinspectioncodes.phase = @phase) AND (inspectioncodes.cat1 = @category) AND (projectinspectioncodes.plantype = @plantype)"
                strSQL = "SELECT     inspectioncodes.description AS Description, projectinspectioncodes.adescription AS Additional, inspectioncodes.code AS Code, inspectioncodes.subcat, i.iresultsid, i.comment, i.attention, inspectionreportdetails.documentid, i.ins_peopleid, i.inspected FROM         projectinspectioncodes INNER JOIN inspectioncodes ON projectinspectioncodes.code = inspectioncodes.code LEFT OUTER JOIN (SELECT projectid, code, iresultsid, comment, attention, ins_peopleid, inspected FROM  iresults WHERE ins_peopleid = @peopleid) i ON projectinspectioncodes.projectid = i.projectid AND inspectioncodes.code = i.code LEFT OUTER JOIN inspectionreportdetails ON i.projectid = inspectionreportdetails.projectid AND i.iresultsid = inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid WHERE     (projectinspectioncodes.projectid = @projectid) AND (projectinspectioncodes.phase = @phase) AND (inspectioncodes.cat1 = @category) AND (projectinspectioncodes.plantype = @plantype)"

                If searchtype = "unit" Then
                    strSQL = "SELECT  inspectioncodes.description AS Description, projectinspectioncodes.adescription AS Additional, inspectioncodes.code AS Code, inspectioncodes.subcat, i.iresultsid, i.comment, i.attention, inspectionreportdetails.documentid, i.ins_peopleid, i.inspected FROM         projectinspectioncodes INNER JOIN inspectioncodes ON projectinspectioncodes.code = inspectioncodes.code LEFT OUTER JOIN (SELECT projectid, code, iresultsid, comment, attention, ins_peopleid, inspected FROM  iresults WHERE ins_peopleid = @peopleid AND unit = @item) i ON projectinspectioncodes.projectid = i.projectid AND inspectioncodes.code = i.code LEFT OUTER JOIN inspectionreportdetails ON i.projectid = inspectionreportdetails.projectid AND i.iresultsid = inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid WHERE     (projectinspectioncodes.projectid = @projectid) AND (projectinspectioncodes.phase = @phase) AND (inspectioncodes.cat1 = @category) AND (projectinspectioncodes.plantype = @plantype)"
                ElseIf searchtype = "bldg" Then
                    strSQL = "SELECT  inspectioncodes.description AS Description, projectinspectioncodes.adescription AS Additional, inspectioncodes.code AS Code, inspectioncodes.subcat, i.iresultsid, i.comment, i.attention, inspectionreportdetails.documentid, i.ins_peopleid, i.inspected FROM         projectinspectioncodes INNER JOIN inspectioncodes ON projectinspectioncodes.code = inspectioncodes.code LEFT OUTER JOIN (SELECT projectid, code, iresultsid, comment, attention, ins_peopleid, inspected FROM  iresults WHERE ins_peopleid = @peopleid AND bldg = @item AND (unit = '' OR unit is null)) i ON projectinspectioncodes.projectid = i.projectid AND inspectioncodes.code = i.code LEFT OUTER JOIN inspectionreportdetails ON i.projectid = inspectionreportdetails.projectid AND i.iresultsid = inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid WHERE     (projectinspectioncodes.projectid = @projectid) AND (projectinspectioncodes.phase = @phase) AND (inspectioncodes.cat1 = @category) AND (projectinspectioncodes.plantype = @plantype)"
                Else
                    strSQL = "SELECT  inspectioncodes.description AS Description, projectinspectioncodes.adescription AS Additional, inspectioncodes.code AS Code, inspectioncodes.subcat, i.iresultsid, i.comment, i.attention, inspectionreportdetails.documentid, i.ins_peopleid, i.inspected FROM         projectinspectioncodes INNER JOIN inspectioncodes ON projectinspectioncodes.code = inspectioncodes.code LEFT OUTER JOIN (SELECT projectid, code, iresultsid, comment, attention, ins_peopleid, inspected FROM  iresults WHERE ins_peopleid = @peopleid AND lot = @item) i ON projectinspectioncodes.projectid = i.projectid AND inspectioncodes.code = i.code LEFT OUTER JOIN inspectionreportdetails ON i.projectid = inspectionreportdetails.projectid AND i.iresultsid = inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid WHERE     (projectinspectioncodes.projectid = @projectid) AND (projectinspectioncodes.phase = @phase) AND (inspectioncodes.cat1 = @category) AND (projectinspectioncodes.plantype = @plantype)"
                End If


                SqlConn = New SqlConnection(strConn)
                cmd = New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@projectid", projectid)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@phase", phase)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@plantype", plantype)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@category", category)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@peopleid", peopleid)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@item", item)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                Dim x As Integer = 0
                Dim result(cnt - 1) As String
                SqlConn.Open()
                sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
                While sqlDr.Read
                    result(x) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString & "^" & sqlDr.GetValue(1).ToString & "^" & sqlDr.GetValue(2).ToString & "^" & sqlDr.GetValue(3).ToString & "^" & sqlDr.GetValue(4).ToString & "^" & sqlDr.GetValue(5).ToString & "^" & sqlDr.GetValue(6).ToString & "^" & sqlDr.GetValue(7).ToString & "^" & sqlDr.GetValue(9).ToString & "^"
                    x = x + 1
                End While
                sqlDr.Close()
                Return result

            Catch ex As SqlException
                Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
                SqlConn.Close()
                Dim result(1) As String
                result(0) = "Error"
                result(1) = ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message
            End Try

        End If
    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function mwInsertPunchlist(ByVal projectid As String, ByVal item As String, ByVal searchtype As String, ByVal category As String, ByVal code As String, ByVal value As String, ByVal peopleid As String, ByVal comment As String, ByVal iresults As String) As Integer
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim strSQL As String = "SELECT lot, unit, bldg, plantype, phase FROM seqsheets WHERE " & searchtype & " = @item AND projectid = @projectid"
        Dim plantype As String
        Dim phase As String
        Dim lot As String
        Dim unit As String
        Dim bldg As String
        Dim approved As Boolean = True
        Dim attention As Boolean
        Dim inspectiondate As Date = Today
        'Dim comment As String
        Dim updatedate As Date = Now
        Dim locationname As String = ""

        Dim ireProjid As String = projectid
        If Len(ireProjid) < 2 Then
            ireProjid = "0" & ireProjid
        Else
            ireProjid = Right(ireProjid, 2)
        End If
        Dim irePeopleid As String = peopleid
        Dim Number As Integer = Len(irePeopleid)
        Select Case Number
            Case 1
                irePeopleid = "000" & irePeopleid
            Case 2
                irePeopleid = "00" & irePeopleid
            Case 3
                irePeopleid = "0" & irePeopleid
        End Select

        Dim ireFrom As String = "01"
        Dim ire_projectid As String = ""
        Dim ire_iresultsid As String = ""
        Dim sub_companyid As Integer = 1
        Dim tstInsertIntoSir As Boolean
        Dim tstRemoveSir As Boolean
        Dim tstInspected As Boolean
        Dim SuperId As String
        If LCase(value) = "requires attention" Then
            'need to insert into inspectionreportdetails table REQUIRES ATTENTION
            tstInsertIntoSir = True
            attention = True
            tstInspected = True
        ElseIf LCase(value) = "no attention required" Then 'NO ATTENTION REQUIRED
            attention = False
            tstRemoveSir = True
            tstInspected = True
        Else
            'need to insert into inspectionreportdetails table NOT INSPECTED
            tstInsertIntoSir = False
            attention = False
            tstInspected = False
            tstRemoveSir = True
        End If
        Dim tstRecords As Boolean
        Dim tstExists As Boolean
        Dim tstUpdateSir As Boolean
        Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
        Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
        Dim iresultsid As String
        Dim CurrentIresultsid As String
        If iresults = "noid" Then 'new item
            tstExists = False
            Dim cntr As Integer = mwMAXGetIresultsid(projectid)

            iresultsid = cntr & ireProjid & irePeopleid & ireFrom
            CurrentIresultsid = iresultsid
            Try
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@projectid", projectid)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@item", item)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                SqlConn.Open()
                sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
                While sqlDr.Read
                    lot = sqlDr.GetString(0)
                    unit = sqlDr.GetString(1)
                    bldg = sqlDr.GetString(2)
                    plantype = sqlDr.GetString(3)
                    phase = sqlDr.GetString(4)
                End While
                sqlDr.Close()
                SqlConn.Close()
            Catch ex As SqlException
                Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
                SqlConn.Close()
            End Try

            If plantype <> "" And phase <> "" Then
                tstRecords = True
            End If
        Else 'old item update
            tstRecords = True
            tstExists = True
            Dim tstExistsSir As Boolean = mwtstExists4UpdateSir(iresults, projectid)
            If tstExistsSir Then
                tstUpdateSir = True
            Else
                tstUpdateSir = False
            End If
            CurrentIresultsid = iresults
            iresultsid = iresults
        End If



        'Dim tstExistsInt As Integer = mwtstExists4Update(projectid, code, phase, lot, unit, bldg, plantype, peopleid)

        'If tstExistsInt > 0 Then
        '    tstExists = True
        '    If tstExistsInt > 1 Then
        '        'has sir attached needs to update??!?!?
        '        tstUpdateSir = True
        '    End If
        'Else
        '    tstExists = False
        'End If



        'check to see if record already exists
        If tstRecords Then
            Try

                If tstExists Then 'record exists do an update
                    If tstInspected Then 'not inspected need to enter in what table???
                        'strSQL = "UPDATE iresults set inspected = @inspected, comment = @comment, attention = @attention WHERE lot = @lot AND projectid = @projectid AND code = @code AND unit = @unit AND bldg = @bldg AND plantype = @plantype AND phase = @phase"
                        If value = "DCDnone" Then
                            strSQL = "UPDATE iresults set comment = @comment WHERE iresultsid = @iresultsid"
                        Else
                            strSQL = "UPDATE iresults set inspected = @inspected, comment = @comment, attention = @attention WHERE iresultsid = @iresultsid"
                        End If

                    Else
                        ' strSQL = "UPDATE iresults set inspected = @inspected, comment = @comment, attention = @attention WHERE lot = @lot AND projectid = @projectid AND code = @code AND unit = @unit AND bldg = @bldg AND plantype = @plantype AND phase = @phase"
                        If value = "DCDnone" Then
                            strSQL = "UPDATE iresults set comment = @comment WHERE iresultsid = @iresultsid"
                        Else
                            strSQL = "UPDATE iresults set inspected = @inspected, comment = @comment, attention = @attention WHERE iresultsid = @iresultsid"
                        End If
                    End If
                    If comment = "DCDnone" Then
                        strSQL = "UPDATE iresults set inspected = @inspected, attention = @attention WHERE iresultsid = @iresultsid"
                        SqlConn = New SqlConnection(strConn)
                        cmd = New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
                        cmd.Parameters.Add("@iresultsid", iresultsid)
                        cmd.Parameters.Add("@inspected", tstInspected)
                        cmd.Parameters.Add("@attention", attention)
                        cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                        Dim x As Integer = 0
                        SqlConn.Open()
                        sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
                        'While sqlDr.Read

                        'End While
                        sqlDr.Close()
                        SqlConn.Close()
                    Else
                        SqlConn = New SqlConnection(strConn)
                        cmd = New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
                        cmd.Parameters.Add("@iresultsid", iresultsid)
                        cmd.Parameters.Add("@inspected", tstInspected)
                        cmd.Parameters.Add("@comment", comment)
                        cmd.Parameters.Add("@attention", attention)
                        cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                        Dim x As Integer = 0
                        SqlConn.Open()
                        sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
                        'While sqlDr.Read

                        'End While
                        sqlDr.Close()
                        SqlConn.Close()
                    End If






                Else 'record doesn't exist do an insert
                    'strSQL = "INSERT INTO iresults (projectid, iresultsid, sub_companyid, locationname, code, ins_peopleid, ire_iresultsid, ire_projectid, lot, bldg, unit, plantype, phase, comment, attention, approved, inspectiondate, updateddate) " & _
                    '                "values (@projectid, @iresultsid, @sub_companyid, @locationname, @code, @ins_peopleid, @ire_iresultsid, @ire_projectid, @lot, @bldg, @unit, @plantype, @phase, @comment, @attention, @approved, @inspectiondate, @updatedate)"
                    If tstInspected Then 'not inspected need to enter in what table???
                        strSQL = "INSERT INTO iresults (inspected, projectid, iresultsid, sub_companyid, locationname, code, ins_peopleid,  lot, bldg, unit, plantype, phase, comment, attention, approved, inspectiondate, updateddate) " & _
                                                   "values (@inspected, @projectid, @iresultsid, @sub_companyid, @locationname, @code, @ins_peopleid,  @lot, @bldg, @unit, @plantype, @phase, @comment, @attention, @approved, @inspectiondate, @updatedate)"

                    Else
                        strSQL = "INSERT INTO iresults (inspected, projectid, iresultsid, sub_companyid, locationname, code, ins_peopleid,  lot, bldg, unit, plantype, phase, comment, attention, approved, inspectiondate, updateddate) " & _
                                                   "values (@inspected, @projectid, @iresultsid, @sub_companyid, @locationname, @code, @ins_peopleid,  @lot, @bldg, @unit, @plantype, @phase, @comment, @attention, @approved, @inspectiondate, @updatedate)"

                    End If
                    If searchtype = "bldg" Then
                        unit = ""
                    End If
                    If comment = "DCDnone" Then
                        comment = ""
                    End If
                    SqlConn = New SqlConnection(strConn)
                    cmd = New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
                    cmd.Parameters.Add("@projectid", projectid)
                    cmd.Parameters.Add("@iresultsid", iresultsid)
                    cmd.Parameters.Add("@sub_companyid", sub_companyid)
                    cmd.Parameters.Add("@locationname", locationname)
                    cmd.Parameters.Add("@code", code)

    Private Function mwMAXGetIresultsid(ByVal projectid As String) As Integer
        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            'Dim strSQL As String = "SELECT MAX(iresultsid) FROM iresults WHERE projectid = @projectid "
            Dim strSQL As String = "SELECT MAX(CONVERT(NUMERIC,iresultsid)) FROM iresults WHERE projectid = @projectid "

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
            Dim mxIresults As String
            Dim incrIresults As Integer
            cmd = New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@projectid", projectid)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                mxIresults = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()
            SqlConn.Close()
            Dim lenIre As Integer = Len(mxIresults)
            If Len(mxIresults) < 9 Then
                incrIresults = 1
            Else
                incrIresults = CInt(Left(mxIresults.ToString, (Len(mxIresults) - 8)))
            End If
            incrIresults += 1
            Return incrIresults
        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
            SqlConn.Close()
        End Try
    End Function

    Private Function mwInsertPunchlistDocSir(ByVal iresultsid As String, ByVal projectid As String, ByVal peopleid As String) As Boolean
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim doy As String = Right(Now.Year.ToString, 1)
        Dim peopleid2 As String = Right(peopleid, 3)
        Dim today As String = Now.DayOfYear.ToString
        Dim strToday As String = today

        If Len(today) < 3 Then
            If Len(today) < 2 Then
                strToday = "00" & today.ToString
            Else
                strToday = "0" & today.ToString
            End If
        End If


        Dim docfilename As String = "m" & peopleid2 & strToday & doy & ".tif"
        Dim tstExistsDoc As String = mwtstExistsDoc(projectid, docfilename)
        Dim strSQL As String = "INSERT INTO documents (projectid, documentcategory, docfilename) values (@projectid, @documentcategory, @docfilename)"
        Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
        Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
        If tstExistsDoc = "none" Then 'deosn't exists in the documents table add to doc table
            Try
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@projectid", projectid)
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@documentcategory", "inspection report")
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@docfilename", docfilename)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                SqlConn.Open()
                sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
                sqlDr.Close()
                SqlConn.Close()
                tstExistsDoc = mwtstExistsDoc(projectid, docfilename) 'inserted now get documentid
                mwInsertPunchlistSir(iresultsid, projectid, peopleid, tstExistsDoc)
            Catch ex As SqlException
                Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
                SqlConn.Close()
            End Try
        Else
            'strSQL = "SELECT documentid FROM documents WHERE docfilename = @docfilename"
            'cmd.Dispose()
            'cmd = New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
            'Try
            '    cmd.Parameters.Add("@docfilename", docfilename)
            '    cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            '    SqlConn.Open()
            '    sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
            '    While sqlDr.Read
            '        Dim documentid As String = sqlDr.GetSqlValue(0).ToString

            '    End While
            '    sqlDr.Close()
            '    SqlConn.Close()
            'Catch ex As SqlException
            '    SqlConn.Close()
            'End Try
            mwInsertPunchlistSir(iresultsid, projectid, peopleid, tstExistsDoc)
        End If


    End Function

    Private Function mwtstExistsDoc(ByVal projectid As String, ByVal docfilename As String) As String
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim strSQL As String = "SELECT documentid FROM documents WHERE projectid = @projectid AND docfilename = @docfilename"
        Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
        Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
        Try
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@projectid", projectid)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@docfilename", docfilename)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                Dim documentid As String = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                Return documentid
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()
            SqlConn.Close()
            Return "none"
        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
            SqlConn.Close()
        End Try
        Return "none"
    End Function

    Private Function mwRemovePunchlistDocSir(ByVal iresultsid As String, ByVal projectid As String) As Boolean
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim documentid As String
        Dim strSQL As String = "SELECT documentid FROM inspectionreportdetails WHERE projectid = @projectid AND iresultsid = @iresultsid"
        Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
        Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
        Try
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@projectid", projectid)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@iresultsid", iresultsid)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                documentid = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()
            SqlConn.Close()
        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
            SqlConn.Close()
        End Try

        cmd.Dispose()
        strSQL = "SELECT count(*) FROM inspectionreportdetails WHERE documentid = @documentid"
        cmd = New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
        Dim cnt As Integer = 0
        Try
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@documentid", documentid)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()
            SqlConn.Close()
        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
            SqlConn.Close()
        End Try

        cmd.Dispose()
        strSQL = "DELETE inspectionreportdetails WHERE projectid = @projectid AND iresultsid = @iresultsid"
        cmd = New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)

        Try
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@projectid", projectid)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@iresultsid", iresultsid)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read

            End While
            sqlDr.Close()
            SqlConn.Close()
        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
            SqlConn.Close()
        End Try

        cmd.Dispose()

        cmd.Dispose()
        strSQL = "DELETE inspectionreports WHERE projectid = @projectid AND iresultsid = @iresultsid"
        cmd = New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)

        Try
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@projectid", projectid)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@iresultsid", iresultsid)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read

            End While
            sqlDr.Close()
            SqlConn.Close()
        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
            SqlConn.Close()
        End Try

        cmd.Dispose()
        If cnt < 2 Then
            strSQL = "DELETE documents WHERE documentid = @documentid"
            cmd = New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
            Try
                cmd.Parameters.Add("@documentid", documentid)
                cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                SqlConn.Open()
                sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
                While sqlDr.Read

                End While
                sqlDr.Close()
                SqlConn.Close()
            Catch ex As SqlException
                Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
                SqlConn.Close()
            End Try
        End If

    End Function

    Private Function mwtstExists4Update(ByVal projectid As String, ByVal code As String, ByVal phase As String, ByVal lot As String, ByVal unit As String, ByVal bldg As String, ByVal plantype As String, ByVal peopleid As String) As Integer
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim strSQL As String = "SELECT iresultsid FROM iresults WHERE lot = @lot AND projectid = @projectid AND code = @code AND unit = @unit AND bldg = @bldg AND plantype = @plantype AND phase = @phase AND ins_peopleid = @peopleid"
        Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
        Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
        Try
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@projectid", projectid)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@lot", lot)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@bldg", bldg)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@unit", unit)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@plantype", plantype)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@phase", phase)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@code", code)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@peopleid", peopleid)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                Dim iresults As String = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                If iresults <> "" Then
                    sqlDr.Close()
                    SqlConn.Close()
                    Dim tstExistsSir As Boolean = mwtstExists4UpdateSir(iresults, projectid)
                    If tstExistsSir Then
                        Return 2
                    Else
                        Return 1
                    End If
                Else
                    sqlDr.Close()
                    SqlConn.Close()
                    Return 0
                End If
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()
            SqlConn.Close()
        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
            SqlConn.Close()
        End Try
    End Function

    Private Function mwtstExists4UpdateSir(ByVal iresultsid As String, ByVal projectid As String) As Boolean
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim strSQL As String = "SELECT iresultsid FROM inspectionreportdetails WHERE iresultsid = @iresultsid AND projectid = @projectid"
        Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
        Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
        Try
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@projectid", projectid)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@iresultsid", iresultsid)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                Dim iresults As String = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                If iresults <> "" Then
                    sqlDr.Close()
                    SqlConn.Close()
                    Return True
                Else
                    sqlDr.Close()
                    SqlConn.Close()
                    Return False
                End If
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()
            SqlConn.Close()
        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
            SqlConn.Close()
        End Try
    End Function
