#Region " Mobile Web General"

    <WebMethod()> Public Function mwGetCompanies() As String()

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim sqlCmd As New SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) from companies where companyid in (select companyid from companiesaccess WHERE mobile = 1)", SqlConn) 'where companyid in (select companyid from buildersco)
            Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
            Dim cnt As Integer = Nothing

            sqlCmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = sqlCmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = sqlDr.GetInt32(0)
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            Dim x As Integer = 0
            Dim result(cnt - 1) As String
            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand("SELECT name from companies where companyid in (select companyid from companiesaccess WHERE mobile = 1) ORDER BY name", SqlConn) 'where companyid in (select companyid from buildersco)
            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlDr = sqlCmd1.ExecuteReader

            While sqlDr.Read
                result(x) = sqlDr.GetValue(0).ToString
                x = x + 1
            End While
            sqlDr.Close()

            SqlConn.Close()
            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
            SqlConn.Close()
            Dim result(1) As String
            result(0) = "Error"
            result(1) = ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

            Return result
        End Try

    End Function

#End Region

    <WebMethod()> Public Function mwCSGetAddressOpenIssues() As DataSet
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim strSQL As String = "SELECT DISTINCT homes.address, homes.houseid FROM problemsfieldnotes INNER JOIN problems ON problemsfieldnotes.problemsid = problems.problemsid INNER JOIN homes ON problems.houseid = homes.houseid WHERE (problemsfieldnotes.notedate IS NULL)"
        ' AND companyid = @companyid"
        Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
        Dim sqlDa As SqlDataAdapter
        Dim ds As New DataSet()
        Try
            ds = New DataSet()
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)
            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds
            SqlConn.Close()
        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
            SqlConn.Close()
        End Try
    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function mwCSGetAddressOpenIssuesProblems(ByVal houseid As Integer) As String()
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim strSQL As String = "SELECT count(*) FROM problemsfieldnotes INNER JOIN problems ON problemsfieldnotes.problemsid = problems.problemsid INNER JOIN people ON problems.hom_peopleid = people.peopleid WHERE (problemsfieldnotes.notedate IS NULL) AND (problems.houseid = @houseid)"
        Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(strSQL, SqlConn)
        Dim sqlDr As SqlDataReader
        Dim cnt As Integer
        Try
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@houseid", houseid)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlDr = cmd.ExecuteReader
            While sqlDr.Read
                cnt = CInt(sqlDr.GetValue(0))
            End While
            SqlConn.Close()
        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
            SqlConn.Close()
        End Try

        Dim SqlConn1 As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim strSQL1 As String = "SELECT problems.problemdesc, problems.problemsid FROM problemsfieldnotes INNER JOIN problems ON problemsfieldnotes.problemsid = problems.problemsid INNER JOIN people ON problems.hom_peopleid = people.peopleid WHERE (problemsfieldnotes.notedate IS NULL) AND (problems.houseid = @houseid)"
        Dim cmd1 As New SqlCommand(strSQL1, SqlConn1)
        Dim sqlDr1 As SqlDataReader
        Dim cnt1 As Integer
        Try
            cmd1.Parameters.Add("@houseid", houseid)
            cmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim x As Integer = 1
            Dim result(cnt) As String
            SqlConn1.Open()
            sqlDr1 = cmd1.ExecuteReader
            result(0) = cnt.ToString
            While sqlDr1.Read
                result(x) = sqlDr1.GetValue(0).ToString & "^" & sqlDr1.GetValue(1).ToString & "^"
                x = x + 1
            End While
            SqlConn1.Close()
            Return result
        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw New Exception(ex.Message)
            SqlConn.Close()
        End Try
    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function iv_SirReprint(ByVal docid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim ds As New DataSet()
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT  dbo.companies.name AS coname, dbo.iresults.iresultsid, dbo.iresults.inspectiondate, dbo.iresults.phase, dbo.iresults.lot, dbo.iresults.bldg, " & _
                            "dbo.iresults.unit, dbo.iresults.plantype, dbo.iresults.comment, dbo.iresults.attention, dbo.inspectioncodes.tradename, dbo.projects.name AS projname, " & _
                            "dbo.people.fname + ' ' + dbo.people.lname AS super, people_1.fname AS inspector, LEFT(dbo.documents.docfilename, " & _
                            "LEN(dbo.documents.docfilename) - 4) AS sirnbr, dbo.documents.documentid " & _
                        "FROM dbo.documents INNER JOIN " & _
                            " dbo.inspectionreports ON dbo.documents.documentid = dbo.inspectionreports.documentid INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.people people_1 INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.iresults ON people_1.peopleid = dbo.iresults.ins_peopleid INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.inspectioncodes ON dbo.iresults.code = dbo.inspectioncodes.code INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.companies INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.projects ON dbo.companies.companyid = dbo.projects.companyid ON dbo.iresults.projectid = dbo.projects.projectid INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.superintendantsprojects INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.people ON dbo.superintendantsprojects.peopleid = dbo.people.peopleid ON dbo.iresults.projectid = dbo.superintendantsprojects.projectid AND " & _
                            "dbo.iresults.projectid = dbo.superintendantsprojects.projectid AND dbo.iresults.inspectiondate = dbo.superintendantsprojects.inspectiondate ON " & _
                            "dbo.inspectionreports.iresultsid = dbo.iresults.iresultsid AND dbo.inspectionreports.projectid = dbo.iresults.projectid " & _
                        "WHERE dbo.documents.documentid = @docid " & _
                        "AND dbo.documents.documentcategory = 'inspection report'"

            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@docid", docid)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline

    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function iv_getsirrptphoto(ByVal projid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT LEFT(documents.docfilename, LEN(documents.docfilename) - 4) AS sirnbr, inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid, iresults.inspectiondate, " & _
                        "iresults.phase, inspectioncodes.tradename, iresults.lot, iresults.unit, inspectionreportdetails.sdate, " & _
                        "inspectionreportreminders.reminder, dbo.inspectionreportfollowups.photo, dbo.inspectionreportfollowups.recd, dbo.documents.documentid " & _
                    "FROM inspectionreportdetails INNER JOIN " & _
                        "iresults ON inspectionreportdetails.projectid = iresults.projectid AND " & _
                        "inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid = iresults.iresultsid INNER JOIN " & _
                        "inspectioncodes ON iresults.code = inspectioncodes.code INNER JOIN " & _
                        "documents ON inspectionreportdetails.documentid = documents.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                        "dbo.inspectionreportfollowups ON dbo.iresults.projectid = dbo.inspectionreportfollowups.projectid AND " & _
                        "dbo.iresults.iresultsid = dbo.inspectionreportfollowups.iresultsid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                        "inspectionreportreminders ON inspectionreportdetails.documentid = inspectionreportreminders.documentid AND " & _
                        "inspectionreportdetails.projectid = inspectionreportreminders.projectid AND " & _
                        "inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid = inspectionreportreminders.iresultsid " & _
                    "WHERE documents.projectid = @projid " & _
                    "ORDER BY inspectionreportdetails.sdate, LEFT(documents.docfilename, LEN(documents.docfilename) - 4), inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid, " & _
                        "inspectionreportreminders.reminder"

        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@projid", projid)

            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            'Return result
        End Try

    End Function

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: patricia kline             
    '   Created Date: 10/1/02                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event:  function to check if developer requires photo followup
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function iv_checkfollowup(ByVal devid As Integer) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim result As String
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT photo FROM buildersco " & _
                        "WHERE companyid = @devid"

        Try
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@devid", devid)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Closed Then
                SqlConn.Open()
            End If

            result = CStr(cmd.ExecuteScalar)

            Return result

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    ' Programmer:  Patricia Kline
    ' Created Date:  6/29/03                                               
    ' Modified Date:                                                    
    ' Event: Get sir items
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function iv_OpenItemSummary(ByVal devid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim ds As New DataSet()
        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT dbo.projects.name, dbo.inspectionreportdetails.sdate, dbo.inspectionreportfollowups.photo, dbo.inspectionreportfollowups.recd " & _
                        "FROM dbo.inspectionreportdetails INNER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.projects ON dbo.inspectionreportdetails.projectid = dbo.projects.projectid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "dbo.inspectionreportfollowups ON dbo.inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid = dbo.inspectionreportfollowups.iresultsid AND " & _
                            "dbo.inspectionreportdetails.projectid = dbo.inspectionreportfollowups.projectid " & _
                        "WHERE dbo.projects.companyid = @devid"

            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@devid", devid)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetDocCategories() As DataSet

        Dim ds As New DataSet()

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT documentscategory FROM documentcategories ORDER BY documentscategory"

            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    ' Programmer:  Patricia Kline
    ' Created Date:  3/29/03                                               
    ' Modified Date:                                                      
    ' Event: Get document catetories and return in dataset
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetSubCategories(ByVal category As String) As DataSet

        Dim ds As New DataSet()
        Dim sqlstmt As String

        If category = "inspection report" Then
            sqlstmt = "SELECT category FROM inspectioncategorieslist ORDER BY category"
        ElseIf category = "plans" Then
            sqlstmt = "SELECT planscategory FROM plancategories ORDER BY planscategory"
        End If

        Try
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    ' Programmer:  Patricia Kline
    ' Created Date:  3/29/03                                               
    ' Modified Date:                                                      
    ' Event: Get barcodelist for Project selected
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function GetBarcodelist(ByVal projid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim ds As New DataSet()

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT documents.documentid, documents.docfilename, documents.documentcategory, " & _
                    "case when inspectioncategories.inspectioncategory is not null then inspectioncategories.inspectioncategory " & _
                    "when plans.planscategory is not null then plans.planscategory end as subcategory, " & _
                    "documentdates.documentdate, documenttitles.title, documentauthors.companyid as authorid, vcnet.dbo.companies.name AS author, plans.pagenbr, details.detailnbr, " & _
                    "documentplantypes.plantype, documentphases.phase, documentlots.lot, documentbldgs.bldg, documentunits.unit, vcnet.dbo.homes.address, documents.peopleid " & _
                "FROM details RIGHT OUTER JOIN " & _
                    "plans ON details.documentid = plans.documentid AND details.pagenbr = plans.pagenbr RIGHT OUTER JOIN " & _
                    "inspectioncategories RIGHT OUTER JOIN " & _
                    "vcnet.dbo.homes INNER JOIN " & _
                    "documenthomes ON vcnet.dbo.homes.houseid = documenthomes.houseid RIGHT OUTER JOIN " & _
                    "documentunits RIGHT OUTER JOIN vcnet.dbo.companies INNER JOIN " & _
                    "documentauthors ON vcnet.dbo.companies.companyid = documentauthors.companyid RIGHT OUTER JOIN " & _
                    "documents LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                    "documentbldgs ON documents.documentid = documentbldgs.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                    "documentphases ON documents.documentid = documentphases.documentid ON documentauthors.documentid = documents.documentid ON " & _
                    "documentunits.documentid = documents.documentid ON documenthomes.documentid = documents.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                    "documenttitles ON documents.documentid = documenttitles.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                    "documentlots ON documents.documentid = documentlots.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                    "documentplantypes ON documents.documentid = documentplantypes.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                    "documentdates ON documents.documentid = documentdates.documentid ON inspectioncategories.documentid = documents.documentid ON  " & _
                    "plans.documentid = documents.documentid " & _
                "WHERE documents.projectid = " & projid & " " & _
                "ORDER BY documents.documentid"

            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    ' Programmer:  Patricia Kline
    ' Created Date:  3/29/03                                               
    ' Modified Date:                                                      
    ' Event: Check and Add document if not already in database and return docid
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function AddDocument(ByVal projid As Integer, ByVal filename As String, ByVal category As String, ByVal pplid As Integer) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim cnt As Integer = 0
        Dim docid As Integer = 0
        Dim sqlstmt As String
        Dim strcat As String

        Try
            SqlConn.Open()

            'check if document has already been added
            sqlstmt = "SELECT count(*) FROM documents " & _
             "WHERE projectid = " & projid & " " & _
              "AND docfilename = '" & filename & "'"

            Dim sqlCmd1 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd1.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            cnt = CInt(sqlCmd1.ExecuteScalar.ToString)

            If cnt = 0 Then 'add document if not already in database
                sqlstmt = LCase("INSERT INTO documents (projectid, docfilename, documentcategory, peopleid)  " & _
                    "VALUES (" & projid & ", '" & filename & "', '" & category & "', " & pplid & ")")

                Dim sqlCmd2 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                sqlCmd2.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                sqlCmd2.ExecuteNonQuery()
                strcat = category
            Else 'check if document category
                sqlstmt = "SELECT documentcategory FROM documents " & _
                 "WHERE projectid = " & projid & " " & _
                  "AND docfilename = '" & filename & "'"

                Dim sqlCmd2 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                sqlCmd2.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                strcat = sqlCmd2.ExecuteScalar.ToString
            End If

            If strcat = category Then 'get documentid 
                sqlstmt = "SELECT documentid FROM documents " & _
                 "WHERE projectid = " & projid & " " & _
                  "AND docfilename = '" & filename & "'"

                Dim sqlCmd3 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
                sqlCmd3.CommandType = CommandType.Text
                docid = CInt(sqlCmd3.ExecuteScalar.ToString)

                Return docid.ToString
            Else
                Return "Dup"
            End If

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function EditDocument(ByVal docid As Integer, ByVal filename As String, ByVal category As String, ByVal pplid As Integer) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim sqlstmt As String

        Try
            SqlConn.Open()
            sqlstmt = "UPDATE documents " & _
                        "SET docfilename = '" & filename & "', " & _
                         "documentcategory = '" & category & "',  " & _
                         "updatedate = '" & Now() & "', " & _
                         "peopleid = " & pplid & " " & _
                        "WHERE documentid = " & docid

            Dim sqlCmd2 As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            sqlCmd2.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            sqlCmd2.ExecuteNonQuery()

            Return "True"

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

#Region " WEB Co. Wide Reports "

    '************************************************************************
    '   Programmer: Marc Olaño            
    '   Created Date: 6/3/03                
    '   Modified Date:                          
    '   Event: get drop down results for each combobox based off other 
    '          filters for the WEB.
    '************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function doc_getResults2_web(ByVal field As String, ByVal whereclause As String) As DataSet

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT DISTINCT " & field & ", documents.documentcategory, documenttitles.title, companies.name " & _
                        "FROM warranties RIGHT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "documentdates RIGHT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "inspectioncategories RIGHT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "documents ON inspectioncategories.documentid = documents.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "inspectionreportdetails ON documents.documentid = inspectionreportdetails.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "companies INNER JOIN " & _
                            "documentauthors ON companies.companyid = documentauthors.companyid ON " & _
                            "documents.documentid = documentauthors.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "documentlocations ON documents.documentid = documentlocations.documentid ON " & _
                            "documentdates.documentid = documents.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "documenttitles ON documents.documentid = documenttitles.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "phases ON documents.documentid = phases.documentid ON warranties.documentid = documents.documentid LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "plans LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "plantypes ON plans.documentid = plantypes.documentid AND plans.pagenbr = plantypes.pagenbr LEFT OUTER JOIN " & _
                            "details ON plans.documentid = details.documentid AND plans.pagenbr = details.pagenbr ON " & _
                            "documents.documentid = plans.documentid " & _
                        "WHERE " & whereclause & " " & _
                        "AND " & field & " is not null " & _
                        "ORDER BY " & field

        Try
            Dim ds As New DataSet()
            Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            'cmd.Parameters.Add("@field", field)
            'cmd.Parameters.Add("@whereclause", whereclause)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(cmd)

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            SqlConn.Close()
            'Return result
        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Marc Olano
    '   Created Date: 08/11/03
    '   Event:  Returns current monthly project name, sdate, and projid
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function rpt_Cum_Anom(ByVal coid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim ds As New DataSet()

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT name, sdate, p.projectid " & _
                    "FROM inspectionreportdetails i, projects p " & _
                    "WHERE(p.companyid = @coid) " & _
                    "AND i.projectid = p.projectid"

        Try
            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@coid", coid)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex
        End Try
    End Function


    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Marc Olano
    '   Created Date: 08/12/03
    '   Event:  Returns overall project name, sdate, and projid
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function rpt_Month_Anom(ByVal coid As Integer, ByVal inspMonth As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim ds As New DataSet()

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT dbo.inspectionreportdetails.sdate, dbo.projects.name, dbo.projects.projectid, dbo.iresults.inspectiondate " & _
                    "FROM dbo.projects INNER JOIN " & _
                    "dbo.inspectionreportdetails ON dbo.projects.projectid = dbo.inspectionreportdetails.projectid INNER JOIN " & _
                    "dbo.iresults ON dbo.projects.projectid = dbo.iresults.projectid AND dbo.inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid = dbo.iresults.iresultsid " & _
                    "WHERE (dbo.projects.companyid = @coid) " & _
                    "AND month(inspectiondate) = @inspMonth " & _
                    "ORDER BY dbo.iresults.inspectiondate"

        Try
            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@coid", coid)
            Sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@inspMonth", inspMonth)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex
        End Try
    End Function

    <WebMethod()> Public Function rpt_Monthly_AnomFound(ByVal coid As Integer, ByVal month As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim ds As New DataSet()

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT dbo.projects.name, dbo.iresults.attention " & _
                    "FROM dbo.iresults INNER JOIN dbo.projects ON dbo.iresults.projectid = dbo.projects.projectid " & _
                    "WHERE (dbo.projects.companyid = @coid) " & _
                    "AND month(dbo.iresults.inspectiondate) = @month " & _
                    "ORDER BY dbo.projects.name"

        Try
            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@coid", coid)
            Sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@month", month)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex
        End Try
    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Marc Olano
    '   Created Date: 08/14/03
    '   Event:  Returns All Anomolies Found
    '*******************************************************************************
    <WebMethod()> Public Function rpt_AttnItemsFound(ByVal coid As Integer) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim cnt As Integer

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT COUNT(*) " & _
                    "FROM iresults " & _
                    "WHERE projectid IN " & _
                    "(SELECT projectid FROM projects WHERE companyid = @coid) " & _
                    "and attention = 1"

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@coid", coid)
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(cmd.ExecuteScalar)

            Return cnt.ToString

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function rpt_Monthly_AtItmsFound(ByVal coid As Integer, ByVal Month As Integer) As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim cnt As Integer

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT COUNT(*) " & _
                        "FROM iresults " & _
                        "WHERE projectid IN " & _
                        "(SELECT projectid FROM projects WHERE companyid = @coid) " & _
                        "AND month(inspectiondate) = @month " & _
                        "and attention = 1"

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@coid", coid)
            cmd.Parameters.Add("@month", Month)
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(cmd.ExecuteScalar)

            Return cnt.ToString

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function


    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Marc Olano
    '   Created Date: 08/18/03
    '   Event:  Attn Items Closed AVG Time
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function rpt_AVG_Time(ByVal coid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim ds As New DataSet()

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT dbo.projects.name, dbo.inspectionreportdetails.sdate, dbo.iresults.inspectiondate, DATEDIFF(dd, dbo.iresults.inspectiondate, " & _
                    "dbo.inspectionreportdetails.sdate) AS days, dbo.iresults.attention " & _
                    "FROM dbo.projects INNER JOIN " & _
                    "dbo.inspectionreportdetails ON dbo.projects.projectid = dbo.inspectionreportdetails.projectid INNER JOIN " & _
                    "dbo.iresults ON dbo.projects.projectid = dbo.iresults.projectid AND dbo.inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid = dbo.iresults.iresultsid " & _
                    "WHERE (dbo.projects.companyid = @coid) " & _
                    "ORDER BY dbo.projects.name"

        Try
            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@coid", coid)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex
        End Try
    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Marc Olano
    '   Created Date: 08/19/03
    '   Event:  Attn Items Open AVG Time in Days
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function rpt_OpenAttnItems(ByVal coid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim ds As New DataSet()

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT dbo.projects.name, dbo.inspectionreportdetails.sdate, dbo.iresults.inspectiondate, " & _
                    "DATEDIFF(dd, dbo.iresults.inspectiondate, GETDATE()) AS days " & _
                    "FROM dbo.projects INNER JOIN " & _
                    "dbo.inspectionreportdetails ON dbo.projects.projectid = dbo.inspectionreportdetails.projectid INNER JOIN " & _
                    "dbo.iresults ON dbo.projects.projectid = dbo.iresults.projectid AND dbo.inspectionreportdetails.iresultsid = dbo.iresults.iresultsid " & _
                    "WHERE (dbo.projects.companyid = @coid) AND (dbo.inspectionreportdetails.sdate IS NULL) " & _
                    "ORDER BY dbo.projects.name"

        Try
            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@coid", coid)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex
        End Try
    End Function


    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Marc Olano
    '   Created Date: 08/20/03
    '   Event:  Returns items by trade.
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function rpt_FoundTrade(ByVal coid As Integer) As DataSet

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim ds As New DataSet()

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT DISTINCT  dbo.projects.name AS projectname, dbo.inspectioncodes.tradename AS trade, dbo.iresults.attention, COUNT(dbo.inspectioncodes.tradename) AS tradecount " & _
                    "FROM dbo.iresults INNER JOIN " & _
                    "dbo.inspectioncodes ON dbo.iresults.code = dbo.inspectioncodes.code INNER JOIN " & _
                    "dbo.projects ON dbo.iresults.projectid = dbo.projects.projectid INNER JOIN " & _
                    "dbo.companies ON dbo.projects.companyid = dbo.companies.companyid " & _
                    "WHERE (dbo.projects.companyid = @coid) " & _
                    "GROUP BY dbo.inspectioncodes.tradename, dbo.projects.name, projects.projectid, dbo.iresults.attention " & _
                    "ORDER BY dbo.projects.name, dbo.inspectioncodes.tradename"

        Try
            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            Sqlcmd.Parameters.Add("@coid", coid)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex
        End Try
    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Marc Olano
    '   Created Date: 08/07/03
    '   Event:  Returns total users for vcnet
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function admin_tot_users() As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim cnt As Integer

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt " & _
   "FROM dbo.users"

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(cmd.ExecuteScalar)

            Return cnt.ToString

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function



    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Marc Olano
    '   Created Date: 08/07/03
    '   Event:  Returns total company users for vcnet
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function admin_tot_CO_users() As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim cnt As Integer

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT COUNT(*) AS cnt " & _
                        "FROM users " & _
                        "WHERE peopleid in " & _
                        "(SELECT peopleid FROM people WHERE companyid IS NOT NULL)"

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(cmd.ExecuteScalar)

            Return cnt.ToString

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Marc Olano
    '   Created Date: 08/07/03
    '   Event:  Returns total homeowner users for vcnet
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function admin_tot_HO_users() As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim cnt As Integer

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT COUNT(*) AS cnt " & _
                        "FROM users " & _
                        "WHERE peopleid in " & _
                        "(SELECT peopleid FROM people WHERE homeowner = 1)"

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(cmd.ExecuteScalar)

            Return cnt.ToString

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Marc Olano
    '   Created Date: 08/07/03
    '   Event:  Returns total hits
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function admin_tot_hits() As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim cnt As Integer

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT COUNT(*) as cnt " & _
                        "FROM dbo.tracking"

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(cmd.ExecuteScalar)

            Return cnt.ToString

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try

    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Marc Olano
    '   Created Date: 08/07/03
    '   Event:  Returns total FAQ hits
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function admin_FAQ_hits() As String

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim cnt As Integer

        Try
            Dim sqlstmt As String
            sqlstmt = "SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT trackingid) " & _
                        "FROM trackingpages " & _
                            "WHERE page LIKE '%FAQs%'"

            Dim cmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)
            cmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text
            SqlConn.Open()
            cnt = CInt(cmd.ExecuteScalar)

            Return cnt.ToString

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Return ex.Number & "^" & ex.Message

        Finally
            If SqlConn.State = ConnectionState.Open Then
                SqlConn.Close()
            End If

        End Try
    End Function

    '*******************************************************************************
    '   Programmer:  Marc Olano
    '   Created Date: 08/07/03
    '   Event:  Returns company name, admin for company, and phone
    '*******************************************************************************

    <WebMethod()> Public Function admin_companyInfo() As DataSet

        Dim SqlConn As New SqlConnection(strConn)
        Dim ds As New DataSet()

        Dim sqlstmt As String
        sqlstmt = "SELECT dbo.people.fname + ' ' + dbo.people.lname AS name, dbo.companies.phone AS cophone, dbo.usersaccess.sysadmin,dbo.companies.name AS coname " & _
                    "FROM dbo.users INNER JOIN dbo.usersaccess ON dbo.users.peopleid = dbo.usersaccess.peopleid INNER JOIN " & _
                    "dbo.people ON dbo.users.peopleid = dbo.people.peopleid INNER JOIN " & _
                    "dbo.companies ON dbo.people.companyid = dbo.companies.companyid " & _
                    "WHERE (dbo.usersaccess.sysadmin = 1)"

        Try
            Dim Sqlcmd As New SqlCommand(sqlstmt, SqlConn)

            Dim da As New SqlDataAdapter(Sqlcmd)
            Sqlcmd.CommandType = CommandType.Text

            da.Fill(ds)
            Return ds

        Catch ex As SqlException
            Throw ex

        End Try

    End Function
#End Region

End Class
